# FREEZE ZERO — THE FRESH-LOOK AUDIT of 2026-11-28

> The evidence behind `PLAN.md` §0. Made by the program's session on the day it took the program over (the owner's
> mandate: «a claim in the journal is a starting point, not a fact — re-measure it on today's head, and say where a
> metric measured the wrong thing»).

**The pin:** `74910b95e2` (v1.2.3632 — the head of `main` at the hand-over; kept as the branch `fz/pin-1128`).

**How it was made.** Thirty read-only auditors, each with one mandate — ten over the exit numbers of §8's stages, eleven
over the instruments themselves (the report, the ledgers, the baselines, the tiers, CI, the two pilots, the bottle, the
judges), eight over visual defects, one over the field — and then ONE INDEPENDENT VERIFIER PER FINDING, told to refute
it: run its re-check literally, open every cited line, look for the guard or the later entry that already answers it.
239 findings; 216 were verified (the 23 of severity «info» were not): **67 confirmed as worded, 148 confirmed in a
narrower form — the statement printed below is the VERIFIER'S corrected one — 1 refuted** (it is kept in part B,
marked). No agent ran a browser, the box, a bottle or a CI dispatch; they read the tree at the pin, ran single guard
specs, re-summed downloaded CI artefacts and built synthetic inputs for the report script.

**How to read a finding.** *Statement* — what is true at the pin. *Evidence* — a `path:line`, a command with its
decisive output, or a CI run id + commit + artefact. *Re-check* — what an engineer runs to see the same thing in
minutes. `<audit>/` is the audit's working directory on the program session's laptop (`C:\Projects\Mods\tm-fz-p-audit`:
per-auditor long reports `reports/<KEY>.md`, the verifiers' notes `reports/verify/`, scratch scripts, downloaded
artefacts); it is not in the repository — an artefact-based re-check is reproducible from the run id alone
(`gh run download <id> --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars`), a scratch-script one from the script's description
in the long report. Severity is the VERIFIER's.

**Part A** is the audit read as one picture (thirty clusters). **Part B** is every finding, by auditor.

---

## PART A — what the audit found

### I · Defects on the latest passes that no judge, net or gate named

**A1 · A frozen Parliament passed as a clean game (CI's browser game 632, `983c3b60d0`).** «Nationalist Movement» from
the hand completed the chairman's quest → the plate «A Открыть Парламент» → the overview stood on «Парламент · A
Выполняется…» from generation 4 to the press cap in generation 8: 2 854 of 3 200 presses, B ×120, twenty-four questions
answered by the pilot's door over the API. Not one diagnostic line; the test PASSED; the report booked «1 capped · 0
STUCK»; no issue was filed; the journal does not hold the words «game 632». The arbiter's own text explains the
silence: on a screen that says it is `accepting`, only presses NO handler took are counted, and «unserved» requires
`!accepting` — a workspace that takes the d-pad while its one commit is disabled for ever is not a stall by that
definition, and the one-owner law checks that the owner PAINTS, not that its commit can be pressed. A slow-machine game
(CPU ÷3); the cause is not established. *Evidence:* Autopilot 46144276012, artefact `autopilot-browser-46144276012-1-7`,
`…game-632-chromium/autopilot-walk.txt` (`"end": "capped"`, 24 `doors` rows all `frames parliament:browse · bar
«ПарламентAВыполняется…» · grey Enter`), its cause-classes file `status passed, records []`. *Re-check:* download the
artefact, `sed -n '1,45p'` of the walk; `grep -c "game 632" docs/claude/console/freeze-zero/PLAN.md` → 0. *(Part B:
S6a/capped-game-632-frozen-parliament, M8/game-632-silent-parliament-freeze, M1/door-and-cap-never-gate.)*

**A2 · A question stood with nobody serving it, the ladder was spent and the page rebuilt — twice in one CI pass, and
again on the pin.** Browser games 621 and 630 of the same run: an `or` question on the bare board, `holder: stranded`,
the plate's lease expired, 10 s, one page rebuild — in 630 one second after an injected failed submit; the one-owner law
read MISS 0 through both. On the pin, the program's own first count on the box named the sibling within 18 games (game
3014: a draft's `cardSelect`, «expected task|draft, on screen nothing», rebuilt at 8 s). Stage 2's literal exit («such
lines impossible by construction → nets retired») and stage 4's («each injected fault ends in served or rebuilt once,
never a standing screen») both read against it. *Evidence:* `autopilot-browser.txt` of 46144276012 lines 7–9 and 125;
the walks of 621 (`400` → `STRANDED PROMPT` → `[screen-shadow] unserved` → `[session-rebuild] page`) and 630; issues
#119 #170 #171 #172 #177 (one family, reopened four times after three pointwise cures). *Re-check:* `grep -n -E
"stranded|NEVER CURED" autopilot-browser.txt`; `gh issue view 119 --comments`. *(S2/nets-not-retired-strand-recurs,
S4/fault-ends-in-standing-screen-real-browser.)*

**A3 · The Parliament's frame left its own protocol twice on 2026-11-28 and nothing failed.** `frame:parliament
verdict → configure` — a move `consoleWorkspaceFrame.ts:104` does not hold — in the nightly Monkey (46140902727, table
`summit-logistics-locked`, «GATE: pass») and in the Autopilot's browser (game 628, passed). Filed tier `info`; the gate
that would refuse it (`kernel-walked --gate`) is run by no workflow; the journal never names the move. *Re-check:*
`grep -n "off-protocol\|^GATE" monkey-report.txt` of 46140902727. *(S4/off-protocol-move-made-and-unread,
M2/off-protocol-verdict-configure-info.)*

**A4 · A defect record inside a passing test is nobody's.** Games 599 (`start:reactor-face`) and 602
(`start-bonus:visible-source`) carried defect-tier records and PASSED; the hand-over's «3 games red of 96, two classes»
counts failed tests — CI's half alone was 9 defect records, 7 classes NEW to its baseline (≈ 4 families), 2 episodes
never cured, and the frozen fifth. In the scripted e2e suite a defect-tier record neither fails its spec nor reaches a
gate (≈ 20 hand-kept red markers against 81 defect shapes): the nightly suite 46126088269 carried three natural defect
classes under green tests. *(S6a/handover-understates-last-pass, S0/stage6-exit-not-met-on-latest-pass,
M6/e2e-suite-does-not-gate-defect-tier, V8/green-suite-gate-not-built.)*

### II · The instruments — where a number does not measure what its name says

**A5 · The owner's ZERO bar is run by nothing.** `monkey-report --zero` (no record of any tier, no door, no line in the
log) is called by no workflow, npm script or gate: CI's Autopilot runs `--gate`, under which a door, a capped game, a
planned / info record and every line of the page's log are green. Re-summed, 0 of 10 downloaded world-passes pass
`--zero` — the one «success» run included (bottle: 110 info records, 17 opening disagreements). *Re-check:* `git grep
-n -- --zero -- .github scripts package.json`. *(S6c/ci-gate-is-not-zero-bar and six more.)*

**A6 · Two of the three words of stage 6's exit are not counts in the real browser.** «rebuilds» is a field the browser
driver never writes (`git grep -n rebuilds -- tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts` → nothing): the report prints
«rebuilds 0» two lines under «episodes 2 (rebuilt 2)». «STUCK» watches the SERVER's game age — unreachable for a client
freeze while the door keeps answering. A «capped» game is not red, and a door answer never gates. *(M1/rebuilds-dead-in-
browser, M8/verdict-stuck-unreachable-finished-is-server-end, M1/door-and-cap-never-gate.)*

**A7 · The report has no denominator.** An empty directory, a results file cut mid-way, a bottle game whose harness
threw (filed `bottle/harness-error` at INFO and dropped from the count), a shard that died of memory (`… || true`) —
each reads «GATE: pass», exit 0 (proven with synthetic inputs). A Sunday `net400` pass reported «pass» over 568 of 606
tables. The one-owner law is gated on the walks that still carry its counters (44 – 47 of 48 browser games — a rebuilt
page loses them). *(M1/pass-not-whole-passes, M7/bottle-crash-green, S1/report-has-no-walk-floor,
S2/owner-law-counters-die-with-rebuild.)*

**A8 · The excuses grew, and some excuse live defects.** `LAYOUT_BLIND` («the bottle is blind here») went from 6 rows
at stage 1's exit to 13 at the pin (stage 1's own number: «shrinks only»); two rows — `start:reactor-face`,
`start-bonus:visible-source` — are classes a REAL browser files as defects on the same pass (#199, #161), and the bottle
keeps printing them as info. The freeze gate's bottle baseline accepts 12 defect classes on every table for good, among
them `console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n` — issue #119. A baseline row's `games` / `tables`
counts are never read; a class re-tiered to info prints «gone». *(S1/blind-list-grew-contradicted,
M9/layout-blind-falsified, M9/monkeybaseline-excuses-119, M5/baseline-counts-not-enforced,
M11/compare-reports-retier-is-gone.)*

**A9 · The zeros were measured on replayed games.** Games 537 – 584 were played seven times; the one green Autopilot
run of the last ten is their fifth replay. Both FRESH ranges were red (1001+48: 3 new classes; 585+48: 7). The count
that says whether the product converges — new classes per fresh hundred — is reported nowhere.
*(M8/ci-zero-measured-on-replayed-seeds, S6a/fresh-range-always-red.)*

**A10 · «100 %» is a union over two weeks, many commits and two worlds.** Cards «672 of 672»: one whole CI pass plays
416 (62 %), its real browser 313 (47 %); 27 of the 672 were never counted in a real browser; a card a scenario fixture
pre-places in the viewer's tableau counts as played (Tharsis Alliance counts only so), and so does a play the door made
over the API. The kernel ledger's «debt 0»: a hand-run union kept outside the repository plus 49 declared keys; one
whole CI pass walks flows 244 of 274 and machine moves 35 of 66; `kernel-walked --gate` over CI's own corpus exits 3.
«Walked» is one `open` key anywhere, ever. Input types 20 of 20 does reproduce per pass. *(M3/…, M4/…, M2/….)*

**A11 · The axes the owner asked for and nobody measures.** Interruption verbs × frame phase: 119 of 624 cells seen, a
reload never met a beat, 97 % of the collapses are in the Parliament. Question shapes × context markers: 10 of the 32
declared markers never answered at top level in 240 browser games. Tables: the product has twelve boards and the
browser pilot deals eleven — Amazonis Planitia (the only 11-row layout) never had a whole game; no 4 – 5 humans.
«Played» is not «walked»: 156 in-scope cards have an action and ≥ 68 a trigger, with no axis for either.
*(M4/interruption-axis-unmeasured, M4/shapes-axis-no-denominator, S6a/twelfth-board-never-dealt,
M3/played-not-walked-no-action-trigger-axis.)*

**A12 · The pilot is not a player on a couch.** Every whole game types on a KEYBOARD into a 1280×720 `handheld` page —
no gamepad, no TV profile — at motion ×0.5 with the e2e boot; it never presses twice inside 164 ms and never goes
silent for more than 9 s. 24 of the 45 commits that taught the pilot changed no product file (a rule added, the path no
longer walked); the start is answered on the screen in 21 % of games. *(M8/no-gamepad-no-tv-profile-in-whole-games,
M8/pilot-timing-is-not-a-players, M8/pilot-only-rules-remove-player-paths.)*

**A13 · The bottle and the browser name different defects.** Over five Autopilot runs (240 same-seed pairs) the browser
named 14 defect classes and the bottle one, none shared at defect tier; the same seed is not the same walk, and the
browser's own classes do not recur run to run. So «a real browser said nothing on this seed» is no evidence for a blind
row, and every story with a rect behind it is judged by the browser half alone (the bottle's `transferEndVerdict` is
always `empty`, `transitionend` never fires). *(M9/same-seed-worlds-disjoint, M9/bottle-degraded-paths,
V6/bottle-cannot-see-stories.)*

**A14 · CI can be green while a freeze class is live.** The Autopilot — the only gate of whole games — has no schedule.
`release.yml` publishes to the self-update feed on every push to `main` and waits for no gate (releases shipped for
commits whose CI failed or was cancelled) — new cause classes are filed daily beside green releases. «The whole
Playwright E2E, success» of the hand-over (46144266613) was a one-test dispatch — the last whole suite had failed. The
findings queue labels a class «regression» from a run on any commit (the six of 2026-11-28 are real; a false one
happened on 11-27). The automatic ci-fix lane's rules do not cover `tests/**` or most nets. *(M7/….)*

**A15 · Three closures were a change of the instrument.** #111 was closed by re-naming the pad rung the measure records
(«the routing is unchanged»); #167 and the sitting-rise story by re-tiering to «planned» (the wait still ends on its
due); #197 by a spec change. About twenty product fixes of cited findings carry no committed replay («game N replayed,
before / after» lives in the commit message), and none of the 44 class replays can switch its fix off. What holds: on a
sample of ten, every fix that DID commit a spec asserts on the changed path. *(M11/….)*

### III · The stages' exit numbers, re-read

**A16 · Stage 1 — confirmed at its exit, not held since.** The 24 cells at 0 defect records on `ab6ad53eba` are real
(18 provably on CI at that commit and 6 on the box — the journal's «fifteen and nine» is a miscount; «no GPU» was never
a cell; the box half's commit is the journal's word only). Since then: 356 commits, 0 cells on the pin, `cpu6` and
`net1500` never re-run, the perturbations only on seed `fz0`, 26 new tables (632, not 606) never perturbed, the blind
list doubled (A8). «0 defect» was the defect TIER: the exit cells carried 361 planned records, and the exit came in the
same 48 h as 29 tier-related commits. Two downgrades can hide a real defect: the frame witness reads frame gaps only (a
page blocked by its OWN script reads as «starved» → planned), and the arbiter is not suspend-aware (one look after an
hour's sleep is a 3 601 s «unserved» episode — a false alarm in the field). *(S1/…, M6/frame-witness-cannot-tell-jank-
from-hidden, F1/liveness-wall-clock-across-suspend.)*

**A17 · Stage 2 — the function stands; «one opinion» does not.** `servingOwnerOf` is total (its three specs pass on
the pin) and the nine prompt surfaces mount by it — and the bar's rungs for those nine read the same derived answer. But
«the surfaces, the pad's ladder and the bar derived from ONE function» holds for those nine only: the workspace owners
(the hand, the colonies, the Parliament, the standard projects, the Hydronetwork, the awards, the board) open by the
task's kind, the bar's order, its finale and its section rungs are its own; half of all looks are «held as said» and
never judged (47 % in the browser); and A2 is the line the stage called impossible. *(S2/….)*

**A18 · Stage 3 — «nothing waits outside a scope» is literally true; «every wait» has no denominator.**
`kernelCoverage` = 0 holds (four raw timer / frame calls, all in the two declared device clocks) and all eight guards
are green. But six of the eight detect by NAME; the wait-graph law reads two idioms (12 sites) and misses reactive
waits behind an import alias (`colonyController.ts:2222`, `:2324` — `watch as vueWatch`, no bound, flags with no
`finally`); about six polled waits end on a wall-clock cap with no node and NO LINE; a `guarantee` that fires by its
timer leaves no trace (≈ 16 safeties in the card motions alone); and the flag class froze CI for 35 s one commit before
the pin (`boardBeatPark.draining`, cured pointwise by `4392fb1fc2`). *(S3/…, V2/wave-safety-ends-done,
V1/guarantee-silent-nets.)*

**A19 · Stage 4 — 100 % by declaration.** At most 45 of 66 machine moves (68 %) and 268 of 274 flows are walked; under
the owner's bar («declared with a reason is not covered») 27 rows are open, not six. The flow denominator counts
literal labels of 5 of the kernel's 15 kinds: about a hundred run-time-labelled flow ids ran outside any denominator
(`gate:tableAnswer` was opened by nothing and stands on no list). No assertion «served» exists for a presentation that
never ends, a throwing presenter, a held answer or any socket outcome. *(S4/…, M2/….)*

**A20 · Stage 5 — clean, and a quarter of its promised size.** The baseline is empty, every finding was closed with a
seed and a card spec, four nightlies are green. The nightly plays 0.22 – 0.32 M engine steps (not «N million» — the
1.85 M were three runs by hand); it never submits a batch (the console's one-press road), never draws the creator's
defaults (random milestones / awards, the shuffled map), the campaign or the production bot scheduler; reload
equivalence judges ≈ 21 % of the answers; there is no shrinker. *(S5/….)*

**A21 · Stage 6 — the exit number was never true per map set.** The one clean hundred (run 45471563686 on
`b724622738`) is 131 commits behind and holds 5 – 15 games a board; no hundred since 2026-11-25; nine of the last ten
Autopilot runs are red. No 8-hour soak was ever completed (the longest: 2.5 h, 134 – 155 commits behind); «flat» is
judged by eye; the soak's own census call (`DOM.getDetachedDomNodes`) pins the nodes it counts; the frame budget of a
press is three opt-in probes at a desktop viewport, with the Parliament left at a 144 ms median. *(S6a/…, S6b/….)*

**A22 · Stage 7 — not started, as said.** The ladder holds the same eleven releases as on 2026-11-17. Acting nets fire
0.081 per 1 000 presses in the browser (18 in 240 whole games — a lower bound: there is no list of acting shapes); no
pass is at zero. The release probe A7 was never run; nobody reads the field's session ledgers (a rise of planned / info
lines per release has no alarm); the ledger leaves as ONE extra value against a measured ≈ 16 KB cut. What holds: at
today's rate there is no Sentry flood (≈ 35 defect events per 100 h, a long tail of 14 classes in 49 h, none repeating);
the policy by tier, the scrubbers and «no DSN in the tree» are as written. *(S7/…, F1/….)*

### IV · Visual defects — why the architecture cannot see them today

**A23 · A story that was not told is a NOTE, and a NOTE is nothing.** Of 127 diagnostic families, 39 (stall, integrity)
carry a cause class and a tier; the 78 NOTE families — where a module «degrades honestly»: «not shown as promised» ×9,
«settled without a flight» ×5, «degraded to teleport» — get none: never a class, never a ci-finding, never an event; in
the field one `+1` on `kinds.note`, which `diag trend` does not print. A healthy CI game's `[start-emerge] degraded to
teleport` (game 594) was filed nowhere. *(M6/note-kind-untiered-visual-layer, V6/story-degrade-is-note-never-a-cause,
F1/note-tier-motion-loss-invisible-in-field.)*

**A24 · The product's own verdicts are compiled out of every build that is measured.** 21 tagged print sites — every
«not shown as promised», «ticked without a token», «the token is not flown», two DEFECT shapes (`cursor/backstop`,
`reading/raw-template`) and the PLANNED `transfer/no-visible-end` — stand behind `NODE_ENV !== 'production'`: absent
from the e2e suite, the monkey, the Autopilot's browser and the field. The degraded STATE is still set (17 private
`.degraded` flags); nothing counts it run-wide. *(M10/motion-sentinels-compiled-out, M6/dev-only-witnesses-compiled-out,
V2/no-visible-end-unobservable.)*

**A25 · Cards have no end witness.** Tokens do (`transfer/end-not-painted`, `painted-under`, `told twice` — defect tier,
every build, red in every spec — the one story asserted over whole games). Of 36 non-designed degradation sites in the
card motions, 26 end silently or in a trail nobody reads; over 240 CI games the log holds ONE card-motion line; a batch
arrival with no deck pile makes up a source above the slot against «a card always leaves from its real source».
*(V1/….)*

**A26 · A token that never STARTED is counted by no gate** (no source, no destination, no stage, a detach that
expired): a 60-entry in-memory trail and an optional `onDegrade` that 16 of ≈ 62 call sites pass. The remote landing's
planned line went to zero partly because the hand-over now ends the wait silently — the tile still appears without its
flight. An unclaimed card-reaction group is dropped at the next answer into a 16-entry ring. *(V2/never-flew-not-
counted, V5/remote-landing-handover-silent, V5/reaction-expired-silent.)*

**A27 · A ceiling names the wait that ended, never the story that was lost** — no typed «instead» field, the planned
reason is cut from CI's artefacts at 220 characters, and the same non-painting page is «planned» in one family and a
defect in another. *(V4/….)*

**A28 · Nothing asks WHERE a flow went.** Every runtime judge is question-centric. `FRAME_PROTOCOL` is descriptive (rows
«read off the walks»: `committed → browse` is legal, an off-protocol move is info); «Действия карт» never writes its
phase past the commit to its frame, so the kernel's machine and the dead-B judge are blind there; crumb continuity (46
writer call sites), «the same instance», the carried card, the landing of a conclusion have no runtime line;
`backUnmoved` sees only «B changed nothing» on a committed top frame. No judge asserts that a cursor exists, is painted
and stands on a live element; nothing compares a painted value with the applied view (invariant 16 has no runtime
reader); «presses-no-effect» covers A and B that no handler took. *(V7/…, M10/….)*

### V · What holds (the audit is not only a list of gaps)

**A29.** The code-debt ratchets only fell since 2026-11-14 and no liveness / hold / wait-graph constant was raised
(M5). The report script re-sums real shards to CI's own numbers; never-cured, NEW class and the laws are computed as
stated (M1). The bottle is deterministic on CI's evidence (M9). The kernel's numbers reproduce as the union they are
(M2); the flow universe shrank only by deleting dead flows. The cards' denominator (535 + 80 + 57) and «only the
viewer's plays» are right (M3). The pilot runs with no retries, a stall or a rebuild is red per game, a door is
recorded with what stood on screen (M8). Token ENDS are asserted in every build (V6); the emblem echo cannot strand and
a section's leave freezes its decided rect (V3); the presentation ledger is a real promise book — with seven owners
(V6). The shape table (39 families, 111 rows: 81 defect · 23 info · 7 planned) and the 2026-11-18 tier audit's fixes
are in code (M6). Stage 5's zero and its closures (S5). #111's whole pass came: pad-MISS 0 in both worlds (S6c).

**A30 · The program session's own first measurement (added 2026-11-28).** Its first box count ran 18 games without the
Russian locale (`assets/locales` is generated into the source tree and was missing from the box worktree): every game
printed «Cannot load ru translations», the pilots' Russian rules met an English bar, and 17 of the 18 «passed». Nothing
in the harness checks that the product booted as it ships — one more row for A7. The count was restarted
(`fp-count2`).

---

## PART B — every finding, by auditor

`[kind · severity by the verifier (auditor’s) · the verifier’s verdict]`. The *Statement* is the verifier’s corrected one wherever the verdict is «partly»; a finding of severity «info» was not verified and prints the auditor’s own statement. Paths are relative to the repository at the pin.

### S0-open-ledger — the stages’ exit numbers

*Scope:* I grepped PLAN.md §0, §4, §6 and §8 for open markers (144 hits) and followed each real one forward by class key and git log. I checked the instruments the journal says are missing with git grep. I ran one server spec: moduleStateGuard with the report env set, which only prints. I read the CI artefacts of Autopilot 46144276012 (counted per game: test status and defect-tier records) and Monkey 46140902727, plus issues #119, #161, #172, #177, #198, #199 and #111 with read-only gh.

**S0/stage6-exit-not-met-on-latest-pass** — Stage 6's exit is not met on the latest whole pass: 2 episodes, 2 rebuilds, 4 browser games with defect records, 1 success in the last 11 autopilot runs  
`[claim-not-met · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* On CI's half alone, 4 of 48 browser games carry defect-tier records: 621 and 630 failed, while 599 (reactor-face) and 602 (start-bonus:visible-source) PASSED with a defect record. There are 7 defect classes in 4 families, 2 episodes and 2 never cured. «Red» counts failed tests, so a defect in a passing game drops out of the headline. Of the last 11 completed Autopilot runs, 10 failed or were cancelled.  
*Evidence:* A scratch count over the per-game files in autopilot-browser-46144276012-1-*/test-results/cause-classes gives passed 46 / failed 2, with defect records in 602 (passed, 1), 621 (failed, 3), 630 (failed, 4) and 599 (passed, 1). autopilot-browser.txt: `records 13 · of them defects 9 · episodes 2 (rebuilt 2) · NEVER CURED 2`, 7 DEFECT classes. `gh run list --workflow Autopilot --limit 12`: the only success is 46072102662 (1d740f2ea5).  
*Re-check:* Read the first 20 lines of autopilot-browser.txt. Then for each of games 599, 602, 621 and 630, open console-autopilot--autopilot-game-N-r0.json and compare .status with the records whose tier is defect.  
*Against it (the verifier):* PLAN.md:27 already says stage 6's own exit numbers are «not re-measured since step 7». So the PLAN does not claim the exit is met, and 48 games could never meet «100 per map set» anyway. What is new is the undercount in the headline: two passing games with defect records, plus the extra class in 630. I did not examine the box half (113–160) and did not check the «4 families» grouping.  
*Re-measure:* The run on the pin, Autopilot 46154080374 (74910b95e2), when it finishes: read its autopilot-browser.txt head. For «per map set», run gh workflow run Autopilot with games=100 per board.

**S0/board-fit-deferral-premise-false** — board-fit hold «tween on, 0 stable · owed nothing», deferred as the Moon's two-board layout, now fires on Hollandia in scope (#198)  
`[claim-stale · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Game 630 (single-board Hollandia, promo+colonies+prelude, in scope) hit the board-fit bound with the same word, «tween on, 0 stable · owed nothing · passes 1 of 6» (#198, open, filed after the pin). The symptom is not Moon-only. The Moon's two-board cause cannot explain one board, so the in-scope cause is still unnamed, and it happened after a stranded-prompt stall in the finale. PLAN 1436-40 already listed in-scope board-fit as an open race. The journal was incomplete, not false.  
*Evidence:* Issue #198 (created 2026-11-28T14:13Z, run 46144276012): `[scene-transition] hold "board-fit" hit its 6000ms bound … tween on, 0 stable · owed nothing · passes 1 of 6 … banned by a tween`. The game-630 cause file autopilot.table reads `vs MarsBot · Hollandia · promo+colonies+prelude · draft · random M&A · 2 humans`. PLAN 1441: `tween on, 0 stable · owed nothing · passes 1 of 6 · trail +87 drift size 116,-14`.

That is a visual judge for token flights, so «no report section counts a motion that did not play» is overstated for tokens. The beat law judges beats with nothing in flight (a stall, not a missing motion).

### S1-stage1-matrix — the stages’ exit numbers

*Scope:* Re-derived stage 1's 24-cell matrix from ci-chain.log, gh job names and headSha for the 18 CI cells, their 18 re-downloaded report artefacts, and the six box reports in tm-fz-tools/stage1-exit-evidence. Listed every Monkey run since the exit and downloaded 15 post-exit reports. Diffed LAYOUT_BLIND and both monkey baselines from the exit to the pin, counted fixtures, and read the monkey-report.mjs tier/gate logic and the monkey.yml schedules. No spec was run.

**S1/blind-list-grew-contradicted** — «The bottle's blind list shrinks only» is false: LAYOUT_BLIND went from 6 to 13 rows, and the real browser names at least 4 of them  
`[claim-not-met · medium (high) · partly]` stage 1  
*Statement:* PLAN.md:495's stage-1 exit number «the bottle's blind list shrinks only» held at the exit commit ab6ad53eba, but it does not hold after it. LAYOUT_BLIND went from 6 to 13 rows (7 added from 11-23 to 11-28, 0 removed). The real browser has since named at least 2 rows as defects: start:reactor-face (#199) and start-bonus:visible-source (autopilot 46144276012). The auditor also cites played-hero:target (nightly 45405220980), which I did not check. That makes 3 cited, not 4.  
*Evidence:* git show <c>:tests/client/bottle/consoleBottle.ts ¦ sed -n '/export const LAYOUT_BLIND/,/^];/p' ¦ grep -c '{cls:' gives 7 at ab6ad53eba and 14 at 74910b95e2 (the header line included). Rows were added by c1531646fb, 9e1f3d57e6, d421a83d39 (×2), b9254000a8 and 3f9cd36d49 (×2). dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt:13,15,43,44 marks start-bonus:visible-source and start:reactor-face as NEW. Exit cell 44638094890 lists start:reactor-face (planned).  
*Re-check:* Run the git show / grep -c above at both commits. Then grep -n 'reactor-face¦visible-source' in autopilot-browser.txt.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The comment on each added row cites a browser run that was silent when the row was added, so the add rule was followed as written. What failed is its premise. «Shrinks only» was true at the exit commit; it was broken after it. The title's «at least 4» is backed by only 3 classes. played-hero:target and the matrix record counts (9 and 10) come only from the auditor's report; I did not check them.

**S1/matrix-not-held-since-exit** — The matrix has not been held since the exit: 0 of 24 cells ran on the pin; cpu6 and net1500 never re-ran; perturbations ran on seed fz0 only  
`[claim-stale · medium (high) · partly]` stage 1  
*Statement:* No Monkey cell has run at 74910b95e2; the newest run is 85203abbe2, 5 commits behind. No schedule runs cpu6 or net1500. The Sunday crons hold only fz0 × cpu4/net400/reload/duo/hidden, now 229-268 commits old, and the hidden run went red. The daily plain pass uses a rotating d<date> seed, not fz0, so PLAN.md:275 («the matrix is the NIGHTLY's ... seed of the day») overstates the schedule. PLAN.md:22 §0 already says the matrix was not re-run.  
*Evidence:* git log --oneline ab6ad53eba..74910b95e2 ¦ wc -l gives 356. monkey.yml crons: plain nightly plus Sunday cpu4/net400/reload/duo/hidden, all SEED=fz0. freeze-gate.yml is the bottle with no perturbation. gh run list --workflow Monkey gives 45037581429 cpu4, 45051613431 net400, 45065721056 reload and 45073729286 duo (all success); 45087994773 hidden is a failure (NEW wait-graph/unshaped ×7); 45103521828 hidden: 1 walk.

*Against it (the verifier):* The finding says all crons use SEED=fz0. That is wrong: the nightly plain pass rotates a seed of the day. «Perturbations on fz0 only» holds for whole passes only; fz1 × cpu4 and fz3 × duo had partial runs. The auditor did not mention the post-exit fz1/fz3 × none whole passes. The journal §0 already admits the matrix was not re-run on head. I did not verify the cause of the hidden run's failure.  
*Re-measure:* Monkey workflow_dispatch on a ref at 74910b95e2, one at a time: seed ∈ {fz0,fz1,fz3} × perturb ∈ {none,cpu4,cpu6,net400,net1500,reload,duo,hidden}, shards=16, presses=60, repeat=1 (24 runs). Box half via npm run e2e:remote on tests/e2e/console-monkey.spec.ts, TM_E2E_MONKEY=1 TM_E2E_MONKEY_PRESSES=60 TM_E2E_BUDGET=12, seeds fz0/fz1/fz3 × TM_E2E_MONKEY_RELOAD=1 / TM_E2E_MONKEY_NET_MS=400¦1500.

**S1/box-half-commit-unverifiable** — The box half's «same commit» cannot be checked: no box artefact records a head; the worktree with the annotations is deleted

**S1/zero-defect-is-tier-dependent** — «0 defect» means 0 records tiered defect at the source. The exit came in the same 48 h as 29 tier-related commits; 361 planned records stayed

### S2-stage2-one-owner — the stages’ exit numbers

### S3-stage3-waits — the stages’ exit numbers

*Scope:* Stage 3 on pin 74910b95e2. Read the wait-graph inventory, the beat law in monkey-report.mjs, and its lines in CI runs 46144276012 / 46140902727. Scripts and raw output: <audit>/scratch\S3-stage3-waits\.

**S3/flag-class-not-closed** — «Flags as runs = 0» is the zero of `.active¦.live` in createFlowRun files; the class froze CI for 35 s one commit before the pin  
`[claim-not-met · medium (high) · partly]` stage 3

Flags outside are unguarded, e.g. boardBeatPark `draining` (7 writes, feeds deriveFlowRun) stuck 35 s on CI until its ceiling, fixed pointwise 4392fb1fc2. Lexically 33 + ~70 more writes exist; some are UI mode, not waits. PLAN §0 «—» and :943 overstate.  
*Evidence:* `git show 4392fb1fc2` (HEAD~1, #187/#189): «board-beat-drain held 35 s and force-released by its ceiling — draining: true with no values held… left the flag up»; the fix is one more `boardBeatParkState.draining = false` (boardBeatPark.ts:714-719). boardBeatPark.ts: 0 createFlowRun(, 1 deriveFlowRun(, 7 `draining =` writes — outside both guards. architectureDebt.spec.ts:69-71: pattern `\b\w+\.(?:active¦live) = (?:true¦false)\b`, `inFilesWith: /\bcreateFlowRun\(/`. scratch byhand.cjs: «in files WITH createFlowRun: 0 ¦ WITHOUT (never counted): 33 in 18 files».  
*Re-check:* git -C . show 4392fb1fc2 -- src/client/console/boardBeatPark.ts ; then `node <audit>/scratch/S3-stage3-waits/byhand.cjs .` and `node …/flags.cjs . out.tsv`.

The :497 exit criterion is scene-scoped and is literally met. The counts are lexical and include UI-mode flags (handSelectState.active, sale.active, awardFundingState.active) that are not waits. The 35 s stall was bounded by a ceiling. The 34 raise-await-lower figure was not verified.

*Not examined by this auditor:* The bottle's fault pass itself (forbidden here): «5 moves only the fault pass makes» is inferred from their absence in CI's corpus plus the ledger's reasons — no fault-pass artefact exists on CI to read. · Whether the unit corpus marks `state submit presenting → abandoned` (gameTransportFunnel.spec.ts:330 drives it; the ledger says no pass walks it) — one `test:client:one` with TM_UNIT_COVERAGE would settle it; not run. · Which commit of f50a9f0721…983c3b60d0 introduced the stranded prompt after a weather fault, and whether 4392fb1fc2 (in the pin) changes it — no Autopilot run exists on the pin. · Whether `verdict → configure` is new or only newly reached, and whether it is a defect of the Parliament's restore or a row the protocol owes — the walk was not replayed. · The proof (or disproof) behind the 12 «never made aside» frame rows — I took the ledger's own «not proven by reading» at its word. · viewDelivery.ts and the poll's outcome handling line by line; realtimeService.ts beyond its state list; the batch submit (`/player/input-batch`) under a fault. · The nightly freeze gate's own coverage numbers (its report artefact was not downloaded) and the Monkey's Sunday perturbation passes. · tests/console/kernel/*.spec.ts (flowLaw, flowMachineRegister, the property spec) were not run — only tests/scripts/kernelWalked.spec.ts and my scratch count spec. · Whether `gate:tableAnswer` is reachable by a viewer's own submit in a real game (the payout may always arrive by the poll's road) — only its absence from the corpus and from every denominator was established.

### S5-stage5-server — the stages’ exit numbers

*Scope:* Read tests/fuzz/{engineFuzz.ts, engineFuzz.spec.ts, randomAnswer.ts (parts), engineFuzzBaseline.json}, engine-fuzz.yml, the PlayerInput / PlayerInputBatch routes, BotTurnScheduler, creator defaults. Downloaded and tabulated 10 Engine fuzz reports (5 scheduled, 5 dispatched). Ran engineFuzz.spec on the pin (plus a no-timeout re-run of 2 tests), a scratch spec measuring reload stats on 6 nightly seeds, and a scratch spec counting in-scope card coverage. Listed fuzz ci-finding issues.

**S5/nightly-volume-quarter-million** — The nightly plays 0.22–0.32 million answers, not «N million». The 1.85 M figure came from three 100-minute runs started by hand  
`[claim-not-met · medium (high) · partly]` stage 5  
*Statement:* Stage 5's exit (PLAN.md:499, «N million engine steps nightly, 0 violations») is not met on volume. Each scheduled night runs 40 min at 90–133 steps/s, about 0.22–0.32 M steps. The nights 11-25 to 11-28 made 946 799 steps over 3 697 games, all ended, 0 classes. The 1.85 M (PLAN:2173) is three hand-dispatched 100-min runs, as the plan itself says, and §0 lists «nightly volume not re-read» as open. There is no volume floor.  
*Evidence:* Report engine-fuzz-<id>-1/engine-fuzz.json, fields steps / stepsPerSecond / wallSeconds: 46106541093 (8395cdaf75) 277009 / 116 / 2400; 45940463172 (cb68ebdb30) 215401 / 90; 45754724050 (a3c1a4cd33) 218022 / 91; 45550698846 (3b512adfce) 236367 / 99; 45339986435 (e8cdeb6d18) 318856 / 133. engine-fuzz.yml: TM_FUZZ_MINUTES default '40'. The 1.85 M comes from dispatches 45444671974, 45444680952 and 45444690139 (wall 6001 s each).  
*Re-check:* gh run download 46106541093 --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars -n engine-fuzz-46106541093-1 -D <dir>; node -e "const r=require('<dir>/engine-fuzz.json');console.log(r.steps,r.stepsPerSecond,r.wallSeconds)"  
*Against it (the verifier):* PLAN:2173 labels the 1.85 M openly as «three nights' ranges played ahead, a hundred minutes each», so it is not passed off as nightly volume. PLAN:26 (§0) claims only that the fuzz stands and lists «the nightly volume and its zero not re-read» as open, so the plan does not claim the exit is met. N is an unset placeholder. The laptop rate of 742–817/s was not checked.

**S5/default-product-table-not-drawn** — Options the fuzz never draws: random M&A and the shuffled map (both on by default in the creator), the campaign, and the production MarsBot scheduler  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage 5  
*Statement:* The ENGINE fuzz (fuzzConfig, FuzzGameOptions engineFuzz.ts:67-73) can't set randomMA, shuffleMapOption, undo, twoCorps or campaign, and MarsBot always resolves inline (scheduler off). So its random reload lane never loads the creator's default-on M&A/shuffled map or a pendingTurn save. The e2e autopilot draws those rules (p=0.3) on a live server with the scheduler on, and unit specs cover pendingTurn reload.  
*Evidence:* engineFuzz.ts:220-237: the options object has no randomMA or shuffleMapOption. defaultCreateGameModel.ts:47 sets `randomMA: RandomMAOptionType.ALL` and :71 `shuffleMapOption: true`. createGameState.ts:171-172 maps these to the rules. GameOptions.ts:175 defaults to `randomMA: NONE` and :182 to `shuffleMapOption: false`. BotTurnScheduler.ts:157: `if (this.enabled === false ...) { this.resolveTurn(game); return; }`. engineFuzz.ts:30-31 states the bot always resolves inline.  
*Re-check:* grep -n 'randomMA\¦shuffleMap\¦campaign' tests/fuzz/engineFuzz.ts (no hits); sed -n 40,75p src/client/components/create/defaultCreateGameModel.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* The e2e autopilot (stage 6) samples random M&A, the shuffled map, fast mode and others on its own stream (console-autopilot.spec.ts:269-292, PLAN:2728/2743) against a real GameServer with the scheduler enabled. That plays production pacing and the ack. pendingTurn save/recover has dedicated unit specs. "Never fuzzed / never played or reload-checked" holds only for the engine fuzz, not for the program.  
*Re-measure:* After widening fuzzConfig: workflow_dispatch «Engine fuzz» with inputs seed=<fresh>, minutes=100, reload=2.

**S5/batch-route-unfuzzed** — The batch route (submitBatch → PlayerInputBatch), which the console uses to play cards in one press, is never exercised. «Next: the batch route» was never closed  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage 5  
*Statement:* The server engine fuzz (stage 5) drives only the single-input route (process + drainBatchTail). It never submits a PlayerInputBatch, an undo or an AcknowledgeDraw, so its I1–I4/R are never judged after a batch request. PLAN «Next: … the batch route» (:1499, :1574) is still open. The batch route itself IS exercised elsewhere: by the CI autopilot composer and by tests/routes/PlayerInputBatch.spec.ts.  
*Evidence:* engineFuzz.ts:743-754 mirrors routes/PlayerInput.ts:107-145 only. `grep -n -i batch tests/fuzz/*.ts` finds only the import and call of drainBatchTail (engineFuzz.ts:58, :753). src/server/routes/PlayerInputBatch.ts:'Submits an ORDERED ARRAY of input responses in one request'. Client: gameTransport.ts:707 `export function submitBatch`.  
*Re-check:* grep -n -i 'batch' tests/fuzz/engineFuzz.ts tests/fuzz/randomAnswer.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* The title's «never exercised» is false. The CI autopilot e2e plays through the composer's /player/input-batch, and it already caught a batch bug (run 46079806794, game 1037, preselectParked fix). A deterministic route spec also covers PlayerInputBatch. The PLAN line numbers are cited off by about 20 and 1.

**S5/reload-sampled-and-unreported** — Reload equivalence judges about 21 % of answers in the nightly, the whole view 20 %, and the nightly report does not count judged saves  
`[metric-gap · low (medium) · confirmed]` stage 5  
*Statement:* Reload equivalence does run nightly (TM_FUZZ_RELOAD=2), but only on answers that saved and did not move afterwards, and then on every second one. Measured on 6 nightly seeds: 384 judged out of 1867 answers (21 %). The full-view check V runs on 1 answer in 5. FuzzReport drops result.reload, so no artefact can tell «0 R» from «0 judged».  
*Evidence:* engineFuzz.ts:899: `savesJudged++ % Math.max(1, hooks.reload?.every ?? 1) === 0`. engineFuzz.ts:684: `result.steps % Math.max(1, config.viewEvery) === 0 ? Server.getPlayerModel(...)`. spec :377 defaults VIEW_EVERY to 5. addToReport (engineFuzz.ts:1036-1077) never reads result.reload. `grep -c reload` over all 10 report JSONs returns 0. Scratch reloadStats.spec.ts, seeds 2078500000..05: judged 60/57/101/110/16/40 of steps 260/251/498/608/86/164.  
*Re-check:* cd . && npx mocha --import=tsx --require tests/testing/setup.ts <audit>/scratch/S5-stage5-server/reloadStats.spec.ts ¦ grep ^20785  
*Against it (the verifier):* By design (engineFuzz.ts:479-481), answers without a save and saves the engine moved past cannot be judged. Only the every=2 sampling is a choice, and it halves the checkable set. engineFuzz.spec.ts:105 and :338 assert judged>0 and judged>50 per seed, which guards against R silently judging nothing (in the unit suite, not in the nightly). V does run on every answer through Server.getWaitingFor; only the whole view is sampled.

**S5/no-shrinking-seeds-drift** — «every violation is a shrunk seed»: there is no shrinker, and a held seed replays a different game once the engine changes  
`[claim-not-met · low (medium) · partly]` stage 5  
*Statement:* The stage-5 engine fuzz has no shrinker. Its held finds are whole 4000-step seed replays (engineFuzz.spec.ts:266/315/335) whose answers come from the live model, so they drift with the engine and config, as the spec admits (262-264). PLAN:499 «shrunk seed» is unmet for the fuzz. The fix half holds (6 issues closed). A shrinker exists only for the bottle monkey (tests/client/bottle/shrink.ts).  
*Evidence:* `grep -rni shrink tests/fuzz scripts/ci/findings.mjs` gives no output. engineFuzz.spec.ts:262-264: «if the space is widened these two stop telling this story and say only «a whole game ends clean»». engineFuzz.spec.ts:315 and :335 hold 16 seeds at maxSteps 4000. gh issue list --label ci-finding --state all shows #85/#86/#99/#106/#107/#133 all CLOSED.  
*Re-check:* grep -rn -i shrink tests/fuzz; sed -n 314,341p tests/fuzz/engineFuzz.spec.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* The repo has a delta-debugging shrinker for bottle-monkey walks: tests/client/bottle/shrink.ts and TM_BOTTLE_MONKEY_SHRINK, used per PLAN:62/230/270/273. So «there is no shrinker» is true only of the engine fuzz. Two PLAN line cites are off by 1-2. I did not check that each of the six has its own card spec.

**S5/nightly-replays-played-ranges** — Two of the four nightlies after 11-24 replayed seed ranges that hand-run dispatches had already played. Fresh-seed volume since 11-25 is 1 916 games  
`[process-risk · low (medium) · confirmed]` stage 5  
*Statement:* Dispatches had played 2078200000 three times (up to 3 067 games) and 2078300000 once (2 051 games) before the 11-25 and 11-26 nightlies ran. Those nightlies (944 and 837 games) were prefixes of ranges already played, on newer code, so they were regression runs. Only 11-27 and 11-28 played fresh seeds: 1 916 games, 492 410 answers. The cron is 22:43 but runs start 04:13–05:01 UTC, so each night plays the next day's range.  
*Evidence:* firstSeed/games: 45360158843 2078200000/819; 45444680952 2078200000/2231 (#133); 45469211894 2078200000/3067; nightly 45550698846 2078200000/944. 45444690139 2078300000/2051; nightly 45754724050 2078300000/837. Nightlies 45940463172 2078400000/853 and 46106541093 2078500000/1063 are fresh. createdAt of the nightlies is 04:13–05:01Z.  
*Re-check:* for id in 45444690139 45754724050; do gh run download $id --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars -n engine-fuzz-$id-1 -D d/$id; node -e "const r=require('./d/$id/engine-fuzz.json');console.log(r.firstSeed,r.games)"; done  
*Against it (the verifier):* The SHAs differ, so the replayed seeds may play out differently on the newer code. They are not identical replays, which fits the finding's own word «regression runs». I found no doc that acknowledges the overlap. There is one citation slip: the line number is wrong (10, not 8).

**S5/judge-level-excuses** — Known classes are excused in the judge, not the baseline: reload comparison drops card `warnings`; refusal classes are never ledgered  
`[check-weakened · low (low) · partly]` stage 5  
*Statement:* engineFuzzBaseline.json is empty, but it does not count two exclusions made in the judge. The reload check drops card `warnings` (engineFuzz.ts:499). The plan admits the hypothetical-play warnings class is still open (PLAN 1541-1546). Refusals are tallied in every report (192 data / 41 floater trade, 5 Soil Enrichment, 1 Deimos Down:ares 'No available spaces') but never classified, and they fail only at 25 in a row. Both are documented with reasons, not silent.

PLAN.md:1541-1546: «…is the same class and is still there: the check reads `warnings` apart, and the finding stands in this line». Aggregated `refusals` over the 10 reports: 'or ¦ action-menu ¦ No card of yours can hold the data this trade pays' 192, '…floaters…' 41, 'or ¦ Deimos Down:ares ¦ No available spaces' 1 (45444671974). Colonies.ts:276-290 explains the trade refusal as a designed re-judge.  
*Re-check:* sed -n 488,506p tests/fuzz/engineFuzz.ts; jq '.refusals' over any engine-fuzz.json  
*Against it (the verifier):* Nothing is silent. Judge-level laws are an explicit, written policy (PLAN 1501 «one in the judge», 1515 «written as the law (one line in the judge), never as a baseline row per card»). The warnings exclusion has its reason in the code and in PLAN. The refusal tally is printed in every report. «Baseline is EMPTY» (1503) was written before the reload step. Citations slip: PLAN line 1503, not 1519; Colonies.ts is src/server/player/.

**S5/zero-and-closure-confirmed** — Confirmed: baseline empty, every fuzz finding closed with a seed and a card spec, four green nightlies, controls bite, project-card axis 535/535  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage 5  
*Statement:* On the pin the baseline is {}. Every invariant's planted-defect control names its defect. All 16 held seeds end clean. The nightlies of 11-25…11-28 report 0 classes, 0 stuck, 0 capped. *Not examined by this auditor:* Why 'Deimos Down:ares ¦ No available spaces' was refused once in 45444671974: possibly an offered card with no cell, the class #133 closed for oceans only. · randomAnswer.ts beyond the OR and colony branches (payment mix, amounts, Hydronetwork answers): not read line by line, for time. · The internals of tableStandsDead / unanswerableInput (which states they declare dead or unanswerable): only their call sites were read. · scripts/ci/findings.mjs from-fuzz filing logic: read only through the issue it produced. · Whether a 40-minute nightly with reload=1 and viewEvery=1 would find anything: that needs CI (workflow_dispatch Engine fuzz, reload=1) and the workflow has no input for viewEvery. · The cards-axis definition (cards counted only when a human menu answer names them) was checked against manifests, but not against bot-played cards.

### S6a-stage6-whole-games — the stages’ exit numbers

*Scope:* Stage 6's first exit number («100 whole games per map set, 0 episodes, 0 rebuilds») on pin 74910b95e2. Read: PLAN.md §0/§8 entries by line, the autopilot spec header + table/game loop, autopilot.yml, monkey-report.mjs gate; all 47 Autopilot runs via read-only gh (ranges from job names); 14 report artefacts (5 given + 9 downloaded) and every game file of run 46144276012; game 632's walk token by token; per-board tallies by a replica of the spec's rng (30/30 vs real tables). No browser, box, bottle or spec run.

**S6a/capped-game-632-frozen-parliament** — Game 632: the Parliament stood on «Выполняется…» for 2 854 of 3 200 presses — booked «1 capped», 0 records, test passed, nothing filed  
`[harness-masks-product · critical (critical) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* A fifth game of that pass froze. Game 632, generation 4: «Nationalist Movement» from the hand → the chairman quest's frame collapsed and re-opened → `parliament:browse` with «Парламент · A Выполняется…» for 2 854 presses (B ×120 a question) under 23 questions, each answered by the pilot's DOOR. No net named it, the test passed, the gate booked «1 capped», no issue exists. Cause not established.  
*Evidence:* dl\autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0\autopilot-browser-46144276012-1-7\test-results\…game-632-chromium\autopilot-walk.txt: "end": "capped", byUi 31, byApi 20 + passes.api 4, 24 `doors` rows all «frames parliament:browse · bar «ПарламентAВыполняется…» · grey Enter»; walk tokens 337-344: [PLAYED Nationalist Movement] → `hand:committed…>parliament:executing¦option#1834` → `{parliament:browse¦option#1834¦ПарламентAВыполняется…}`. Its cause-classes file: status passed, records []. ci-findings-browser.json: 8 findings, none names 632. `grep -n "game 632" PLAN.md` → nothing.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/S6a-stage6-whole-games/walk632b.mjs <that autopilot-walk.txt> 330 346 — prints the 24 runs of ≥ 25 presses under one state (23 are this bar) and the road in; `sed -n '1,45p'` of the walk shows end / byApi / doors.  
*Against it (the verifier):* This was a slow-machine game (cpu 3), driven by the pilot and not a human, and the cause is unknown. The pilot's Escape ×120 and HOME presses did not free the screen, but there is no proof a human could not have got out. The browser report does list the door rows (all from 632) to a reader, so the symptom was visible, just not filed or turned red. The spec path is tests/e2e/, not tests/console/.  
*Re-measure:* gh workflow run "Autopilot" --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --ref main -f from=632 -f games=1 -f shards=1 -f name=ap0 -f where=browser (a game is a function of its number; 632 is a slow-machine game, CPU ÷3). Then the same game on the box with TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_SLOW=off to learn whether the slow frame is a condition.

**S6a/fresh-range-always-red** — Nine of the last ten Autopilot runs red; the one green is the fifth replay of one range; every fresh range names new classes (4, then 7)  
`[process-risk · high (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Of the last ten completed Autopilot runs, nine are red. Range 537+48 was played 7 times: 4 red, then one green (46072102662), then 2 red. The two fresh ranges (1001 and 585) each failed the gate, with 4 and 7 classes labelled «NEW». Many of those are returns, not first sightings: flow:rally, mandatory-choice×n, the stranded-choice family, board-fit and start-bonus. Each fresh range brings about 3 classes never seen before. Convergence is not shown.

*Against it (the verifier):* The gate counts a class as «NEW» against its known-class baseline, not as first ever seen. flow:rally first fired in 46018935113. PLAN:3296 limits its «Zero» to the browser half, admits the bottle episode, and lists the confirming pass on the head as open.

*Re-measure:* The pass on the pin is in flight: run 46154080374 (74910b95e2, games 633 – 680, both worlds). When done: gh run download 46154080374 --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars -D <empty dir>; node scripts/monkey-report.mjs <dir of the browser shards> --against tests/e2e/autopilotBaseline.json --gate --zero --out <file>; open every capped row and every door row.

**S6a/rebuilds-and-stuck-not-measured-in-browser** — «rebuilds 0» is a constant in the real browser and «STUCK» watches the server's game age — two of the exit's three words are not counts  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* In the browser autopilot, «rebuilds» is a constant 0: the spec never writes it and the report counts a missing field as 0. So «rebuilds 0» sits beside «episodes 2 (rebuilt 2)» (46144276012). STUCK only counts 10 minutes of unchanged server gameAge while no question stands for the viewer; a frozen screen with a question standing becomes a door row. The gate still fails rebuilt episodes through NEVER CURED, and the 100-game passes had 0 episodes.  
*Evidence:* `grep -c -i rebuil tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts` → 0; tests/client/bottle/autopilot.bottle.ts:237 `rebuilds = bottle.rebuildsAsked()`, :265; scripts/monkey-report.mjs:730 `out.rebuilds += Number(a.rebuilds ?? 0)`. Run 46144276012 autopilot-browser.txt: line 2 «episodes 2 (rebuilt 2) · NEVER CURED 2», then «THE WHOLE GAMES — 48 game(s): 47 to the final scoring · 1 capped · 0 STUCK · rebuilds 0»; the same pair in run 45997519544 («rebuilt 1» / «rebuilds 0»). Spec:1049 `if (model.game.gameAge !== moved.age)` … 1064 `played.end = 'stuck'`; the door: 1206-1248.  
*Re-check:* cd . && grep -c -i rebuil tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts ; grep -n rebuilds scripts/monkey-report.mjs tests/client/bottle/autopilot.bottle.ts ; sed -n '1046,1066p' tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts ; grep -n 'rebuil' <run 46144276012>/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt  
*Against it (the verifier):* STUCK does count something real, a stalled server while no question stands; it just cannot see a frozen screen, which goes to the door rows instead. The «0 rebuilds» quoted from the 100-game passes is backed indirectly by «episodes 0», and a liveness rebuild fails the gate through NEVER CURED. So the printed number misleads, but the gate has not falsely passed. Not checked: rebuilds outside liveness episodes, and whether door rows fail the gate.

### S6b-stage6-time — the stages’ exit numbers

*Scope:* I re-read PLAN.md step 7 (1891–2025) and the soak, census and soak-2 entries (1946, 1959, 1974, 2080, 2101, 3302). I read the soak code in console-autopilot.spec.ts, the frame-budget, key-trace and style-cost probes, longFrameWatch.ts, classify.ts, hub.ts, the workflows, playwright.config.ts, App.vue and loadingScreenState.ts, plus the storage writers. I counted [long-frame] lines in every pre-downloaded artefact and matched each game against its [SLOW ×3] mark. I ran no specs and did not touch the box.

**S6b/soak-8h-never-run** — No 8-hour soak was ever completed: the longest is 2.5 h (game 402); game 514 was stopped at about 2 h; both ~134-155 commits before the pin  
`[claim-not-met · high (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Not met. The longest soak on record is game 402 on the box: 5 spells, 2.5 h of idle (PLAN.md:1974). Game 514 (40 min a spell, planned 8.7 h) was stopped at about 2 h when the box was taken for the owner's game (PLAN.md:2101 «it is owed too»). No later run exists. The soak code dates from e0c73ed8d9, 155 commits before the pin.  
*Evidence:* PLAN.md:1974 «THE SOAK, FIVE SPELLS IN (game 402, 2.5 h of a page left alone in all)»; PLAN.md:2101 «The second soak (game 514, two hours in) went with them — it is owed too»; PLAN.md:27 and 3302 «the 8-hour soak — not re-measured since step 7». `git rev-list --count e0c73ed8d9..74910b95e2` = 155; `2e26031ea2..74910b95e2` = 134. No [autopilot-soak] line exists in tm-fz-tools (grep -c = 0 in g514.txt, e2e_fzd.log, ap_soak5*.log).  
*Re-check:* grep -n -i soak docs/claude/console/freeze-zero/PLAN.md ¦ cut -c1-200 (nothing after 2101 except 3302); git -C . rev-list --count e0c73ed8d9..74910b95e2  
*Against it (the verifier):* The journal does not hide the gap: 2101 says "owed", and §0 row 27 and line 3302 say "not re-measured". The game 514 "≈2 h" comes from the journal only; no artefact backs it. The box lane directories were not readable under the audit rules. Lines 1946-1948 also record node-count growth across game 402's spells, which leaves "flat memory" open even for the 2.5 h already run.  
*Re-measure:* On the box, inside a do-not-disturb window: npm run e2e:snapshot <task>; then node tm-fz-tools/monkey-lanes.mjs <worktree> <task> soak-8h 0 1 1 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT=1 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_GAMES=<n with n%5!=2, n%3!=0> TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_IDLE_MIN=40 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_SLOW=off TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_WEATHER=clear. Read the [autopilot-soak] series.

**S6b/flat-is-by-eye** — «Flat» is decided by eye: no expect, report or threshold reads the spells, and nobody computes the trend across spells  
`[metric-gap · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* No code, expect, threshold or report script judges the soak series. console-autopilot.spec.ts:1197-1200 only pushes and logs each spell. Its only verdict expects (1969-1970) are page-alive and not-stuck, and monkey-report / kernel-walked / autopilot-cards never read spells. The cross-spell trend was computed by hand in the journal (PLAN:1947-1949, nodes 5 078 → 8 118 → 14 535) and led to detached-tree reporting, but it has no automated slope or ceiling.  
*Evidence:* tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts:1197-1200: `played.spells.push(spell)` and a console.log, with no expect on any field. The end-of-test expects are only `page.isClosed()` and `played.end !== 'stuck'`. A grep for spells/heapDelta finds 0 matches in scripts/monkey-report.mjs, kernel-walked.mjs and autopilot-cards.mts. PLAN.md:1947-1949 records the node growth between spells (5 078 · 8 118 · 14 535).  
*Re-check:* grep -n 'spells' tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts scripts/monkey-report.mjs; sed -n '1180,1202p' tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* "Nobody computes the trend across spells" is overstated. PLAN:1947-1949 explicitly computes the growth between spells and contrasts it with the roughly 1 300 attached elements. That led to a follow-up instrument: per-spell detached-tree reporting (spec 1193, 1199-1200; game 403). The "flat" wording at PLAN:1899-1901 covers the first spell's in-spell deltas only, and the journal does not call the series flat. The soak is an instrument still being read, not a claimed gate.

**S6b/census-pins-soak-series** — Every spell calls DOM.getDetachedDomNodes, which the journal found PINS what it reports, so the soak's own node/heap series is inflated by the probe  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The perturbation is still in the soak on the pin. detachedNow() runs unconditionally after every spell. The next spell's CDP Nodes reading (and probably heapMb) therefore includes trees the census itself keeps alive. The cross-spell growth that the 8-hour number must judge would be partly the probe's. Game 402 ran without the census; any soak on the pin runs with it.  
*Evidence:* tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts:1153-1178 defines detachedNow() around `cdp.send('DOM.getDetachedDomNodes')`. :1185 calls `const detached = await detachedNow();` once per spell with no flag. The call was added by 824071bdbe (2026-11-24), after the soak commit e0c73ed8d9.  
*Re-check:* sed -n '1148,1190p' tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts ¦ cut -c1-200; git -C . log --format='%h %s' -S getDetachedDomNodes -- tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* The within-spell nodesDelta/heapDeltaMb come from ask() before the census, so they are unaffected. cdp.detach() runs after each census, and I did not check whether Chromium drops the pins on detach; the journal's empirical note is the only evidence that they persist. "Probably heapMb" is unproven. The journal already acknowledges the contamination. In game 403 the census total was about 2.9k nodes, real leaks included.

**S6b/frame-budget-is-probe-only** — The «frame budget of a press» has no guard: three opt-in probes with no number, measured at a desktop viewport, not the 4K TV profile  
`[claim-unmeasured · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 6

The quiet-box table (PLAN.md:2007-2020) leaves the Parliament open at 144 median / 191 ms worst, «a hitch a player can feel», and the next step (one measure, one write) is not recorded as done. The probes set no viewport or consoleProfile, so they run at Playwright's Desktop Chrome / 1440x1000 viewport, not the TV profile.

The key-trace probe (:366, :412) and the style-cost probe (:28) both say «a measurement, never a gate». A grep for setViewportSize/viewport/consoleProfile/3840 in the frame-budget and autopilot specs returns nothing. playwright.config.ts:126 sets viewport {1440,1000} and :158 spreads devices['Desktop Chrome']. No workflow sets TM_E2E_FRAME_BUDGET.  
*Re-check:* grep -n 'test.skip\¦expect(' tests/e2e/console-frame-budget.spec.ts; grep -rn FRAME_BUDGET .github/ (empty)  
*Against it (the verifier):* console-longgame-perf-probe.spec.ts:55-62 measures at tv-4k (3840x2160, consoleProfile=tv), but it is opt-in (LONGGAME_PERF=1) and its expects are census sanity checks, not budgets. Commit 94ebed5104 did partial Parliament work (publishMidOffsets 49 to 26 ms), but the item stays open in PLAN.  
*Re-measure:* On the box: TM_E2E_FRAME_BUDGET=1 TM_E2E_FRAME_BUDGET_GENERATIONS=7 TM_E2E_GPU=1, with the page at ?consoleProfile=tv and a 3840x2160 viewport (the spec needs a knob for both): npm run e2e:remote -- <task> tests/e2e/console-frame-budget.spec.ts

**S6b/slow-frames-booked-apart** — All 122 long frames of the last full Autopilot run are from CPU×3 games and are booked apart wholesale; unthrottled games print 0  
`[blind-spot · medium (medium) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0): 38 unthrottled games print 0 [long-frame]. All 122 come from 10 CPU×3 games (505/647/1033 ms; 57 onkeydown + 25 onkeyup). The spec (:1839-1842) keeps them out of the finding book, and the report keeps only a total (txt:50). The raw lines do survive in each walk's artefact, but nothing aggregates them by screen or site or ratchets them run over run, so «no slow-machine long-frame growth» is unmeasured.  
*Evidence:* Run 46144276012: browser walk files carry 122 [long-frame] lines in 10 games (602:37, 592:34, 587:14, 632:9, 622:9, 627:8, 597:5, 607:4, 617:1, 612:1); every one of the 10 walks carries [SLOW ×3]; the other 38 games have 0. Durations 505 / 647 (median) / 1033 ms. Sites: onkeydown 57, onkeyup 25. The diversion is spec :1839-1841 `if (slow > 1 && text.startsWith('[long-frame]')) { slowFrames++; return; }`. autopilot-browser.txt:50.  
*Re-check:* cd tm-fz-p-audit/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0; for f in $(find . -path '*browser*' -name autopilot-walk.txt); do echo $(grep -c '\[long-frame\]' $f) $(grep -o '\[SLOW ×[0-9]\]' $f¦head -1); done ¦ sort ¦ uniq -c  
*Against it (the verifier):* «Dropped from the log» is overstated. Each slow walk's autopilot-walk.txt still holds every raw [long-frame] line with its attribution (site, script, «a press waited in it»), and the recheck counts exactly those. Only the book and the report drop them. A key-listener frame does not always mean a press waited: only the lines marked «a press waited in it» show that. The upper bound is 1.03 s, not 1.0 s.

**S6b/soak-not-automated** — The soak and the frame-budget probe are not in any workflow; the Autopilot workflow (by hand, 300-min timeout) cannot host an 8-hour soak  
`[process-risk · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The soak is a one-off, by hand, on a box that the owner's games preempt. I did not examine the box lane tooling in tm-fz-tools.

**S6b/long-frame-never-sent** — [long-frame] is PERF tier and is never sent to Sentry, so it cannot flood tickets; the monkey and the e2e suite do not count it at all  
`[claim-confirmed · low (low) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* [long-frame] is PERF (classify.ts:75). The hub skips perf by policy (hub.ts:132); the line only rides the next event as trail and is counted per screen in sessionLedger.longFrames. The monkey and the cause-class harvest of every e2e spec drop it: causeOf gives no cause to perf, so cause-classes/monkey-report show 0 by construction. Only the autopilot spec and the opt-in frame-budget specs measure long frames.  
*Evidence:* src/common/diagnostics/classify.ts:75 PERF = ['long-frame', 'perf', 'bot-turn', 'console-overflow']; src/server/diagnostics/hub/hub.ts:132 `if (entry.kind === 'perf' ¦¦ …) return {skip: 'policy'}`; longFrameWatch.ts:133 console.warn(line). The line is not in shapes.ts. grep long-frame in tests/e2e/console-monkey.spec.ts finds nothing; monkey-report-46140902727 and e2e-46144266613 cause-classes have 0 mentions.  
*Re-check:* grep -n "'long-frame'" src/common/diagnostics/classify.ts; sed -n '131,134p' src/server/diagnostics/hub/hub.ts; grep -c long-frame tm-fz-p-audit/dl/monkey-report-46140902727-85203abbe2/monkey-report.txt

The monkey does record long-frame lines word for word in the console tail of monkey-walk.txt (last 400 lines), but it does not count them. I did not trace the hub code that attaches the trail line by line.

**S6b/cross-game-state-unseen** — «One page = one game» holds (full reload), but the Electron main process, the reused renderer and per-game localStorage keys outlive a game and no soak reads them  
`[blind-spot · low (medium) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* A game enters and leaves by a full reload (App.vue:933-938, loadingScreenState.ts:614), but no soak runs a multi-game Electron session, so main-process and cross-game localStorage state goes unobserved. CardOrderStorage is dead code (nothing imports it). The real unpruned per-game store is fieldRecorder's `tm_field_rec:<playerId>` (fieldRecorder.ts:338-343): each key is ring-bounded, but there is one per game and none is ever removed. Main-process growth has not been measured.  
*Evidence:* App.vue:933-938 «GAME-BOUNDARY navigations … intentionally stay full reloads»; loadingScreenState.ts:614 window.location.assign(url). Bounded stores: sessionLedger.ts:23-25 (120 classes / 60 episodes); diagnosticsJournalState.ts:98 slice(-80). Unbounded: CardOrderStorage.ts:30 setItem(`${STORAGE_PREFIX}${playerId}`). No IndexedDB; the service worker is empty and skipped on app://.  
*Re-check:* sed -n '930,942p' src/client/components/App.vue; grep -n 'location.assign' src/client/console/loadingScreenState.ts; grep -n setItem src/client/utils/CardOrderStorage.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* The cited unbounded store, CardOrderStorage, never runs because nothing imports it. The real accumulating key is size-bounded per game, roughly tens of KB, so it can hit the quota only after very many games. GameLoader has cache eviction and an idle sweep. There is no evidence of main-process growth; it is unmeasured, not shown. I did not examine the hub or ledger bounds in electron/.  
*Re-measure:* On the box, an Electron session of N≥6 consecutive games through «next game» (not a fresh launch), sampling the main process's and the renderer's memory (process.memoryUsage / app.getAppMetrics) at each game boundary.

*Not examined by this auditor:* The box's soak logs (fzs-soak, fzd-soak): on the box, which the rules forbid. · console-heap-retainers.spec.ts and the retainer fixes it led to (CardZoomModal loreModel, ConsoleJournalPanel focusable): not re-verified on the pin. · console-longgame-perf-probe.spec.ts and console-played-perf-probe.spec.ts: seen by name only. · Timer drift over hours, named in the stage row: no instrument located, not searched further. · The session ledger's per-screen longFrames in field / Sentry trails: not read, because npm run diag talks to Sentry. · The per-game spells field in the downloaded autopilot game files: those CI runs had IDLE_MIN=0, so spells are empty; not opened.

### S6c-zero-bar-data — the stages’ exit numbers

*Scope:* Read autopilot.yml (report job) and monkey-report.mjs (main, gateVerdict, compareReports, aggregateAutopilot). Re-ran monkey-report --gate --zero over copies of the bottle's and the browser's 8 shards of run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0) and diffed the output against the artefact JSON. Recomputed gate and zero verdicts with the pin's gateVerdict for the four earlier report artefacts. Mapped every defect class to a ci-finding issue. Read the cause files and walk of the games with odd outcomes (621, 630, 632), the bottle's LAYOUT_BLIND list and its baseline.

**S6c/game-632-parliament-frozen-by-door** — Browser game 632: Parliament stood at «Выполняется…» for gen 4→8, 24 answers by the door, test PASSED, zero records  
`[harness-masks-product · high (critical) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0), browser game 632: in every generation 4-8, the Parliament adjourn, draft and buy stretch was answered by the door (20 answers + 4 passes) under parliament:browse with «Выполняется…» and a grey Enter. The test passed (capped, 0 records) and the journal is silent. Not a continuous freeze: action phases were played via the UI. Owner is announce-gate only on draft/buy rows. The --zero bar flags it, CI does not apply it.  
*Evidence:* Cause file autopilot-browser-46144276012-1-7/test-results/cause-classes/console-autopilot--autopilot-game-632-r0.json: status passed, end capped, gen 8 drafting, byApi {option:5, card:15}, passes.api 4, doors 24, records 0. Walk …game-632-chromium/autopilot-walk.txt: «[GEN 4…] [BY-DOOR option] [PASS-BY-DOOR] [BY-DOOR card] [GEN 5…] [BY-DOOR option] [BY-DOOR card]×4 [PASS-BY-DOOR] …». The report's THE DOOR ANSWERED rows read «owner «draft held admission:announce-gate — frames [parliament]»».  
*Re-check:* node -e over the 632 cause file, printing status, autopilot.end, byApi and doors.length. Then grep -oE "\[GEN [^]]*\]¦\[BY-DOOR [^]]*\]" on its autopilot-walk.txt.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The owner is announce-gate only on the 16 draft/buy rows. Chairman/adjourn rows read «serving parliament» (some add a remote-landing block), and the passes read «free». The game progressed: the UI played 15 action-menu turns and T went -26 to -14, so «stood gen 4→8» overstates it. monkey-report --zero would fail this game; CI's gate just omits --zero. Product freeze vs pilot blind spot is unresolved without the remeasure.  
*Re-measure:* gh workflow run Autopilot -f from=632 -f games=1 -f where=browser (and the same seed with TM_BOTTLE_AUTOPILOT_SEEDS=632 in the bottle) to see whether «Выполняется…» under announce-gate is a product freeze or a pilot blind spot

**S6c/ci-gate-is-not-zero-bar** — The zero bar (--zero) is run by no workflow; a CI «success» is a FAIL under it; 0 of 10 world-passes are clean  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The flag works (monkey-report.mjs:950-973), but nothing invokes it. CI gates with `--against <baseline> --gate`, which judges only NEW defect classes, never-cured, stuck, rebuilds, the owner law and the beat law. Under --zero all 10 world-passes of the last five runs FAIL. That includes the one CI «success», 46072102662: 112 records, 21 opening-disagree, door answers, 2 log lines.  
*Evidence:* autopilot.yml:219 and :225 pass `--gate` with no `--zero`. `git grep -n -- "--zero" -- .github scripts package.json tests` finds hits only in scripts/monkey-report.mjs:25,949,1054,1057. Run 46072102662 (1d740f2ea5, success): bottle has 110 info records, 1 door answer, opening-disagree 17; browser has 1 planned + 1 info, 2 log lines, band-fallback 1. gateVerdict(zero) fails both worlds. Run 46144276012: bottle CI gate pass, zero FAIL (119 info, opening-disagree 14).  
*Re-check:* cd tm-fz-p; git grep -n -- --zero -- .github; then node scripts/monkey-report.mjs <copy of browser-results> --against tests/e2e/autopilotBaseline.json --out /tmp/x.json --gate --zero and read the line «GATE: FAIL — ZERO: …».  
*Against it (the verifier):* The workflow header (autopilot.yml:16) states the gate is a ratchet, so no workflow claims that green means zero. Every record is printed in the report text. --zero is unit-tested. I did not check whether PLAN or the journal cites CI «success» as proof of the zero bar. Running the report over the mixed download directory merges the two worlds, so the shards must be split first.

**S6c/browser-rebuilds-blind** — `rebuilds` is never written by the browser driver: the headline says «rebuilds 0» while two page rebuilds happened  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* monkey-report sums `f.autopilot.rebuilds` (line 730). Only the bottle writes that field (autopilot.bottle.ts:237). tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts has no rebuild count. So in the browser world «rebuilds» is 0 by construction and the gate row cannot fire. Run 46144276012 browser had 2 session-rebuild/page/choice records and 2 episodes «rebuilt» (games 621 and 630), but printed «rebuilds 0».  
*Evidence:* `grep -n rebuil tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts` finds nothing. The browser report line reads «THE WHOLE GAMES — 48 game(s): 47 to the final scoring · 1 capped · 0 STUCK · rebuilds 0», while the class table shows «0 test(s) 2 rec session-rebuild/page/choice» and the totals show «episodes 2 (rebuilt 2) · NEVER CURED 2». The 621 and 630 cause files have autopilot.rebuilds undefined.  
*Re-check:* grep -n 'rebuil' tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts tests/client/bottle/autopilot.bottle.ts; then grep 'THE WHOLE GAMES' and 'session-rebuild' in the browser report of 46144276012.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The gate is not blind overall. A rebuild episode counts as NOT_CURED (monkey-report:68), so gateVerdict fails it through 'notCured'. Under --zero, any session-rebuild record class fails as well. This run's gate did FAIL. So only the headline number and the dedicated row are wrong. A rebuild with no episode or record would slip through, but I did not show that one can happen.

**S6c/bottle-blind-list-hides-browser-defects** — Two of the bottle's LAYOUT_BLIND rows are classes the real browser files as DEFECT in the same pass (#199, #161)  
`[harness-masks-product · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* LAYOUT_BLIND rows start:reactor-face (consoleBottle.ts:98) and start-bonus:visible-source (:109) rest on «a real browser said not there» (:91). Real browsers now file both as defects: #199 (46144276012, game 599) and #161 (45911869971 and 46144276012). The bottle keeps them at info (reactor-face 24 rec over 5 runs) and flags no contradiction. The browser still files both, and the issues are open.  
*Evidence:* tests/client/bottle/consoleBottle.ts:98 has `{cls: /^wait-graph\/ceiling\/start:reactor-face$/` and :109 has `start-bonus:visible-source`. The browser report of 46144276012 shows «1 test(s) 1 rec wait-graph/ceiling/start:reactor-face first: … game 599» and «… start-bonus:visible-source first: … game 602» under DEFECT classes. In the bottle report, «4 test(s) 4 rec wait-graph/ceiling/start:reactor-face» sits under INFO.  
*Re-check:* grep -n 'reactor-face\¦visible-source' tests/client/bottle/consoleBottle.ts; then grep the same two classes in the bottle and browser reports of 46144276012 (scratch/S6c-zero-bar-data/*.txt).  
*Against it (the verifier):* The product defect is not hidden: the browser pass files both classes, and #199 and #161 are open and assigned. The browser's reactor-face reads «scene unmounted, shelf none»; the bottle's reads «scene ceremony, shelf empty». These may be different causes, so the bottle's info records may really be layout-blind. #161 was contradicted earlier, in 45911869971, not first in this pass.

**S6c/journal-count-by-status-not-report** — The journal reads the 585–632 pass by test status («two classes»); the report printed 7 NEW defect classes in 4 games, plus game 632  
`[claim-not-met · medium (medium) · partly]` stage 6

The pass's report (GATE: FAIL) also names 7 defect classes that are new against the previous run. Four of them are facets of the 621/630 choice-strand episodes, so the journal does cover those. *Evidence:* Artefact autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt ends «GATE: FAIL … 2 episode(s) were never cured; 7 NEW defect class(es): console-foreground-watchdog/…, console-leak-detector/…, liveness/stall/…, scene-transition/hold-bound/board-fit, session-rebuild/page/choice, wait-graph/ceiling/start-bonus:visible-source, wait-graph/ceiling/start:reactor-face». ci-findings-browser.json holds 8 findings.

**S6c/bottle-baseline-tolerates-defects** — The bottle gate tolerates 8 defect classes from its baseline; «gone» also lists classes that are still present  
`[check-weakened · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* compareReports (monkey-report.mjs:607-616) fails only on NEW defect classes. 8 defect classes, including notifications/starvation and presentation-ledger story-due, can recur in the bottle and pass. compareReports also compares the defect tier only, so a baseline class that comes back at info prints «gone». In 46144276012, «gone» lists summary-tiles-unmeasured (30 records) and board-card-bonus:hover (9 records), both present.  
*Evidence:* node -e over tests/client/bottle/autopilotBaseline.json lists 8 classes, all tier defect. The bottle re-run reports «against the earlier run: 0 NEW · 8 gone · 0 kept» with «gone invariant/broken/console-start:summary-tiles-unmeasured» while «30 test(s) 30 rec invariant/broken/console-start:summary-tiles-unmeasured» appears under INFO.  
*Re-check:* grep -A9 'against the earlier run' scratch/S6c-zero-bar-data/bottle.txt and compare it with the INFO classes block above it.  
*Against it (the verifier):* monkey-report.mjs:949-972 has an opt-in `--zero` bar that fails on any record of any tier, and it would catch these classes; the auditor's re-run used it and got GATE FAIL. But CI does not pass it. Each baseline row carries a written «why» (jsdom has no layout), and monkeyReport.spec.ts:189-194 pins the baseline's shape, so the tolerance is deliberate. The weakness is the missing expiry and the misleading «gone» label.
