### S7-stage7-nets — the stages’ exit numbers

*Scope:* Stage 7 on pin 74910b95e2, read-only: enumerated every acting net from source (the arbiter's ladder by name, last line, pulse, watchdog, wait-graph ceilings and doors, hold ceiling, overrule, server TableLiveness) with shape and tier from shapes.ts; counted net fires in 5 pre-downloaded Autopilot reports (bottle + browser), the nightly Monkey and the e2e artefact with scratch node scripts; read release.yml / main.yml / autopilot.yml / freeze-gate.yml; pulled 72 h of runs and all ci-finding issues with read-only gh. No spec was run.

**S7/release-ships-on-every-push** — «A release cannot ship on a new class» is false: release.yml ships every push; 32 new classes were filed in 72 h beside 32 green releases  
`[claim-not-met · medium (high) · partly]` stage 7  
*Statement:* At the pin, release.yml (:6-8, :55-57) releases on every push to main and reads no gate, autopilot or monkey verdict, so the stage 7 exit «a release cannot ship on a new class» (PLAN.md:501) is NOT MET and is not wired. This is an open target, not a false claim. PLAN.md:28 says stage 7 is «not started» and PLAN.md:438-439 leaves the release wiring to the owner. Only the present-tense line PLAN.md:421 misleads. Recent rate: 7-28 new classes a day; the last 40 releases (about 28 h) were 32 success, 0 refused.  
*Evidence:* .github/workflows/release.yml:6-8 «INDEPENDENT of CI … the release does NOT wait for tests»; :55-58 `on: push: branches: [main]`; its jobs need only each other. autopilot.yml:20-21 is workflow_dispatch only; freeze-gate.yml:17 a nightly cron. gh issue list (ci-finding, all states, 180 issues): created since 2026-11-25T14:20Z — 48, of them kind:new-class 32; new classes per day 11-24…11-28: 28 · 13 · 7 · 12 · 12. gh run list --workflow "Release Electron App" --limit 40: 32 success, 8 cancelled, 0 failure.  
*Re-check:* grep -n -i -E "freeze¦autopilot¦monkey¦ci-finding¦needs:" .github/workflows/release.yml ; gh issue list --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --label ci-finding --state all --limit 200 --json number,labels,createdAt (count kind:new-class by day).  
*Against it (the verifier):* PLAN.md:28 (§0): stage 7 is «not started as a stage». PLAN.md:438-439: «Wiring it into the release workflow or CI spends runner minutes — the owner's call». PLAN.md:3362-3364 lists CI minutes for the gate as an owner decision. Nothing claims the gate is in place, so «claim-not-met / FALSE» overstates. The 32 releases cover about 28 h, not the 72 h window of the issue counts.

**S7/blind-list-grew-and-hides-two-game-classes** — The bottle's blind list went 6 → 13 rows since «shrinks only»; a real browser now names two of its rows as defects  
`[harness-masks-product · medium (high) · partly]` stage 1  
*Statement:* LAYOUT_BLIND grew from 6 rows (including at d27953d3f7, stage 1 CLOSED) to 13 at the pin, against PLAN:495 «shrinks only»; 2 rows were added 2026-11-28. No spec pins the count. Run 46144276012's browser pass names two blind rows as defects: start:reactor-face (#199, open) and start-bonus:visible-source. It is not shown that the bottle files the game's own case as info: bottle 599 reads «scene ceremony», browser 599 «scene unmounted».  
*Evidence:* Rows of LAYOUT_BLIND via git show <c>:tests/client/bottle/consoleBottle.ts — a91b3da288: 6 · ab6ad53eba (stage 1's exit): 6 · pin: 13 (lines 86-114). Run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0): browser game 599 [defect] «ceiling start:reactor-face (… scene unmounted; seat shows Poseidon …) did not come in 8000 ms»; bottle game 599 of the SAME run [info] «(the bottle is blind here) … scene ceremony; seat shows Poseidon». The class in the bottle over five passes: 8 · 6 · 3 · 3 · 4 records. git grep -l LAYOUT_BLIND -- tests → the two bottle files only.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/S7-stage7-nets/lines.mjs <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0 "start:reactor-face¦start-bonus:visible-source" 9  
*Against it (the verifier):* The bottle's 599 record differs in state from the browser's (seat/shelf empty, scene ceremony vs unmounted), so it is plausibly a real 0×0 blind wait. By design a blind row hands its class to the browser, and the browser caught both classes and #199 was filed, so they are not hidden from the program. landRules.mjs:21 forbids the CI lane from editing consoleBottle.ts, a partial guard. Each added row cites a browser check made when it was added.  
*Re-measure:* With the two rows removed: workflow «Freeze gate» (seeds fz0 fz1 fz3 + the day's) and workflow «Autopilot» where=bottle over games 585 – 632 — how many bottle records of the two classes are the game's (scene unmounted / ends unseen) and how many a 0×0 rect.

**S7/acts-without-a-class** — «The fires of EVERY net» omits acts with no cause class: ~70 NOTE degradation families and the server's TableLiveness  
`[blind-spot · medium (medium) · partly]` stage visual

NOTE lines (~70 degradation/fallback families, e.g. [start-emerge] degraded to teleport) get no class, tier or per-1000 rate; they show only in a browser autopilot's THE LOG, where --zero fails on them (monkey-report.mjs:964). The bottle files no log, so it never sees them. TableLiveness goes to Sentry only and no test harness reads it.  
*Evidence:* src/common/diagnostics/classify.ts:78-99 (the NOTE list) + cause.ts:57-61 (`causeOf` returns undefined unless the kind is stall / integrity). Run 46144276012, browser LOG: «[start-emerge] degraded to teleport {source: Point Luna …}» (game 594) and «[console play] no computable preview for Colonial Envoys — showing fallback result» ×2 — neither is in «the fires of every net». src/server/liveness/TableLiveness.ts:72-103; git grep -n -i -E "dead table¦TableLiveness" -- tests/e2e scripts .github tests/client/bottle is empty. autopilot.bottle.ts:263-267 files no `log`.

*Against it (the verifier):* NOTE lines are not invisible. Under --zero, monkey-report.mjs:964-965 fails the run with «the log is not clean», so in a browser run they already block the zero bar. What they lack is a rate and a class. TableLiveness also reports to Sentry through reportServerError and is unit-tested (tests/liveness). It is only the e2e and bottle harnesses that never read it. I did not exhaustively check for a generic server-stdout harvest in CI.

### M1-monkey-report — the instruments

*Scope:* Read all 1 101 lines of scripts/monkey-report.mjs, freeze-gate.mjs, the three workflows, both cause-file writers, the fixture that reads the page-side measures, the four baselines, LAYOUT_BLIND and findings.mjs's autopilot branch. Re-summed the real shards of Autopilot run 46144276012 with the pin's script (reproduces CI: 48/48 in both worlds). Ran the gate on 16 synthetic directories built from copies of real cause files. Downloaded the green run 46072102662's browser shards. Ran tests/scripts/monkeyReport.spec.ts (10 passing). Pin left clean.

**M1/door-and-cap-never-gate** — A screen the pilot could not answer is served by the door, printed, and turns no gate red: browser game 632 stood on one bar for four generations  
`[blind-spot · high (critical) · partly]` stage 6

Browser game 632 of run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0) stood on «ПарламентAВыполняется…» from gen 4 to 8: 24 door rows, status passed, 0 records, capped. The run itself was red for other reasons, so it is no confirming green pass. Root cause of 632 unknown.

**M1/pass-not-whole-passes** — The report has no denominator: no file, a cut file, zero files and a bottle game that threw all read GATE: pass  
`[harness-masks-product · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* monkey-report.mjs has no denominator. An empty dir, a cause file that will not parse (silently dropped) and a bottle game with status 'harness' all print GATE: pass, exit 0. In Autopilot the bottle world inherits this: its step ends in `¦¦ true` and the test asserts nothing. The browser world is narrower: a failed or thrown game fails the browser job, so the run goes red; only a cut or lost file escapes. Did not bite: 48/48/48 in the reports read.  
*Evidence:* monkey-report.mjs:1060-1066 (`catch { return undefined; }`), :90 `tests: last.size`, `status` only in the JSDoc :76. autopilot.yml:108 `… ¦ grep -E '· game ¦passing¦failing¦HARNESS' ¦¦ true`. autopilot.bottle.ts:253-255 (`status = 'harness'`, `filed('bottle/harness-error', 'info', …)`), :263. Synthetic: S2 empty dir → «0 test(s) with a file · GATE: pass», exit 0; S3 never-cured file cut at 4 000 bytes → exit 0 (control S3c: exit 3); S4 harness game → «2 test(s) … THE WHOLE GAMES — 1 game(s) … GATE: pass», exit 0. freeze-gate.mjs:139-145 knows a dead shard by two log strings, never by exit code or walk count.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/M1-monkey-report/build-syn.mjs, then the loop of report § 12 over S2-empty, S3-truncated, S4-harness — each «GATE: pass», exit 0. grep -n "¦¦ true" .github/workflows/autopilot.yml  
*Against it (the verifier):* In the browser job the Playwright step has no `¦¦ true` and `--retries=0`. A thrown game, a dead page or a killed shard fails the job, so the workflow concludes red, and Findings passes --gate-red on needs.browser.result. That means «the pass reads green» is only true of the GATE line in that world, not of the run. freeze-gate.mjs also has an `unwalked` check (:162-175) besides the log-string one. I re-opened only four of the five passes.

**M1/owner-law-on-survivors** — The one-owner law is gated on 44–47 of 48 browser games: a session reset wipes the measure, the shell's teardown deletes its reader  
`[metric-gap · medium (high) · partly]` stage 2  
*Statement:* The owner-law gate judges only games whose page returned a measure: 44-47 of 48 browser games per run (bottle 48/48). Games without `owner` are skipped (monkey-report.mjs:203-206) and no check compares owner walks with tests. A session reset only zeroes the counts (one 18-look walk). The absent cases need the reader deleted (wiringController.ts:1448), a 5 s timeout or a closed page; the 4 absences in 46072102662 are not attributed.  
*Evidence:* servingOwnerMeasure.ts:78 `sessionState('serving-owner-measure', resetServingOwnerLog, 960)`; shell/wiringController.ts:1448 `delete … __conServingOwner`; consoleTest.ts:578-610 (one read at the end, 5 s); monkey-report.mjs:203. «THE ONE FUNCTION … N walk(s)» of 48 in the five browser reports: 44 (46072102662, success — games 550, 561, 580, 581: passed, finished, coverage present, owner absent), 46, 47, 45, 47 (46144276012: game 621 absent, 630 = 18 looks; others 90…1 807). S7miss → «GATE: FAIL — the one-owner law — MISS ×3»; the same file without `owner` (S7lost) → «GATE: pass».  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/M1-monkey-report/survey.mjs <audit>/dl/M1-monkey-report/46072102662 → four rows "owner":"NO"; grep "THE ONE FUNCTION" <audit>/dl/autopilot-report-*/autopilot-browser.txt  
*Against it (the verifier):* A session reset leaves `__conServingOwner` in place, so it gives a walk with fewer looks, not an absent `owner`. The finding names two causes but does not show which one produced the 4 absences in the green run. monkey-report.mjs:679 already says "where the walks carried their measure". Coverage is 92-98 %, and no hidden MISS has been shown. I did not re-run the S7miss/S7lost synthetic test.

**M1/one-title-one-game** — The `file::title` merge is still there: CI's gates do not suffer on distinct titles, a repeated or unioned pass keeps one run a title  
`[metric-wrong · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage metrics

But Monkey's `repeat` input is summed as run 0 alone, any union of passes under one root keeps one game a title by path order, and owner / coverage are summed over ALL files while classes are summed over the survivors.

Shards of the run: 6 cause files × 8 × 2 worlds.

*Against it (the verifier):* Monkey repeat defaults to 1 and autopilot titles are distinct, so no scheduled gate loses a file today. The loss needs a manual repeat>1 dispatch or a hand-made union of passes. I did not check whether such a dispatch ever ran, and I did not verify the quoted previous-session gotcha.

**M2/off-protocol-verdict-configure-info** — The Parliament frame moved OFF its protocol on CI twice on 2026-11-28 (verdict → configure): tier «info», no issue, not in the journal  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 4  
*Statement:* Two CI runs of 2026-11-28 made `frame:parliament verdict → configure`: Autopilot 46144276012 real-browser game 628 (passed, finished) and nightly Monkey 46140902727 (success). ### M3-cards-axis — the instruments

*Scope:* Read autopilot-cards.mts, autopilotDeal.ts, autopilotScenarios.spec (ran: 23 passing), seatPlayed/loggedPlays, the door fallbacks, PreludesExpansion.fizzle. Recounted the denominator from ALL_MODULE_MANIFESTS. Re-ran autopilot-cards.mts over CI run 46144276012 per world, over the 342 per-pass reports in tm-fz-tools/dl, and over union-all.json. Split 752 per-game files by world, fixture signature and door kind. Counted in-scope cards with an action or trigger. Checked workflows for a gate.

**M3/axis-not-gated-one-time-union** — 672/672 is a one-time union over ~2 weeks of passes on many commits. No workflow gates it; one CI pass reaches ~50 %  
`[claim-unmeasured · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The 672/672 cards axis is a cumulative union over CI Autopilot passes from 2026-11-23 to 11-28 (47 runs, many commits), possibly plus box or local passes. The PLAN discloses this as "summed over each pass's own report". One 48+48-game CI pass on 983c3b60d0 played 416/672 (61.9 %): bottle 334, browser 313. No workflow or spec runs autopilot-cards.mts. Autopilot is run by hand only. With the worklist [], `missing` deals the same as `rotate`. The hand-over table lists 672/672 as claimed without noting that it is ungated.  
*Evidence:* tsx scripts/autopilot-cards.mts dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-bottle.json → «played through the console 334 (49.7 %)»; …/autopilot-browser.json → «313 (46.6 %)»; both → «416 (61.9 %)». .github/workflows/autopilot.yml:19 «BY HAND for now (no schedule)». grep autopilot-cards .github → only the deal input's description (autopilot.yml:40). scratch dealcover.mts: rotate deals 165 distinct of 672 over games 537–584.  
*Re-check:* cd tm-fz-p; node node_modules/tsx/dist/cli.mjs scripts/autopilot-cards.mts <dl>/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.json ¦ head -1; grep -rn autopilot-cards .github  
*Against it (the verifier):* The PLAN does not hide the union: PLAN:3257 says the axis is summed over each pass's own report, and autopilotDeal.ts:72 says the list covers every pass. The CI span is about 5 days, not "~2 weeks". A pass on the pin 74910b95e2 was running, so a per-pin number is pending. I did not check whether CI passes alone reach 672 or whether box or local passes are needed.  
*Re-measure:* Autopilot workflow on 74910b95e2, where=both, deal=rotate, from=537 games=48, then twice more with from=633 and from=681. Run scripts/autopilot-cards.mts over each pass's own report and record the per-pin number next to the 672.

`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The tableau term credits the 42 cards the 14 scenario fixtures put in play at boot, in every scenario game. Tharsis Alliance appears in 6 games, all with the scenario-opening fixture signature, and in no other game on disk. Dutch Mountains, Habitat Science, Venus Governor, Gene Repair and Thermophiles have one game each outside their fixture.

`[metric-wrong · low (low) · confirmed]` stage 6

But Player.playCard logs «${0} played ${1}» before card.play runs. Eccentric Sponsor, Double Down and Merger fizzle inside bespokePlay, so the log union credits them although their effect never happened.

The impact is unmeasured: I did not check whether any report credits one of these three cards only through a fizzled play, so the 100 % claim may be unaffected. Arguably the console did carry the player through the card's play and its fizzle, but its effect UI never ran.

**M3/whose-plays-and-denominator-hold** — Confirmed: only the viewer's plays count (rivals/MarsBot filtered by colour); denominator 672 = 535+80+57 is correct  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Both pilots count one seat only (table.seats[0] / the viewer), and log lines are filtered by that seat's colour, so MarsBot's «${0} played ${1}» (AutomaController.ts:203) and rival API plays are excluded. Denominator: projects 535 + preludes 80 + corporations 57 of the scope's 11 modules, minus 8 Redux replacements and Delta Project. Not covered and not stated: 12 standard projects/actions, 36 global events, resolutions, MarsBot cards, tenders.  
*Evidence:* bottleServerHost.ts:233 `who.value === player.color`; console-autopilot.spec.ts:426 `who.value === color`. scratch/denominator.mts: «axis denominator … 672», «SCOPE_CARDS (deal): 672 p 535 pre 80 corp 57», «instantiate:false in scope: deltaProject.preludeCards:Delta Project», base standardProjects 7 / standardActions 2, venus 2, colonies 1, turmoil globalEvents 36.

*Not examined by this auditor:* Choice/OR/variant cards were not counted against a denominator; only action and trigger hooks were, and the behaviour-DSL triggers are undercounted. · Whether the 54 initialCards door answers changed WHICH corporation was credited was not checked; that needs per-game walks plus the deal. · Which mid-game door `card` answers played a counted card was not checked; the doors record the question, not the outcome. · The rest of the e2e suite (deliberate card plays in ~571 specs) was not merged into the axis. · The six cards scenarios' bottle results «13/13 … 11/11» (rep-sc*.json) were not re-read game by game. · src/genfiles/cardInfoAudit.json was not used. · No CI download was made; only artefacts already on disk were used.

### M4-axes-denominators — the instruments

*Scope:* Read the axis code on the pin (monkey-report.mjs inputTypesReach/aggregateAutopilot/aggregateCoverage, kernel-walked.mjs, autopilot-cards.mts, both pilots, engineFuzz.fuzzConfig, autopilotDeal, PlayerInputModel/PlayerInputType, consoleWorkspaceFlow/Frame) and re-measured every axis from pre-downloaded CI artefacts (5 Autopilot passes; per-game files and walks of run 46144276012 on 983c3b60d0) with kernel-walked/autopilot-cards (print-only) and scratch scripts. No browser, box, build or suite. Instruments are identical between 983c3b60d0 and the pin (empty git diff).

**M5/e2e-suite-nets-filed-not-failed** — Whole-e2e green ignores watchdog/leak-detector/wait-graph/page-error lines: they are filed annotations, no gate reads them  
`[blind-spot · medium (medium) · partly]` stage 2  
*Statement:* In the e2e suite only these guards throw: transfer-end, latch, vue-error, pre-select, post-commit, covered prompt, start stranded, beat stall, liveness stall/session rebuild, raw template and foreign faces. [page-error], [wait-graph], still-hold, start-shadow and the STALL_MARKERS nets are only annotations or filed per test. playwright-e2e.yml uploads the cause-classes and no step gates on them, so a green run alone does not prove no net fired. The PLAN does read nets from the artefacts, but by hand.  
*Evidence:* tests/e2e/consoleTest.ts:462-465 STALL_MARKERS (annotations). :1075-1107 the waitGraph/pageError/stillHold/startShadow harvests use fileShadowFindings and never throw. The throw lines are 754,778,813,943,967,990,1018,1041,1067,1173. .github/workflows/playwright-e2e.yml:209-215 is «Upload the cause classes» with no report or gate step.  
*Re-check:* grep -n 'throw new Error(`' tests/e2e/consoleTest.ts; grep -n 'cause' .github/workflows/playwright-e2e.yml  
*Against it (the verifier):* The sub-claim that the PLAN treats a green suite as clean is overstated. PLAN.md:875 reads the e2e-cause-classes artefacts. :1155 points to a manual reader, tm-fz-tools/read_suite.mjs. At :1182 the reading of run 45093794154 counts «not one [start-stranded], not one [start-shadow] cure» next to «1 497 passed». So the nets are read by hand for each claim. What is missing is an automatic gate.  
*Re-measure:* Playwright E2E workflow, then `node scripts/monkey-report.mjs <merged cause-classes> --zero` over all shards' e2e-cause-classes artefacts, to count net firings on a green suite.

The game gets status 'harness' and a 'bottle/harness-error' INFO record, with no autopilot field (autopilot.bottle.ts:128,253-256,263). aggregateAutopilot skips it (monkey-report.mjs:694), compareReports counts defect classes only, the gate runs without --zero, and the play step ends in `¦¦ true` (autopilot.yml:107). The game disappears from N with nothing going red. Monkey walks are covered: freeze-gate.mjs:161-173 goes RED on status 'harness'.

The auditor's escape 'unless the play job goes red' does not happen: `¦¦ true` and a test that never throws mean it stays green. That makes the autopilot side slightly worse, not better. No CI artefacts checked for an actual case.

### M6-tiers — the instruments

*Scope:* Tier system on pin 74910b95e2: dumped all 111 shape rows (tsx over shapes.ts), scanned 239 tagged print sites in src/client + electron, read every dynamic-downgrade site (frameWitness, ceilingWhy, storyCoveredByPlayer, notifications, LAYOUT_BLIND, baselines, autopilot weather), checked the pin's production build/ for compiled-out lines, ran the shapes guard (4 passing) and the pure starvedReason, aggregated the 5 downloaded Autopilot runs, the nightly Monkey and the E2E cause classes. No browser, no box, no CI writes.

**M6/note-kind-untiered-visual-layer** — The «story not told» lines live in 78 NOTE families with no tier: never harvested, never classed, never sent  
`[metric-gap · medium (critical) · partly]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* Of 127 diagnostic families, 39 (stall+integrity) carry a cause and tier. The 78 NOTE families get no cause class or tier (cause.ts:59), so the cause harvest skips them and they never become an event. About 30 of the 96 note sites state a visual degradation ("not shown as promised" x9, "settled without a flight" x5). They are still harvested as raw lines in the autopilot's game LOG (`--zero` fails on them) and they ship in player reports, but nothing classes or rates them.  
*Evidence:* src/common/diagnostics/cause.ts:59 returns undefined unless the kind is stall or integrity; tests/e2e/causeHarvest.ts:91 `if (entry.cause === undefined) continue;`. scratch/M6-tiers/sites.mjs prints `print sites 239 {note:96, stall:111, integrity:22, perf:3, error:7}`; the list is note-all.txt. In CI: run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0) browser LOG `[start-emerge] degraded to teleport` (game 594); `[console play] no computable preview for Colonial Envoys` in 3 of 5 browser runs. docs/claude/diagnostics-tier-audit.md:48: the pad line of row 9 is still open.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/M6-tiers/sites.mjs and read note-all.txt beside it; or: git -C . grep -n 'not shown as promised' -- src/client  
*Against it (the verifier):* "Never harvested" is false. monkey-report's log book keeps every warning line of the autopilot games, notes included, and `--zero` exits 3 on any of them; the auditor's own CI log quote comes from that harvest. "Never sent" is half true: no Sentry event, but a player report carries the whole journal. Two counts are inflated: 11 should be 9, and 7 should be 5. Many of the 78 NOTE families are not visual at all (net, selftest, electron, velopack, the lore lookups). The NOTE kind is a documented choice ("evidence around a real one", classify.ts:77-80).

**M6/dev-only-witnesses-compiled-out** — 21 diagnostic print sites — 2 DEFECT shapes, 1 PLANNED, every «not shown as promised» verdict — are absent from the production build  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage field

Three shapes are silent in production with no other witness I found: the DEFECT cursor/backstop and reading/raw-template, and the PLANNED transfer/no-visible-end. The «not shown as promised» verdicts still set production degraded flags (data-*-degraded attributes) that e2e specs assert. Only the diagnostics line is lost for those.

Sources: console/cursorArrival.ts:96, console/readingText.ts:51, console/resourceTransfer/consoleResourceTransfer.ts:1986. tests/console/diagnosticsShapes.spec.ts passes (4 passing) — it reads sources, not the bundle.  
*Re-check:* cd build && grep -l -F 'not shown as promised' main.js chunks/*.js ¦ wc -l (0); grep -o -h '.{60}never signalled ready in' main.js chunks/*.js (the shapes table only).

So «real-browser CI can never say them» is false for the verdicts; CI does observe the degrade, but only through per-spec asserts. I did not check whether every one of the 21 sites has such a twin.

**M6/frame-witness-cannot-tell-jank-from-hidden** — The frame witness downgrades the product's own main-thread stall to planned and suspends the judges: it reads frame gaps only  
`[check-weakened · medium (high) · partly]` stage 1  
*Statement:* frameWitness.ts reads only frame gaps (:65-67, :88-95), with no visibility, focus or longtask input. A visible page blocked by its own script (one 700 ms task in a 1.8 s flight) reads as starved: its ceiling ends tier planned, the watchdog judges nothing (:427-431) and the wait graph leaves starved spans out of bounds. The stall itself is still logged as untiered perf `[long-frame]`. One task suspends the judges for about 1-2 looks; only jank that recurs every second suspends them for longer.

Pure run on the pin (scratch/M6-tiers/starved.mts): starvedReason(1133,1800,1) → 'the page gave the motion 1133 of 1800 ms, 1 frame gap over 500 ms — …'; (532,1000,1) → starved; (4000,4000,0) → undefined; (25033,35000,1) → undefined. Consumers: consoleForegroundWatchdog.ts:427-431 «nothing on it is judged»; waitGraph/waitGraph.ts:265; waitGraphModel.ts:251,254; ceilingWait.ts:113; animationHold.ts:442. `long-frame` is kind perf (classify.ts:75): no cause, no tier.

*Against it (the verifier):* longFrameWatch.ts (Long Animation Frames, >=500 ms) names the stall as `[long-frame] … SCRIPT` and the autopilot counts these lines, so the jank is not hidden, only untiered. pageStarvedNow holds one 1 s look plus the previous one, so an isolated task suspends the judges briefly. PLAN:236 shows the excused case (a page behind another) itself shows up as ~1000 ms long frames, which supports the finding's point about the input.  
*Re-measure:* On the box: one spec, page visible and in front, a 700 ms busy loop injected (page.evaluate) while a rail transfer flies; read the tier of wait-graph/ceiling/transfer:touchdown in its cause-classes file (expected today: planned).

*Scope:* Read on pin 74910b95e2: autopilot, monkey, freeze-gate, playwright-e2e, engine-fuzz, reactive-census, ci-findings, land-ci-fix, main, release yml; scripts/ci/{findings,land,landRules}.mjs; monkey-report.mjs gateVerdict/aggregateAutopilot; autopilot.bottle.ts and console-autopilot.spec.ts result writers. **M7/release-ungated** — Every push to main is published to players (auto-update) with no gate: CI, freeze gate, monkey and autopilot are not waited for  
`[process-risk · high (critical) · partly]` stage phase1  
*Statement:* On the pin, release.yml publishes a Velopack release to the self-update feed on every push to main. It has no workflow_run trigger and no `needs` on any gate. Releases shipped for 7752ea0189 even though its CI was cancelled, and for 9db45debcb and c397238e88 even though their CI failed. The freeze gate, monkey, e2e and autopilot only run on a schedule or on dispatch, so none of them is a precondition for publishing. CI and the nightly runs do still measure the head, but only in parallel with publication or after it.  
*Evidence:* .github\workflows\release.yml:6-8 «This workflow is INDEPENDENT of CI … the release does NOT wait for tests». gh run list: CI 46132206523 on 7752ea0189 = cancelled; Release Electron App 46132206544 on 7752ea0189 = success. release.yml jobs build→windows/linux→prune have no `needs` on any gate and no workflow_run trigger.  
*Re-check:* sed -n 1,10p .github/workflows/release.yml; gh run list --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --workflow 'Release Electron App' --limit 10 --json headSha,conclusion and compare with --workflow CI for headSha 7752ea0189.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The ungated release is a documented, deliberate choice ("as fast as possible"), not an oversight. The claim that Sentry is the FIRST place a regression is measured goes too far: per-push CI runs in parallel and often ends before a player updates, and the nightly gates measure the head later. I did not examine whether updates are forced on players, how many players there are, or any staged rollout.

**M7/bottle-crash-green** — Bottle autopilot: a crashed shard or a game whose harness threw is GATE: pass. The exit code is swallowed, the error is filed as info, and no game count is checked  
`[blind-spot · high (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The claim holds for the freeze gate (freeze-gate.mjs:137-149). It does not hold for the Autopilot bottle. The play step ends in `¦¦ true`. A game that throws is written as `bottle/harness-error` at tier info with no `autopilot` block, so it is not counted. gateVerdict never compares the games played with the games asked for. A shard that dies part-way through (OOM) is green, and so is a directory with no games.  
*Evidence:* autopilot.yml:108 `…¦ tee autopilot.log ¦ grep -E '· game ¦passing¦failing¦HARNESS' ¦¦ true`; autopilot.bottle.ts catch → `filed('bottle/harness-error', 'info', …)`, `autopilot: played === undefined ? undefined`; monkey-report.mjs:947-1000 has no count check. Scratch: one harness-error file → `GATE: pass` exit=0; empty dir → `MONKEY REPORT — 0 test(s) with a file … GATE: pass` exit=0.  
*Re-check:* Create <dir>/cause-classes/x.json = {file,title,retry:0,status:'harness',records:[{cls:'bottle/harness-error',tier:'info',count:1}]}; run `node scripts/monkey-report.mjs <dir> --against tests/client/bottle/autopilotBaseline.json --gate; echo $?` → GATE: pass, 0.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The harness error still shows in the report text as an info class, so a reader can see it. A broken-run net exists (--gate-red when needs.play.result != success), but `¦¦ true` defeats it. I did not check how download-artifact@v5 handles a pattern with zero matches, so the empty-dir case is proven for the script only.

**M7/autopilot-by-hand** — No whole-game gate runs unattended: the Autopilot is dispatched by hand only. 5 of its last 7 runs failed, and on the pin only CI and Release have finished  
`[process-risk · high (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Whole games are gated only when a session dispatches the Autopilot. Since 2026-11-27 20:07, one run succeeded (46072102662 on 1d740f2ea5). Four failed, one was cancelled, and one (on the pin) was in progress at 14:44 UTC. Nightly coverage is the freeze gate (fixtures), the monkey (60-press walks), e2e and the fuzz. On the pin, CI and Release finished green, and the Autopilot and Freeze gate runs were still in progress.  
*Evidence:* gh run list --workflow Autopilot: 46154080374 74910b95e2 in_progress · 46144276012 983c3b60d0 failure · 46132235664 7752ea0189 failure · 46098613041 9db45debcb failure · 46096030106 cancelled · 46079806794 failure · 46072102662 1d740f2ea5 success · 46061450089 failure. autopilot.yml `on:` has only workflow_dispatch. Schedules found: engine-fuzz 22:43, freeze-gate 23:17, e2e 00:47, census 01:41, monkey 03:13. The actual starts were 5–9 h late.  
*Re-check:* grep -n -A3 '^on:' .github/workflows/*.yml; gh run list --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --workflow Autopilot --limit 8 --json headSha,conclusion,createdAt  
*Against it (the verifier):* The workflow file gives a reason for dispatching by hand («a pass is hours of runners»), so this is a deliberate interim choice, not an oversight. I did not check whether the failures are product findings or infrastructure; reds the gate files correctly are not themselves a defect. The pin's Autopilot and Freeze gate results were still pending.  
*Re-measure:* Read the conclusion of Autopilot 46154080374 and Freeze gate 46154082798 (both dispatched on 74910b95e2 at 14:19 UTC) when they finish.

**M7/regression-label-commit-blind** — The findings queue reopens a closed class as «regression» from a run on any commit. The 2026-11-28 six are real, but a false one happened on 11-27  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · partly]` stage field  
*Statement:* The six reopens of 2026-11-28 (14:12:50–14:13:11, #119 #170 #171 #172 #161 #177) came from run 46144276012 on 983c3b60d0. That commit contains every cited fix, so they are real. findings.mjs:711-723 reopens any CLOSED issue as «regression» without comparing the run sha to a fix. This falsely reopened #119 on 11-27 21:42 from run 46061450089 on 16f6bbef91, which predates 45f9bf2703. Classes close after one absent pass. The per-48 rate figure was not verified.  
*Evidence:* gh issue view 119/161/170/171/172/177 --comments: github-actions 2026-11-28T14:12:49–14:13:11 «Seen again … run 46144276012 (browser · ap0 · games 585+48)». `git merge-base --is-ancestor X 983c3b60d0` is true for 1d740f2ea5 9b18aa2259 45f9bf2703 6d2068b2bb 00d2ef2a4e. findings.mjs:713-723 reopens on `issue.state === 'CLOSED'` with no sha check. #119 comment 2026-11-27T21:43 «run 46061450089 … ran on 16f6bbef91 — BEFORE the fix».  
*Re-check:* gh issue view 119 --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --comments; git -C . merge-base --is-ancestor 45f9bf2703 16f6bbef91; echo $? → 1 (not an ancestor).  
*Against it (the verifier):* The false reopen was caught by hand within about an hour, so the cost was triage noise and a withdrawn label, not a lost fix. The «1–2 per 48» rate was not re-measured. #119 shows sightings in 4 runs and one absent pass before it was closed. That supports «closed after one pass» but not the exact rate.

**M7/autopilot-gate-not-zero** — The CI autopilot gate is not the program's zero bar: door answers, capped games, log lines and the owner MISS all pass, and `--zero` is used by no workflow  
`[check-weakened · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* `--zero` (monkey-report.mjs gateVerdict) is used by no workflow; autopilot.yml:219/225 use `--gate` only. A green run is not zero: 46072102662 passed with 110 records/7 classes, byApi 1, log 2; caps and log lines never fail --gate. But law-level owner verdicts (MISS, APART, …) ARE refused by --gate via OWNER_LAW; only per-row owner fallback/disagree is zero-only. 46072102662 is the latest green, not the only one.  
*Evidence:* monkey-report.mjs:949-972 (`opts.zero` → byApi, capped, log, owner MISS); `grep -rn -- --zero .github/workflows` → no hit; autopilot.yml report steps call `--gate` only. From the artefacts: 46072102662 bottle games 48 byApi 1, browser log 2, both GATE: pass; 46144276012 browser capped 1 («game 632 … generation 8, drafting, 3200 presses»), byApi 25, log 18.  
*Re-check:* node -e "const r=require('<audit>/dl/autopilot-report-46072102662/autopilot-bottle.json');console.log(r.autopilot.byApi)" → 1; grep GATE: on its .txt files → pass.  
*Against it (the verifier):* OWNER_LAW (monkey-report.mjs:623-632) holds MISS/pad-MISS/band-APART/bar-APART/flag-MISS/flag-EXTRA/edge-LATCH, and ownerLawBroken refuses them under plain --gate, so the title's "owner MISS passes" is wrong at the law level. There are three earlier green autopilot runs in the window. Most runs are red under --gate anyway, which limits how often the gap misleads.

**M7/browser-rebuilds-unwritten** — The gate's «rebuild(s) in whole games» bar is structurally 0 in the real browser: the browser writer never sets `rebuilds`  
`[metric-wrong · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Only the bottle writes `autopilot.rebuilds`. The browser's result object has no such key, so `report.autopilot.rebuilds` is 0 for every browser pass. In 46144276012 the browser had 2 episodes with outcome `rebuilt`, and session-rebuild/page/choice was counted with 2 records in 0 tests (its attribution was lost). The run went red only through notCured and new-class.  
*Evidence:* tests/client/bottle/autopilot.bottle.ts:265 `… tookMs: played.tookMs, rebuilds, …`; tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts:1954-1959 `autopilot: {...played, table, pilot, cards, slow, slowFrames, sent, weather, log}`, which has no rebuilds. In the game-621 cause file, autopilot keys = end,presses,…,log with no rebuilds. In autopilot-browser.json of 46144276012: autopilot.rebuilds 0, totals.outcomes {rebuilt:2}, session-rebuild/page/choice tests 0.  
*Re-check:* node -e "const r=require('<audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.json');console.log(r.autopilot.rebuilds, JSON.stringify(r.totals.outcomes), r.classes['session-rebuild/page/choice'].tests)" → 0 {"rebuilt":2} 0  
*Against it (the verifier):* The rebuilds still show up. They are a defect-tier class (session-rebuild/page) and episode outcomes (`rebuilt` 2), and that run was red anyway. So the dedicated bar and its CI ticket are dead in the browser, but the event can still surface through the class, the new-class check or notCured. I did not trace why the class lost its test attribution.

**M7/land-lane-can-weaken** — landRules does not cover tests/** or most nets, so an automated ci-fix may relax a guard spec or a net threshold and land on main  
`[process-risk · medium (medium) · partly]` stage phase1  
*Statement:* The landing gate (landRules.mjs:12-29, called alone from land.mjs:143) does not refuse edits to tests/** guard specs, to most nets (foreground watchdog, leak detector, beat stall, sessionRebuild, rebuildBound, screenHandoff, reactivityWitness) or to their thresholds. ci-findings.md §3a forbids these, but only by the agent's discipline. A tests-only diff deleting guard lines also passes. #20 LANDED (96c0276754), not just closed.  
*Evidence:* scripts/ci/landRules.mjs:12-29 (the FORBIDDEN list); l.49-51 only requires a `tests/` file to be present; src/client/console/consoleForegroundWatchdog.ts:144 `ANIMATION_STALL_GRACE_MS = 20_000`; workspaceBeatStall.ts:50 `WORKSPACE_BEAT_STALL_MS = 12_000`. `gh pr list --label ci-fix --state all` → only #20 CLOSED.  
*Re-check:* node scripts/ci/landRules.mjs '[[{"path":"src/client/console/workspaceBeatStall.ts","additions":1,"deletions":1},{"path":"tests/console/x.spec.ts","additions":1,"deletions":0}]]' → [[]] (no refusal) — run from ..  
*Against it (the verifier):* ci-findings.md §3a forbids, by contract, the liveness nets, a motion's timing and a diagnostic's shape, and limits expectation fixes to a named deliberate commit. So the hole is in enforcement, not in policy. Size caps apply (product ≤3 files/80 lines). The finder, not the fixer, picks the proof. The cited line 49-51 is really 55-58. #20 landed, it was not only closed.

**M7/e2e-success-was-one-test** — «The whole Playwright E2E run (success)» 46144266613 was one test. The last whole suite failed, and the flake ledger has lost its history  
`[metric-wrong · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* 46144266613 ran 1 shard and 1 test (a grep proof for #197). The last whole suite (nightly 46126088269 on 8395cdaf75) failed 1 of 1872. The flake ledger on 2026-11-28 held only 3 runs, so its memory is lost (it is kept in the actions cache). Grep runs enter its 20-run window, and retries=2 turns a flaky test into a green run.  
*Evidence:* flake-ledger/e2e-flake-ledger.json runs: 46126088269.1 total 1872 failed 1 · 46139599053.1 total 1 failed 1 · 46144266613.1 total 1 failed 0. gh run view 46144266613 jobs: «E2E shard 1/1 success». playwright-e2e.yml:312 (the ledger update excludes repeat/retries only, not grep); l.272 «retries=2 rescues a flaky test into a green run».  
*Re-check:* node -e "for(const r of require('<audit>/dl/e2e-46144266613-983c3b60d0/flake-ledger/e2e-flake-ledger.json').runs)console.log(r.runId,r.total,r.failed.length)"  
*Against it (the verifier):* The ledger does record `flaky` per run, so a retry rescue is counted. The "whole run" label comes from the audit brief or artefact naming, not from a repo file. The history loss predates 11-28, and the exact mechanism (cache eviction vs restore race) is unproven.  
*Re-measure:* Playwright E2E, workflow_dispatch, no inputs (the whole suite) on 74910b95e2.

*Not examined by this auditor:* sentry-findings.yml / sentry-housekeeping.yml logic beyond their schedules. · from-fuzz / from-playwright behaviour when their report file is missing. · Whether actions/download-artifact with a pattern matching zero artefacts errors. If it does, an autopilot run with all shards missing would be red through exit 2. Not tested. · freeze-gate.mjs replay logic and the content of monkeyBaseline.json / autopilotBaseline.json rows (another auditor's scope). · The final conclusion of Autopilot 46154080374 and Freeze gate 46154082798 on the pin: still in progress at 14:44 UTC. · The land.mjs proof-selection path for each finding kind, beyond the PROOF_WORKFLOWS check.

### M8-browser-pilot — the instruments

*Scope:* Read tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts whole (1 973 lines), the guards of consoleTest.ts, the drivers it imports (cinematicBeat, fastBootQuery, openConsole), scripts/monkey-report.mjs's gate, autopilot.yml; blamed every pilot rule and counted src/ files per commit (45 commits). Measured the 48 browser games of run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0) from their cause files and walks with two scratch scripts (per-game table, press cadence), ran the CI gate and --zero over one shard, read job names of 9 Autopilot runs with gh. No browser, no box, no client bundle.

**M8/game-632-silent-parliament-freeze** — Browser game 632 (983c3b60d0): Parliament frozen on «A Выполняется…» for 4 generations — no net fired, test passed, no issue filed  
`[harness-masks-product · critical (critical) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* On 983c3b60d0 a browser game froze: «Nationalist Movement» completed the chairman's quest, the plate's «A Открыть Парламент» opened a Parliament whose bar read «A Выполняется…» (A grey, no B) from generation 4 to the 3200-press cap, ≈35 min. The door gave 20 answers and 4 passes. 0 cause records, 0 log lines, test passed, shard green; unfiled, unjournaled.  
*Evidence:* Run 46144276012, artefact autopilot-browser-46144276012-1-7: cause-classes/console-autopilot--autopilot-game-632-r0.json → status passed, end capped, generation 8, presses 3200, byApi {option 5, card 15}, passes.api 4, 24 doors, records [], log []. autopilot-walk.txt: «[PLAYED Nationalist Movement] … [WAY-BACK] 33052:Enter{-¦option#1834¦КартаAОткрыть Парламент…} 33617:ArrowRight{parliament:browse¦option#1834¦ПарламентAВыполняется…}» — the stood-state never changes again; console tail 11 lines. gh run view: «Browser · games 592,600,608,616,624,632 => success». PLAN.md: grep -c 'game 632' = 0; issue search «Выполняется» = none.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/M8-browser-pilot/games.mjs <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0 ¦ grep '^632'; then search «Выполняется» in …/autopilot-browser-46144276012-1-7/test-results/*game-632-chromium/autopilot-walk.txt.  
*Against it (the verifier):* CPU×3 game; whether it reproduces without the slow machine is unknown. The server kept progressing via API doors, so the freeze is in the console UI, not in the game. ≈35 min is an estimate (the whole game took 39.6 min). The console tail has 11 display/gamepad/long-frame lines, but autopilot.log is empty. Earlier chairman-quest fixes (PLAN l.226/249) cover a different path (the second A during the round trip). Run 46154080374 was not examined.  
*Re-measure:* On the pin: `gh workflow run Autopilot -f from=632 -f games=1 -f name=ap0 -f where=browser` (game 632 is a CPU ×3 game by n % 5 === 2). Then on the box with the slow machine off, to learn whether it is necessary: `TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT=1 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_GAMES=632 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_SLOW=off npm run e2e:remote -- <task> tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts`. Also read run 46154080374 (the pin, games 633–680, in progress at the time of the audit) for capped games and doors.

**M8/ci-gate-is-not-the-zero-bar** — CI's Autopilot gate is --gate: a door, a capped game, a log line, a planned/info record are not red; --zero is called by no workflow  
`[metric-gap · high (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The zero bar is a flag no workflow passes. CI's Autopilot runs `--gate`: red on never-cured, STUCK, a rebuild, a NEW defect-tier class, the owner and beat laws. A door answer, a pass by the door, a capped game, every LogBook line and every planned / info record pass. «Gate pass» in the journal is not the zero bar; the one green run of the last six had 2 records and 2 log classes.  
*Evidence:* .github/workflows/autopilot.yml:225 «monkey-report.mjs browser-results --against tests/e2e/autopilotBaseline.json … --gate»; `grep -n -e '--zero' .github/workflows/*.yml package.json` → nothing; scripts/monkey-report.mjs:950–973 the door / cap / log reasons live under `if (opts.zero === true)`. Shard 7 of run 46144276012 through both: --gate → «records 0 … 1 capped … through the door 20 (4.4 % of the answers) … GATE: pass», exit 0; --zero → «GATE: FAIL — ZERO: 20 answer(s) and 4 pass(es) went through the door (9 place(s)); ZERO: 1 game(s) ended on the pilot's cap», exit 3. Report 46072102662 (the green run): «records 2», «THE LOG — 2 class(es)», «GATE: pass».  
*Re-check:* cd .; D=<audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-browser-46144276012-1-7/test-results; node scripts/monkey-report.mjs $D --against tests/e2e/autopilotBaseline.json --gate --out <scratch>/g.json ¦ grep GATE; node scripts/monkey-report.mjs $D --zero --out <scratch>/z.json ¦ grep GATE  
*Against it (the verifier):* PLAN l.3296 does not claim a --zero exit. It says every door and planned line of that run was cured by replay, and it lists «the confirming CI pass on the head» as open. So the journal is mislabelled, not concealing anything. Run 46072102662 (1d740f2ea5) is not the run the journal cites (4d74b4f9a0).

**M8/ci-zero-measured-on-replayed-seeds** — CI's autopilot zeros were measured on the same 48 games (ap0 537–584), replayed 6 times; both passes on fresh numbers were red  
`[metric-wrong · high (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The only CI browser zero (46072102662) is on games 537–584. PLAN l.3128 scopes it to that range and l.3299 lists the confirming whole pass as Open. Of 8 runs from 46018935113 on, 6 replayed 537+48 and 1 was green. Both fresh ranges were red: 1001+48 had 3 new defect classes; 585+48 had 9 defects, 7 new classes, 1 capped game and 25 byApi answers. Neither red run is in PLAN at the pin. Stage 6's exit needs fresh-range passes.  
*Evidence:* `gh run view <id> --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --json jobs` → Report job names: 46018935113, 46045100090, 46061450089, 46072102662 (success), 46098613041, 46132235664 = «ap0 · games 537+48»; 46079806794 = «games 1001+48», failure; 46144276012 = «games 585+48», failure. autopilot-browser.json of the reports in dl/: 537+48 → doors 0 / 3 / 2 places, capped 0, log 2 / 13 / 3 classes; 585+48 → 12 door places (byApi 25), capped 1, log 18, 7 NEW defect classes; 1001+48 → 3 NEW. Seeds are functions of the number: spec :236 rng(`${NAME}:${n}:table`), :680 rng(`${seed}:pilot`).  
*Re-check:* export PATH="$PATH:/c/Program Files/GitHub CLI"; for id in 46072102662 46079806794 46132235664 46144276012; do gh run view $id --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --json jobs,conclusion --jq '.conclusion, (.jobs[]¦select(.name¦startswith("Report"))¦.name)'; done  
*Against it (the verifier):* The journal does not claim a general zero: it scopes it to games 537–584 and lists the confirming pass on the head as Open. The replays did not manufacture greens: 1 of 7 was green. The 4d74b4f9a0 run itself (46018935113) failed overall. Commits after the pin (#198, #199) target classes from the 585 run. I did not check door counts, log class counts or the spec seed lines.  
*Re-measure:* Read run 46154080374 (the pin 74910b95e2, games 633–680) when it completes. Then two more fresh ranges on the pin: `gh workflow run Autopilot -f from=681 -f games=48 -f where=browser` and `gh workflow run Autopilot -f from=2001 -f games=48 -f name=ap1 -f where=browser` (other tables, another press stream). Judge each with `--zero`, not `--gate`.

**M8/verdict-stuck-unreachable-finished-is-server-end** — The pilot's own verdicts: `stuck` is unreachable for a client freeze while the viewer is asked; `finished` means the SERVER reached `end`  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The pilot's own verdict can't classify a client freeze. `stuck` is set only when nobody asks the viewer (spec l.1053-1066). An asked question goes to the door at its budget and ends `capped`/`finished`. `finished` = server phase `end` plus ≤40/90 presses, with no finale-screen check. «0 STUCK» is not «no freeze». The test stays red on a freeze the product's own nets name (consoleTest livenessGuard/beatStallGuard). A freeze no net names (game 632) passes as `capped`.  
*Evidence:* tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts: :886 `end: 'capped'` at birth; :1053–1066 `if (wf === undefined) { … if (Date.now() - moved.at > 10 * 60_000) { played.end = 'stuck'` — the only place; :1206, :1239–1250 at `standing.presses >= standing.budget` the door answers (refused → budget 90, the walk goes on); :1674–1697 `if (played.phase === 'end') { for (… i < (rematchOwed ? 90 : 40) …) press(pilotPick(…)); played.end = 'finished'; }` — no expect on the finale; :1969–1970 the only two assertions (page not closed, end ≠ 'stuck'). Game 632 of run 46144276012: 35 min on one dead screen → `capped`, `status: passed`.  
*Re-check:* grep -n "played.end = \¦end: 'capped'\¦expect(played.end\¦expect(page.isClosed" tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts — three assignments, two expects; then sed -n '1053,1066p' of the same file.  
*Against it (the verifier):* "The only two assertions" is false. Auto fixtures in tests/e2e/consoleTest.ts run on every autopilot game: livenessGuard l.1050 throws on `[liveness] stall` or a session rebuild; beatStallGuard l.1027; plus the latch, vue-error and transfer guards. The autopilot does not opt out (no EXPECTS_LIVENESS_STALL). Doors are recorded in played.doors and counted by the report. I did not independently confirm game 632's Playwright `status: passed` or the 35-min single-screen span.

**M8/no-gamepad-no-tv-profile-in-whole-games** — No whole game uses a gamepad or the TV profile: the pilot types on a keyboard into a 1280×720 `handheld` page  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The seat is played through the keyboard fallback: the harness answers «no gamepad» on every e2e page, so the pad's poll loop, hold-repeat, sticks, dead zone, arbitration and connect / disconnect — 1 388 lines above `dispatchConsoleIntent`, the couch player's only input path — run in no whole game. Every whole game is laid out as `handheld` at 1280×720; none at 3840×2160.  
*Evidence:* tests/e2e/consoleTest.ts:489–497 «NO GAMEPAD REACHES AN E2E PAGE. The driver speaks keyboard… Object.defineProperty(navigator, 'getGamepads', {… value: () => []})»; spec :909–913 page.keyboard.down / press; walk console: «[gamepad] installed — pads=0 slots=0 []». playwright.config.ts:165 `...devices['Desktop Chrome']` and no viewport in the spec → all 76 `[console-display]` lines kept in the 48 walks of run 46144276012 read «profile=handheld scale=1 viewport=1280×720». `grep -ln 3840 tests/e2e/*.spec.ts ¦ wc -l` → 381 of 598, not the autopilot, not the monkey. `wc -l src/client/gamepad/{gamepadCore,gamepadPollModel,nativePadBridge}.ts` → 657 + 586 + 145.  
*Re-check:* grep -h -o 'profile=[a-z]* scale=[0-9.]* viewport=[0-9×]*' <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-browser-*/test-results/*/autopilot-walk.txt ¦ sort ¦ uniq -c; sed -n '488,497p' tests/e2e/consoleTest.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* The pad code is not untested. There are unit specs (gamepadPollModel.spec, nativePadBridge.spec, electron nativeGamepadLinux.spec). Virtual-pad e2e specs exist (console-gamepad-first-press, console-gamepad-reload-carryover, console-lobby-pad-refusal, console-info-effects-scroll, wheel-rework). Over 380 specs run at 3840. The gap is only in whole-game coverage. I did not check whether the tv profile changes any freeze-relevant path.  
*Re-measure:* After a pilot-only change (a 3840×2160 viewport for the spec; no product change), on the box with the GPU: `TM_E2E_GPU=1 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT=1 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_GAMES=681-704 npm run e2e:remote -- <task> tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts`, judged with `node scripts/monkey-report.mjs <results> --zero`.

**M8/pilot-timing-is-not-a-players** — Whole games run at motion ×0.5 with the e2e boot; the pilot never presses twice inside 164 ms and never goes silent for more than 9 s  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Whole games (autopilot run 46144276012, 48 walks) run at motion 0.5 with fastboot=1. The gap between presses is 164 ms min and 8.8 s max, none under 150 ms or over 30 s. No whole game mashes or goes away mid-prompt. The soak idles only at the action menu. A double press is walked only at named sites by targeted specs (composer-preselect, stdp-workspace, placement-confirm), also at motion 0.5. The monkey's minimum gap is 120 ms, so it has the same gap.  
*Evidence:* scratch/M8-browser-pilot/cadence.mjs over the 48 walks: «interval ms: min 164 p1 188 p5 231 median 595 p95 1743 max 8798 · < 150 ms: 0 · > 30 s: 0 · presses whose top frame was executing/committed/completing: 3582, of them B 1871». tests/e2e/consoleStart.ts:319 `const FUNCTIONAL_MOTION = 0.5`; :345–353 fastBootQuery() adds `&fastboot=1` and `&motion=0.5` unless a timing spec (the autopilot is not in timingSpecs.ts; autopilot.yml sets no TM_E2E_MOTION; the record's perturb.motion is ""). Spec :919–922 pause() ≥ 150 ms after a truth() fetch and a look(); :1216–1227 the pass polls state between its presses; :1118–1203 the soak idles only at the empty action menu.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/M8-browser-pilot/cadence.mjs <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0; sed -n '310,353p' tests/e2e/consoleStart.ts ¦ cut -c1-200  
*Against it (the verifier):* Targeted functional e2e specs already walk a double press and assert one submit: console-composer-preselect.spec.ts:24/238/328-345, console-stdp-workspace.spec.ts:317 and console-placement-confirm.spec.ts:114. So the by-construction claim is guarded at named sites. Only bursts inside a whole game are unwalked. The monkey's minimum gap is 120 ms, also with no bursts. Nobody has measured whether motion 1 or the real boot would change any outcome.  
*Re-measure:* Box, the same 24 fresh games twice and the records / doors / log compared: default, and `TM_E2E_MOTION=1 TM_E2E_FAST_BOOT=0 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT=1 TM_E2E_AUTOPILOT_GAMES=681-704 npm run e2e:remote -- <task> tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts`.

**M8/pilot-only-rules-remove-player-paths** — 24 of the 45 commits that taught the pilot changed no product file; one rule steers by window.__conOwnerNow(); the start is answered on screen in 21 % of games  
`[harness-masks-product · medium (medium) · partly]` stage 6

The [HOME] rule (357 tags in 48 games, run 46144276012) fires on 6 still presses (DOM) OR an owner hold read via window.__conOwnerNow() (no DOM read); the share is unsplit. The start rule never presses LT/B. Start answered on screen in 10/48 games; the 6 n%8===2 games split 3 UI / 3 API. That doors fell because of pilot avoidance is unproven.

Run 46144276012: tags HOME 357, WAY-BACK 1073; byUi.initialCards in 10 games (586 589 594 597 600 602 605 613 621 629), byApi.initialCards in 610 618 626; spec :1765 `n % 8 === 2`.

The owner facts it reads mirror visible cues (plate «A Открыть»). Pilot-only commits also remove non-player habits (A pressed 11× in the viewer), and the spec l.1584 notes the pilot's walk-away habit CREATED doors. Door-drop attribution is not shown. About 19 console-start-* specs walk the start separately; I did not check whether they press LT/B.  
*Re-measure:* Box, a pilot-only experiment: 24 fresh games with the `[HOME]` rule (spec l.1607) and the look-closing rule (l.567) switched off behind an env flag; read each door by what STOOD on screen — it is either a place where the product gives the player no cue, or a habit of the pilot.

CI runs the pilot with `--retries=0`; `[liveness] stall` and `[session-rebuild]` fail the game's own test; any DEFECT-tier class is red in the Report job because the browser baseline holds none; each door carries the question, the frames, the bar, the grey verbs and the owner; the pilot presses under beats, opens looks mid-flow, collapses committed workspaces and meets faults, a reload and a sleep in 1 game of 3.
