### M9-bottle-fidelity — the instruments

*Scope:* I read the bottle harness (bottleDom, bottleTime, consoleBottle with LAYOUT_BLIND, autopilot/monkey.bottle, classReplays, bottleServerHost), both bottle baselines, the autopilot and freeze-gate CI wiring, the gate logic in monkey-report, and the product readers of rects and animation ends. I compared the bottle and browser worlds across five pre-downloaded Autopilot reports (240 same-seed game pairs) and one game file per world for games 599 and 621. I read issues #119, #161 and #199 and ran freezeGateBaseline.spec (3 passing). I read 5 of the 44 replays.

**M9/same-seed-worlds-disjoint** — Same seeds: browser named 14 defect classes in 5 Autopilot runs, the bottle 1, zero overlap at defect tier  
`[metric-wrong · high (critical) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* In 5 Autopilot runs (240 same-seed pairs) the browser named 14 defect classes, the bottle 1 (flow:rally), none shared at defect tier. Same seed is not the same walk (presses/generations diverge, 621: 2545/g12 vs 1195/g17), and browser defects never recur run to run (same seeds: 0 vs 4), so a silent browser pass is no evidence for a LAYOUT_BLIND excuse. Disjointness alone does not prove bottle infidelity.  
*Evidence:* scratch\M9-bottle-fidelity\cmp.js over dl\autopilot-46144276012-…\autopilot-report-46144276012-1, autopilot-report-46132235664, -46098613041, -46079806794, -46072102662. Browser defects: 7 + 0 + 4 + 3 + 0 classes. Bottle: only wait-graph/overdue/flow:rally (46079806794). «browser defects also printed by bottle (any tier): only start:reactor-face». Game 621: bottle-autopilot--game-621-r0.json presses 2545, 6 info records; browser console-autopilot--autopilot-game-621-r0.json presses 1195, stranded-prompt + watchdog + liveness/stall defects.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/M9-bottle-fidelity/cmp.js <the five report dirs>. Then compare j.monkey.presses of the game-621 cause-class JSON in the bottle shard and in the browser shard of 46144276012.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The browser's own defect classes share nothing across its 5 runs either (rare 1-2 record events). Runs 46132235664 and 46098613041 have identical bottle totals (76085 presses, 96 records; deterministic), yet the browser gave 0 vs 4 defects on the same seeds. So zero overlap reflects walk divergence and rarity, not proven bottle blindness. The runs are also at different commits.

**M9/monkeybaseline-excuses-119** — Freeze gate's monkeyBaseline excuses console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n — open real-browser regression #119  
`[harness-masks-product · medium (critical) · partly]` stage 7  
*Statement:* The bottle freeze gate passes defect classes by NAME only (compareReports: any count, any table). Its monkeyBaseline row 'console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n' therefore hides every cured expiry of a class that real-browser #119 has open (regression, reopened, run 46144276012). The CI autopilot gates (bottle and browser) do NOT hold this class and do go red on it. A baseline row should be dropped or scoped once an open ci-finding names it.  
*Evidence:* tests\client\bottle\monkeyBaseline.json classes['console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n'].why. scripts\freeze-gate.mjs:46 BASELINE. ci-findings-browser.json of 46144276012: key autopilot¦class¦console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n, first game 621. gh issue view 119: «Seen again after it was closed — run 46144276012» 2026-11-28T14:12:49Z. tests\console\freezeGateBaseline.spec.ts only requires why ≥ 24 chars (ran it: 3 passing).  
*Re-check:* node -e "console.log(Object.keys(require('tests/client/bottle/monkeyBaseline.json').classes))". Then: gh issue view 119 --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --comments.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The masking is limited to the bottle monkey gate: the autopilot gates caught and reopened #119, so the product is not masked overall. An uncured episode still turns the freeze gate red. The row claims one seed/table was clean in a browser. Autopilot games 621/630 are different walks (fault-injected), so they don't falsify that claim. 'Not geometric by name' reads the symptom names, while every why gives a layout cause.

**M9/layout-blind-falsified** — LAYOUT_BLIND rows start:reactor-face and start-bonus:visible-source are falsified by the real browser and still stand  
`[check-weakened · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Both classes are now real-browser defects: start:reactor-face in browser game 599 of 46144276012 (#199, «scene unmounted; seat none»), and start-bonus:visible-source (#161, triaged «harness», then fixed in product 00d2ef2a4e, seen again in browser 45911869971 and 46144276012). Both rows still re-tier the class to info in the bottle. No guard holds the add-only-after-browser law.  
*Evidence:* consoleBottle.ts:98 and :109 (blame 57ab29900b, d421a83d39). Browser game 599 JSON: «[wait-graph] ceiling start:reactor-face (ConsoleStartScene: seat none; shelf none; scene unmounted…) did not come in 8000 ms». The bottle printed it in 4 games (594, 596, 599, 602) as «(the bottle is blind here)». git grep LAYOUT_BLIND -- tests scripts src → only landRules.mjs:21 and a comment.  
*Re-check:* grep -o '[^"]*reactor-face[^"]*' dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-browser-46144276012-1-6/test-results/cause-classes/*game-599*.json. Then: gh issue view 161 --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --comments.  
*Against it (the verifier):* landRules.mjs:21 forbids CI-fix lanes from editing the bottle file, but it does not check the law. Bottle game 599 printed «seat empty; scene ceremony», a different and truly 0×0 cause than the browser's «scene unmounted», but the class regex excuses both. The browser lanes still file both classes (#161, #199), so only the bottle's own evidence is muted. That lowers the severity below high.

**M9/info-tier-is-retier** — The bottle's entire INFO tier (119 records, 7 classes) is defect lines re-tiered by the blind list; the CI gate never passes --zero  
`[claim-confirmed · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* In run 46144276012 all 119 bottle records (7 classes) are defect-tier journal lines whose class matches LAYOUT_BLIND, refiled as info. The CI bottle gate runs --gate without --zero (autopilot.yml:219), so no info record can fail it. LAYOUT_BLIND is a standing, documented waiver; PLAN §0 line 27 admits it. reactor-face shows up as a NEW defect in the same run's browser report, so its "not in browser" justification is stale.  
*Evidence:* tests\client\bottle\autopilot.bottle.ts:244 records.push(filed(blind.cls, 'info', …, `(the bottle is blind here) ${blind.text}`)); monkey.bottle.ts:155,182. autopilot-bottle.txt of 46144276012: «records 119 · of them defects 0», INFO classes take-turn 26, reward-place 17, start-flight:no-source 30, summary-tiles-unmeasured 30, board-card-bonus:hover 9, reactor-face 4, ma-price 3. All 7 match LAYOUT_BLIND rows.  
*Re-check:* head -12 <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-bottle.txt. Then: grep -n zero .github/workflows/autopilot.yml (comments only).  
*Against it (the verifier):* The waiver is documented on purpose ("named, never failing", rows added after browser checks) and PLAN §0 already says it (line 27, not 25). In code, info also comes from harness-error and the fault's own-word re-tier, so "entire tier" holds per run only. The bottle and browser runs use different seeds, so "hides a real defect" is an inference. Commit c41f1c2bb7 (#199), after the pin, addresses reactor-face.

**M9/replays-no-fix-off** — 44 class replays: none can turn its fix off; some assert absence without proving the situation was reached  
`[claim-unmeasured · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 2  
*Statement:* Not mechanised. 44 tests (39 it-sites, 4 in loops). There is no switch that disables a fix, so red-without-fix is an assertion. Of five read, three are strong with positive reach checks or fault injection (692, 1043, 1386). Line 253 asserts only the absence of ceilings after a 60-press monkey walk, with no check that the rating was owed. Line 905's class is bottle-shaped (an unmeasurable stage).  
*Evidence:* grep -n -i "fix off¦without the fix¦disable¦revert¦__TM_" classReplays.bottle.ts → only an eslint comment. :253-266 expects waits == [] and graph nodes == [] after monkeyWalk(…'fz0:terraforming-deal-start-nodraw', 60) with no positive reach assertion. :905 'a pick dealt onto a stage that cannot be measured'.  
*Re-check:* sed -n 253,266p and 884,904p tests/client/bottle/classReplays.bottle.ts.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The test at :905 does check positively that it got there: frames are ['hand:executing'] then ['hand:committed'], and the bar matches /Select/. So it is not an absence-only replay. Its «bottle-shaped» label is plausible but I did not verify it. The fault tests (:1012, :1053, :1092, :1120) assert wire.faulted, which proves the fault was injected but not that the fix is what keeps them green. I did not review all 44 tests for reach checks. I did not check the history for red runs at each fix's parent commit.

**M9/bottle-degraded-paths** — CI bottle is layout 'none': transferEndVerdict always 'empty', transitionend never, animationend without animationName  
`[blind-spot · low (high) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* The bottle runs with layout 'none' everywhere it runs on CI and in the gate. So transferEndVerdict stops at 'empty', no transitionend fires, Vue Transitions resolve at once, and its animationend (inline-duration elements only) carries no animationName. Name- and property-filtered end handlers fall to their safety ceilings. This is declared bottle-by-charter. CI's autopilot pairs it with a real-Chromium lane.  
*Evidence:* bottleDom.ts:23 «stays jsdom's 0×0 … left AS IS»; :284 layout default 'none'; :456 new PageEvent('animationend',{bubbles:true}). autopilot.bottle.ts:34 LAYOUT from env. git grep TM_BOTTLE_.*LAYOUT -- .github scripts → empty. transferEndVisible.ts:143-145 width<=4 → 'empty'. ConsoleRevealOverlay.vue:3133 checks ev.animationName. ConsoleCardAlbum.vue:199-205 onStripSettled (transitionend) → TURN_SAFETY_MS :272. runtime-dom.cjs.js:246 `if (!type) return resolve()`.  
*Re-check:* git -C . grep -n "TM_BOTTLE_AUTOPILOT_LAYOUT\¦TM_BOTTLE_MONKEY_LAYOUT" (only autopilot.bottle.ts:34 and monkey.bottle.ts:41). Then read bottleDom.ts:440-458.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The limitation is declared, not hidden: bottleDom.ts:22 and :265 leave geometry to the browser monkey. The PLAN B2 row measured the synthetic layout (no gain, its own artifacts) and kept it opt-in on purpose. A class goes into LAYOUT_BLIND or the bottle baseline only after a real browser did not name it. autopilot.yml runs a real-Chromium job (where=both/browser) with its own zero baseline, so CI as a whole is not blind to visual defects.  
*Re-measure:* Autopilot workflow with one bottle shard under env TM_BOTTLE_AUTOPILOT_LAYOUT=synthetic (workflow change needed), compared with the layout-none shard on the same games.

**M9/autopilot-baseline-stale** — autopilotBaseline.json: all 8 rows 'gone' in 46144276012 — a stale second copy of excuses now in LAYOUT_BLIND  
`[process-risk · low (low) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* autopilotBaseline.json: in run 46144276012 all 8 rows are unmatched («0 NEW · 8 gone · 0 kept»), and the gate ignores gone rows. Only 2 rows (summary-tiles-unmeasured, board-card-bonus:hover) are duplicated in LAYOUT_BLIND, and those are dead weight because LAYOUT_BLIND files them at info. The real risk is the other 6 (board-beat-park, cell-resource and its siblings, parliament-world:story-quiet): if they return at defect tier they stay excused.  
*Evidence:* autopilot-bottle.txt of 46144276012: «against the earlier run: 0 NEW · 8 gone · 0 kept», listing all 8 keys of tests\client\bottle\autopilotBaseline.json.  
*Re-check:* sed -n 13,24p <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-bottle.txt  
*Against it (the verifier):* The title's «second copy now in LAYOUT_BLIND» covers only 2 of the 8 rows. For those 2 the risk is nil, since LAYOUT_BLIND demotes them to info before any baseline match. Evidence that the rows are «gone» comes from one pass only; I did not check whether earlier passes also left them unmatched. Some may be gone because of fixes (for example 4392fb1fc2) rather than chance seeds.

**M9/bottle-determinism-ok-oom-masked** — Bottle determinism holds on CI evidence; but a crashed/OOM shard cannot fail the job and the gate has no game count  
`[claim-confirmed · low (low) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Holds for the bottle: two runs on the same games, 10 commits apart, gave identical 76 085 presses and identical info counts. But six games share one process per shard, the step ends `¦¦ true`, and monkey-report --gate checks no expected game count. A shard lost to OOM would shrink the denominator silently.  
*Evidence:* cmp.js: 46132235664 (7752ea0189) and 46098613041 (9db45debcb), both games 537+48, bottle presses 76085 = 76085. seedProcessRandom bottleServerHost.ts:507-514. autopilot.yml:106-108 NODE_OPTIONS 6144 + `¦ grep … ¦¦ true`. autopilot.bottle.ts:87 for (const n of SEEDS). Gate reasons in monkey-report.mjs:950-990 have no expected-games check.  
*Re-check:* node scratch/M9-bottle-fidelity/cmp.js on the two report dirs. Then: sed -n 100,108p .github/workflows/autopilot.yml  
*Against it (the verifier):* The report job adds --gate-red when needs.play.result != success. But `¦¦ true` keeps the play job green, so that check does not catch a dead shard. Determinism is shown only for the totals (press count and class counts), not game by game. The masking is a weakness in how the workflow is built: I found no run where a shard was actually lost, though I did not search all runs.

*Not examined by this auditor:* bottleNet.ts fault semantics vs a real socket/HTTP stack · The synthetic-layout mode's fidelity (it would need a bottle run, which is forbidden) · 39 of the 44 class replays · Server-side real timers beyond the BotTurnScheduler probe; cross-game server module state within one shard process · Per-world attribution of the 672/672 cards axis (autopilot-cards.mts per report) · The nightly Monkey (browser) report beyond grepping for baseline class names

### M10-judges-blind-spots — the instruments

*Scope:* Read the headers and decisive bodies of all twelve judges at pin 74910b95e2 (arbiter, oscillation, wait graph, one-owner + measure, screen shadow, leak detector, supervisor sampler, unanswerable, hand-over, reactivity / frame / motion witnesses). Ran one scratch script over the pinned PURE cores. Grepped the pin's own product bundle (build/main.js) for sentinel strings. Read Autopilot run 46144276012's reports. Counted opt-in e2e witnesses. No browser, no box, no spec run.

**M10/motion-sentinels-compiled-out** — The product's own «motion did not play / counter jumped / token not flown» lines are compiled out of the build every real-browser lane and the field run  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage visual  
*Statement:* The product detects these itself (railReward.noteDegrade, vpShareLedger «ticked without a token», transfer «the token is not flown», 17 private `.degraded` flags), but the lines sit behind `NODE_ENV !== 'production'` (26 gates, 20 console files) and are absent from the pin's product bundle: e2e, Monkey, the Autopilot's browser shards and the field cannot print them.  
*Evidence:* build/main.js of the pin (carries «74910b95e2», «1.2.3632»): 0 occurrences of «not shown as promised», «ticked without a token», «released before its flight had finished», «the token is not flown». Sources: railReward.ts:337, vpShareLedger.ts:293 and :353, consoleResourceTransfer.ts:1986. playwright-e2e.yml:102, monkey.yml:116, autopilot.yml:144 build with `npm run build`. Autopilot run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0), autopilot-browser.json: 48 games, 43 357 presses, class families liveness-side only; 0 lines of rail-reward / score-cell / [transfer].  
*Re-check:* `cd build && cat main.js chunks/*.js ¦ grep -c -F "not shown as promised"` → 0. `git -C . grep -n "NODE_ENV !== 'production'" -- src/client/console src/client/components/console ¦ wc -l` → 26.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The degrade state is still kept in production (railRewardState.degraded, vpShareState.last.mismatches, many data-*-degraded DOM attributes). Many per-card e2e specs read these, which fits PLAN:29's "specs written for it". Other degrades are always on through diagnostics (invariant.ts, netEnded, monkey "story degraded" lines). I did not verify the "17 flags" count or that every one of the 26 gates is a defect line. This is an observability gap, not a gameplay defect.  
*Re-measure:* After un-gating the degrade lines: `gh workflow run Autopilot --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars -f from=585 -f games=48 -f where=<the browser world>` (inputs as in autopilot.yml:240); read the new classes in autopilot-browser.json — the first census of motions that did not play in a real browser.

**M10/visual-defects-no-judge-confirmed** — CONFIRMED: no runtime judge asks «is what the player sees right?» — measured on the arbiter's pure core  
`[claim-confirmed · medium (high) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* No STAGE owns visual defects (PLAN.md:29). The liveness and oscillation arbiter is blind by construction to an accepting screen that is answerable but wrong: wrong stage, no cursor, taken presses that change nothing. Runtime visual-truth checks exist only as narrow per-subsystem sentinels ([transfer] end not painted / token painted under / told twice, which fail every e2e spec), not as a general judge.  
*Evidence:* Scratch arbiterBlind.mts over the pinned pure cores: «accepting, asked, still picture, no press: NEVER stalled in 600s» · «300 A presses all taken, picture never moves: NEVER stalled in 600s» · «no cursor on screen: NEVER stalled in 600s»; controls «not accepting: stalled at 11s», «6 A presses dropped: stalled at 6s». supervisorController.ts:539 (`accepting = beat === undefined && stranded === undefined && !isSurfaceAwaitingHandoff() && !standsOnDeadFrame`), :522–533 (the signature); servingOwnerMeasure.ts:12 «STATS ONLY».  
*Re-check:* `cd . && node node_modules/tsx/dist/cli.mjs <audit>/scratch/M10-judges-blind-spots/arbiterBlind.mts` (8 lines of output, under a minute).  
*Against it (the verifier):* Runtime visual-correctness sentinels do exist, printed in every build and failing every e2e spec: '[transfer] end not painted', 'surface folded under its token', 'token painted under', and 'told twice' (oneTeller.ts, the transferEndsGuard fixture). So the absolute claim 'no runtime judge asks is what the player sees right' is overstated. These sentinels cover only resource-transfer flights. Case 6 (rail digit) is not a distinct measurement.

**M10/one-teller-covers-one-unit** — «One unit — one teller»: the architectural guard claims exactly ONE unit at two call sites  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* oneTeller's claim-and-sentinel guard covers one unit only. That unit is `scale-step-rating`, claimed at railReward.ts:393 and scaleStepRatingBeat.ts:480, and any collision on it fails every e2e via `[transfer] told twice`. One other pair is kept apart structurally: the table's answer, by the answer ledger (declareHeldAnswers), which has no sentinel. Every other pair relies on local exclusions. No judge counts a unit told twice in general, or a surface present twice.  
*Evidence:* oneTeller.ts:26 `SCALE_STEP_RATING_UNIT = 'scale-step-rating'` — the only unit; `git grep -n "claimTeller(" -- src/client` minus the module → railReward.ts:393 and scaleStepRatingBeat.ts:480. The arbiter's signature counts the nine `PICTURE_SURFACES` (surfaceEdges.ts:21, supervisorController.ts:526) and judges no count; the shadow takes the owners present as a set. In run 46144276012 «told twice» occurs 0 times in both reports.  
*Re-check:* `git -C . grep -n "claimTeller(\¦_UNIT =" -- src/client` → three lines.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The oneTeller.ts header (lines 15-17) names a second architectural exclusion, the ANSWER LEDGER (consoleResourceTransfer.ts:1991-2013). It keeps the Greens' answer single by construction (the seeder attaches instead of playing) and is neither a phase gate nor a host flag, so "every other pair = local exclusion" is overstated, though it still has no sentinel. The finding is a coverage gap: no actual double-tell elsewhere is shown.

**M10/flow-route-unjudged** — A finished flow folding back into its list is ON the frame protocol; destination, crumb and carried card have no runtime judge  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage visual  
*Statement:* The only runtime route judge is FRAME_PROTOCOL: 20 of the 30 phase moves are legal, among them committed → browse and executing → browse, so a finished flow landing in its list is on protocol and silent. Nothing judges a conclusion's destination, the crumb's continuity or the carried card; the autopilot and the monkey read no crumb.  
*Evidence:* consoleWorkspaceFrame.ts:100–107 (`committed: ['browse', 'configure', 'executing', 'verdict', 'completing']`, `executing: ['browse', …]`); the one line — consoleWorkspaceStack.ts:2341 «off the frame's protocol». `git grep -c -E "con-wshead¦crumb" -- tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts tests/e2e/console-monkey.spec.ts tests/client/bottle/autopilot.bottle.ts` → no match, while 189 of 598 e2e specs read it.  
*Re-check:* `cd . && sed -n '100,107p' src/client/console/consoleWorkspaceFrame.ts`; the grep above.  
*Against it (the verifier):* workspaceConclusionFor (consoleWorkspaceFlow.ts:310) is the one leave-or-stay policy, and it has unit tests (consoleWorkspaceFlow.spec, consoleWorkspaceStack.spec). verbTrail records every X→browse fold with its callers (stack.ts:2326), but the trail is printed only inside other alarms (outcomeController:717, supervisor:856), so on its own it stays silent. No observed misrouting is cited, so high severity is overstated.

**M10/cursor-no-judge** — No judge checks that a cursor exists, is painted and stands on a live element; the one cursor line is dev-only and its «defect» shape is unreachable  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* No product judge asserts that a cursor exists, is painted or stands on a live element. The cursor only feeds the signature and press-picture strings (supervisorController.ts:521/528, :1153/1159), where null maps to '' and the first `[class*="--focused"]` anywhere in the document wins. The `[cursor] … never signalled ready` warn is dev-only and stripped from build/main.js, so the shapes.ts:445 `defect` row cannot be filed. A hidden v-show element satisfying the selector is plausible but not demonstrated.  
*Evidence:* supervisorController.ts:521 and :528 (`focused === null ? '' : …`); cursorArrival.ts:96–97 (gate on `process.env?.NODE_ENV !== 'production'`); build/main.js: «never signalled ready» only as `cursor:[{name:`backstop`,re:/^«([^»]*)» never signalled ready in /,tier:`defect``; docs/claude/console/cursor.md:81 «a named backstop with a dev warn»; `focusWitness` is imported by 3 of 598 specs. Scratch: «accepting, asked, no cursor on screen: NEVER stalled in 600s».  
*Re-check:* `git -C . grep -n -F -- '--focused"]' -- src/client/console` → 2 lines; `cd build && cat main.js ¦ grep -o -E ".{80}never signalled ready.{30}"`.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The hidden-collapsed-workspace example is reasoning, not reproduced. The 600 s scratch figure was not verified. e2e specs (console-cursor and 2 others) do check the cursor on specific flows, but they are not live judges. The finding shows no incident in the field, so 'high' looks overstated.

**M10/painted-vs-applied-unjudged** — Nothing compares a painted value with the applied view; 3 truth witnesses and 7 owed stories stand against dozens of private display holds  
`[blind-spot · medium (medium) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* Invariant 16 has no RUNTIME reader. The 3 truth witnesses (view-freshness: applied vs server; two hydro liveness witnesses) never compare a painted value with the applied view. That comparison exists only per scenario in e2e (e.g. console-board-story-waits.spec.ts:68). Display holds outside the hold registry (e.g. holdDeckDisplay) are bounded only by their owner flight's ceiling. Registry hold suppliers ARE reactive-witnessed (animationHold.ts:303).  
*Evidence:* `git grep -n "registerTruthWitness(" -- src/client` → 3 registrations (consoleHydroFlow.ts:772, :778; gameTransport.ts:554); `owePresentation(` → 7 call sites; `reactivePredicate(` → 6 call sites; 35 modules of src/client/console export a *Held* / *Display* / *Shown* function (boardBeatDisplayParams, holdDeckDisplay, trayDisplayCards, rallyPoolHeld, enactmentHeld, tallyShownOf …) — by name; at least 15 are holds.  
*Re-check:* `git -C . grep -l -E "^export function [a-zA-Z]*(Held¦Display¦Displayed¦Shown¦Frozen¦Withheld)[a-zA-Z]*\(" -- src/client/console ¦ wc -l` → 35; the `registerTruthWitness(` grep → 3.  
*Against it (the verifier):* E2e specs DO compare painted with applied per scenario: console-board-story-waits.spec.ts:68 checks that the top strip reads the applied view, plus console-discard-pile-truth and console-preservation-program. So "nothing compares" overstates it. The "6 reactivePredicate" count hides that every animation-hold supplier is witnessed via animationHold.ts:303. "At least 15 are holds" rests on name matching only; some matches are pure derivations (tallyShownOf).

**M10/overlap-clip-tokens-only** — Paint order is judged for tokens and 8×3 prompt/scene pairs; the generic overflow guard is written and inert in the product build  
`[blind-spot · medium (medium) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* No GENERIC judge for paint order or clipping covers the product build, apart from tokens and COVERED PROMPT. The root-overflow guard (composables/consoleOverflowGuard.ts:121-123) is inert under the production `npm run build` that CI e2e uses. Only console-effect-forecast arms it (`conOverflow=1`), yet about 41 specs assert there is no `[console-overflow]` message, so those asserts can never fail. Opt-in, per-spec paint and clip checks are wider than stated: about 27 specs call elementFromPoint, 39 specs and parliamentDrive check scrollWidth, and 6 specs read root overflow.  
*Evidence:* consoleOverflowGuard.ts:121–123 (`return process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production' ¦¦ guardForced()`); `git grep -l conOverflow -- tests` → console-effect-forecast.spec.ts only; `elementsFromPoint` / `elementFromPoint` in 6 files of src (consoleResourceTransfer, transferEndVisible, reactorPlaces, ConsoleParliamentParties, stickScrollTarget, startController); `expectHeadHonest` 1 spec, `expectRailHonest` 5, `paintWitness` 1.  
*Re-check:* `git -C . grep -l "elementsFromPoint\¦elementFromPoint" -- src`; `git -C . grep -l conOverflow -- tests`.  
*Against it (the verifier):* "Text cut read in 1+5 specs" and "every other presence probe is a rect" are undercounts. Many specs run their own elementFromPoint paint checks and scrollWidth clip checks, and parliamentDrive clip-checks the chassis blocks in about 125 files. These are local and opt-in, so the blind spot is still real. The path is missing its composables/ directory. I did not verify the COVERED PROMPT 8x3 count.  
*Re-measure:* One run of the Playwright E2E workflow with the harness appending `conOverflow=1` to every console URL: count the guard's warnings per spec — the first census of root overflow on the product build (needs that one harness line first).

**M10/press-taken-no-effect** — «presses-no-effect» covers A and B only; a taken A that does nothing and every direction are never counted  
`[blind-spot · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage visual  
*Statement:* On an accepting screen the arbiter counts only A/B that NO handler took, a taken B that left the picture unmoved on a frame past its commit (`backJudged`), and A/B swallowed by a declared gate. An A a handler took and answered with nothing, and any direction / X / Y / trigger, is outside the judge by its own text. The per-press picture probe exists for B only.  
*Evidence:* consoleLiveness.ts:218–221 (the three terms) and :113–114 («A press a surface took and answered with nothing visible … is no evidence»); inputEcho.ts:163–189 (`backUnmoved` is the probe's only consumer); supervisorController.ts:1150 `pressPicture` — «The picture a B is judged against». Scratch: «accepting, asked, 300 A presses all taken, picture never moves: NEVER stalled in 600s».  
*Re-check:* Read consoleLiveness.ts:218–221; run the scratch script (second line of its output).  
*Against it (the verifier):* The exclusion is deliberate and documented: A on a disabled tile is "no evidence", and the 2026-10-31 noop false stall involved X/<-. So this is a gap the design chose, not an oversight. A taken A that starts a request, a hold or a flight, or changes the signature, is still seen. The scratch is a pure-core simulation; no real field wedge from a taken A is shown. Small nuance: what the arbiter calls "backTaken" is already the unmoved count.

*Not examined by this auditor:* No browser, box or CI run: every statement about a live page is from source and from the product bundle's strings; no visual defect was reproduced. · The start director / start shadow (startDirector.ts, startServing.ts) — the opening's own judge — was not read. · docs/claude/console/screen-director.md, liveness-arbiter.md and state-delivery-and-handoff.md were not read in full (module headers were); a class the docs name and the code does not is not covered. · The screen shadow's cure table (which classes are armed) was not enumerated. · The 26 dev gates were not classified one by one as «defect line» vs «developer trace»; 5 strings were verified against the bundle. · The 35 display-hold modules are a grep of exported names, not a reading of each; the honest floor from the names read is 15. · The Monkey report 46140902727 and the E2E cause-classes artefact were not read for painted-content classes; only Autopilot run 46144276012 was. · Whether the client unit suite (development build) ever prints `[rail-reward] not shown as promised` and what it does with it was not checked. · No server-runner guard spec was run; none of the eight findings rests on one.

### M11-law-guard-replay — the instruments

*Scope:* Classified the 292 FREEZE ZERO commits since 2026-11-21 on the pin 74910b95e2. Method: a file-type classifier over `git log --name-only` (script in scratch), then by hand from the diffs of about 40 commits. Cross-checked the LAYOUT_BLIND and autopilotBaseline rows against Autopilot run 46144276012 (bottle and browser reports) and against issues #161/#194/#195/#199. Read the code of ten A commits to see whether each new spec fails without its fix. Read compareReports in monkey-report.mjs.

**M11/layout-blind-refuted-by-browser** — LAYOUT_BLIND rows hide two classes a real browser names as DEFECTS (#161 start-bonus:visible-source, #199 start:reactor-face)  
`[check-weakened · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* At pin 74910b95e2, LAYOUT_BLIND rows start:reactor-face and start-bonus:visible-source make the bottle file both as info, yet a real browser names both as defects (run 46144276012: ap0:599, ap0:602). The journal already logged browser hits before the pin (reactor-face 2026-11-17 runs 44085588741/44085603578; visible-source run 45911869971 g317, PLAN:2805) but kept the rows, against the :91 law. Only the bottle is blind; browser runs still file them (#161, #199).  
*Evidence:* dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.json classes: `wait-graph/ceiling/start:reactor-face` tier defect, first ap0:599 «scene unmounted; seat shows Poseidon»; `wait-graph/ceiling/start-bonus:visible-source` tier defect, first ap0:602. autopilot-bottle.json: reactor-face tier info, 4 tests, «(the bottle is blind here) … scene ceremony». tests/client/bottle/consoleBottle.ts:98,109. Issue #161 «Seen 3 time(s) — last … browser · games 585+48».  
*Re-check:* node -e "const j=require('<audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.json');for(const k of ['wait-graph/ceiling/start:reactor-face','wait-graph/ceiling/start-bonus:visible-source'])console.log(k,j.classes[k].tier,j.classes[k].first.seed)"; then read consoleBottle.ts:86-119.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The browser monkey/autopilot still detects and files these classes (#161 open, #199 fixed post-pin). Coverage loss is limited to the bottle's cheap signal, so "hide" overstates it. "Later disproved" is also wrong in timing: the journal knew of both browser hits at or before the pin. Bottle cause (scene ceremony) differs from the browser cause (scene unmounted), so the bottle lines may well be layout-only.

**M11/issue-111-relabel** — #111 closed by re-labelling the pad rung: the press is still swallowed («the routing is unchanged»)  
`[check-weakened · medium (high) · partly]` stage 2  
*Statement:* #111 was closed as "Fixed" by a stats-only relabel. During the deck pick's outgoing beat the press is still absorbed (routing unchanged); only its label moved from pad-MISS:deckPick to pad-beat:cinematic, and the CI confirmation (46144276012) counts that same label. Beat absorption is the ladder's standing convention for every cinematic, so this is not shown to be a regression. No test or replay pins the case, and a lost press on a visible question is unproven.  
*Evidence:* git show 983c3b60d0 -- src: padRouterController.ts `- notePadRung('deckPick');` `+ notePadRung(shell.deckPickOutgoing ? 'cinematic' : 'deckPick');` with handedTo(...) unchanged. git show -s 7c92666bd2: «→ pressed 50 – 600 ms into ANY opening of the hand said «consumed» and the cursor never moved … The presses of the opening are KEPT». The commit adds no test.  
*Re-check:* git -C . show 983c3b60d0 -- src/client/console/shell/padRouterController.ts ; git -C . show -s 7c92666bd2  
*Against it (the verifier):* Every other cinematic rung (trade fleet, deck draw, played hero, terraformed scene, and others) also absorbs the press and records 'cinematic'. The pre-existing comment said the outgoing beat "deliberately swallows everything". wiringController/revealController already treated deckPickOutgoing as serving nothing (2026-11-22). 7c92666bd2 was navigation inside the player's own surface, which is only an analogy. No artefact shows the start's question was painted and actionable at the press.  
*Re-measure:* Autopilot workflow, where=bottle, from=566 games=1 on the pin: read whether the press under the next question moves anything (not only the rung name).

**M11/b-fixes-without-replay** — ~20 product fixes of cited findings ship with no committed replay — proof is «game N replayed, before/after» in the message  
`[process-risk · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* About 16 FREEZE ZERO commits since 2026-11-21 fix a named finding in src/ only, with no committed class-specific spec or replay; proof is an uncommitted bottle or box replay. That breaks PLAN.md:107 («Every class found gets a law, a guard and a replay»). The generic judges that found them still run on every pass, and any game N replays by its number. What is missing is a pinned regression replay per class.

Examples: 85203abbe2 (#190, «Reaction specs 57/57» but no new spec), a3ec39f652 (game 18 «replayed: no defect»), 80f08d4209 (#192 «game 11 replayed in the bottle»), 55b63c5204 (#181), 76829e525c (box game 2907), c5414ee3ab (#113), d79bdbd45a, d93e15aa43, fa37325692, 7fdcf226fd (#138), 1be2cf4413 (#118), 1e8d51f607 (server change, fuzz find). `git show --stat` of each: src/ files plus PLAN/package only.

Examples include 1e8d51f607 (B.txt: finding none) and measurement commits (1be2cf4413). 3e34ede433 is covered by the gate's finale-diverted walks. Seeds do not rotate: Autopilot is manual, defaults to 1..24, and is deterministic per game number. The generic judges (stacked-faces witness, wait-graph ceilings) still guard each class at the symptom level.

**M11/compare-reports-retier-is-gone** — Report diff counts a re-tier as «gone»: «8 gone» includes classes present in the same report as INFO  
`[metric-wrong · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* compareReports only compares keys at the DEFECT tier. A class moved to info (by LAYOUT_BLIND or a planned tier) is printed as «gone», which looks like a cure. In this report summary-tiles appears in 30 of 48 games and board-card-bonus in 9.  
*Evidence:* scripts/monkey-report.mjs:606-615: `const defects = (report) => new Set(Object.entries(report?.classes ?? {}).filter(([, c]) => c.tier === 'defect')…` → `goneClasses: [...b].filter((cls) => !a.has(cls))`. autopilot-bottle.txt: «30 test(s) 30 rec invariant/broken/console-start:summary-tiles-unmeasured» under INFO and «gone invariant/broken/console-start:summary-tiles-unmeasured».

Only 2 of the 8 are affected, and no automation (ticket closing) reads goneClasses. I did not check whether PLAN.md actually counted these 2 as cured.

**M11/retier-to-planned-closes-issues** — #167 and the sitting-rise story closed by re-tiering to «planned» — the wait still ends on its due  
`[check-weakened · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 6

**M11/issue-197-instrument** — #197 closed by a spec change; the product diff is diagnostics only and the box proof has no run id  
`[harness-masks-product · low (medium) · partly]` stage 4  
*Statement:* #197 was closed by an e2e-only fix (the spec brings the driven client to the front). The src diff of 862a61cc78 adds only diagnostics (`sitting`, `paint` in __conReady); this is class C. The box proofs ("2 of 2", "12 of 12") have no run id. The painted-time law (4c867dbc20) deliberately leaves a starved page's hold unbounded. Its resume path IS unit-guarded (holdRuns.spec, green). Missing: a real-browser/e2e guard and a measurement of an occluded Electron window (ships backgroundThrottling:false).  
*Evidence:* git show --numstat 862a61cc78: src/client/console/e2eReadiness.ts +8 (`sitting`, `paint` diag fields), sittingDirector.ts +12 (`sittingDirectorDiag()` «diagnostics only»), tests/e2e/console-parliament-water.spec.ts, tests/e2e/parliamentDrive.ts. Message: «the page gave the motion 0 of 1004 ms» on a page reporting `visible`.  
*Re-check:* git -C . show 862a61cc78 -- src ¦ grep '^[+-]'  
*Against it (the verifier):* The proposal's starve-then-resume guard already exists as a unit test: holdRuns.spec "ON A PAGE THAT DOES NOT PAINT the ceiling waits". A visible page that paints nothing is detected as starved, because frame-based starvation is the design. The production Electron window sets backgroundThrottling:false (electron/main.ts:525), which lowers the occluded-window risk. The finding presents the law as having no guard at all; it lacks only an e2e/real-browser guard.  
*Re-measure:* Box, e2e: a one-page spec of a Parliament sitting with the Electron/Chromium window occluded but `visibilityState=visible` (backgroundThrottling as shipped). Does the hold resolve once the window is in front again, with no stuck line?

**M11/class-key-concat** — Notification starvation class keys concatenate without separator («cell-resourcenotification-only», «board-beat-drainnotification-only»)  
`[metric-wrong · low (low) · partly]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* Starvation class keys glue the hold label to its scope (`cell-resourcenotification-only`). The hold registry writes `label[scope]` (animationHold.ts:744), and causePart (cause.ts:33-44) strips the brackets. Splitting is still possible: scope is a closed two-value set. With several holds, `a[x], b[y]` becomes `ax-by`, so the class also varies with the set of standing holds. Fix in causePart or the label form, not the shapes row.  
*Evidence:* tests/client/bottle/autopilotBaseline.json row `notifications/starvation/cell-resourcenotification-only`; gh issue #189 title «notifications/starvation/board-beat-drainnotification-only».  
*Re-check:* grep -n 'notification-only"' tests/client/bottle/autopilotBaseline.json  
*Against it (the verifier):* "Cannot be split or read by a rule" is overstated: scope is only `blocking` or `notification-only` (animationHold.ts:84), so a suffix regex splits it. The proposal names the wrong place: the gluing is in causePart, not the shapes row. "Stable" holds only for a fixed set of holds; a multi-hold line gives a different, merged class. No spec pins the class form (specs pin only the raw bracketed line).

**M11/a-sample-holds** — Class-A discipline holds on a sample of ten: every added spec asserts on the changed code path  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Confirmed on 10 of 10 sampled A commits: 4392fb1fc2, 153a8c16e4, 7c92666bd2, 1d740f2ea5, 5503f494b5, 48155de46d, 9b271cbba9, 6d2068b2bb, b22c08f405, 3f9cd36d49. Each assertion reads the state or term the fix changed. Two caveats: 3f9cd36d49 checks that a class key is absent, which would also pass if the line were renamed; and classReplays.bottle.ts runs only in freeze-gate.yml (nightly or by hand), not on push CI.  
*Evidence:* git show 4392fb1fc2 -- tests: `expect(boardBeatParkState.draining, 'the drain the scene cut is over').to.equal(false)` after `releaseBoardScaleForScene()`. git show 1d740f2ea5: product term `consoleState.sheet === undefined`, and the spec presses LB then B and expects FINALE_BLOCK count 1. grep -rn classReplays .github → no hit; package.json:109 `test:bottle`; freeze-gate.yml:4 «the replays of every closed freeze class».  
*Re-check:* git -C . show 4392fb1fc2 1d740f2ea5 -- src tests ¦ grep -E '^[+-]' ¦ head -80

*Not examined by this auditor:* The 29 tests-only (T) commits, and the class-A commits beyond the sample of ten. · Whether each B fix's cited game still reproduces: that needs a bottle or Autopilot run, which is forbidden here. · Commits before 2026-11-21, including 4c867dbc20's painted-time law in depth. · Browser shards and reports of the other Autopilot runs (46132235664, 46098613041, 46079806794, 46072102662). · The source of the class-key concatenation (only its symptom was observed). · The Monkey report and the e2e cause-classes artefacts.

### V1-card-motion — visual defects

*Scope:* I read every card-motion director in scope (handDock delivery/reveal, cardDeal, cardExit, cardRise, cardIntake, cardReceive, cardDiscard, cardFlight, batch arrival, action reveal) on the pin, plus the seal and turn paths of ConsoleRevealOverlay, the draft clone flights, playedCardReturn and ConsoleStartScene's start-emerge. I traced how each degradation is reported, from the site through classify.ts, cause.ts, causeHarvest.ts and monkey-report to ci-findings. I re-measured on the THE LOG and cause-class files of 5 CI Autopilot runs (240 real-browser games) and on e2e run 46144266613. 38 site rows are in V1-card-motion-sites.json.

**V1/other-roads-not-named** — «the next passes name every other road that makes a card appear in the pack» — not met: ≥6 such roads in the hand delivery alone print nothing  
`[claim-not-met · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6

About 4 roads still put a card in the pack with no console line: no delivery element (landFallback, :797/:820), quietOut (:926), aborted (:731) and the safety cut (:870-880). A face-up packet is not silent: its plain carry hits no-source.  
*Evidence:* handDeliveryDirector.ts:626 `if (consoleReducedMotionActive() ¦¦ dock === null) { releaseRemaining(); …return; }` (the only record is noteTransferTrail('hand-intake:run',{dock})); :797 `landFallback.push(entryIdx)`; :926-931 quietOut → noteTransferTrail('hand-intake:quiet-out') + fade; :877 guarantee → finish(); noteTransferTrail (consoleResourceTransfer.ts:130) only pushes to an in-page ring. playedCardReturn.ts:219 `if (cards.every((card) => card.faceDown)) netEnded(...)`.  
*Re-check:* git -C . show 74910b95e2:src/client/console/handDock/handDeliveryDirector.ts ¦ sed -n '620,645p;790,800p;924,932p;870,890p'  
*Against it (the verifier):* A face-up return with no rect counts as hand-intake:no-source via plainCarry → intake, so playedCardReturn.ts:219-221 adds no silent road. A missing dock (:626) means there is no pack to appear in. Reduced motion is the designed path (:210-211). So the true count is about 4, not ≥6. The PLAN line also states a purpose for the next passes, not a delivered fact.

**V1/zero-log-not-evidence** — A zero in THE LOG / cause classes is not evidence that card motions played: 26 of 36 sites print nothing; only one spec reads the trail  
`[metric-gap · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Over 5 CI Autopilot runs (240 games) the log has one card-motion line ([start-emerge]). 26 of the 36 non-designed degradation sites (auditor's table; 5 spot-checked) end silently or trail/state-only, and the autopilot never reads __conReady().transfer.trail. So the zero bar cannot see a card that did not fly. 13 specs read the trail, but only research-startups:409 asserts a card-intake end. No spec or guard checks quiet-out/aborted/prompt-receive:field.  
*Evidence:* grep over dl/autopilot-report-{46072102662,46079806794,46098613041,46132235664}/autopilot-browser.txt and dl/autopilot-46144276012-…/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt for [hand-reveal¦hand-delivery¦hand-intake¦cardDealSequence¦consoleCardDiscard¦card-receive¦start-emerge¦played-return¦net-ended] → only `autopilot-browser.txt:94 warning [start-emerge] degraded to teleport` (game 594). `grep -n 'transfer\b\¦\.trail' tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts` → no match. V1-card-motion-sites.json: 26/38 rows reported_as silent/trail/state.  
*Re-check:* node -e over V1-card-motion-sites.json (count reported_as silent¦trail¦state); grep -rn "hand-intake:landed" tests → one spec  
*Against it (the verifier):* "Only one spec reads the trail" is false: 13 e2e specs read __conReady().transfer.trail (mostly rail/reaction events or diagnostic dumps). "Visible only in the trail" overstates it: per-card specs also see flights in the DOM (research-startups' carried proxy, deal-held/deal-proxy checks). The silent ends are visual degradations released by guarantees, not freezes, so high is too strong. The 26/36 count is the auditor's classification; I did not re-derive 21 of the rows.  
*Re-measure:* Autopilot workflow on 74910b95e2 with a patched console-autopilot.spec.ts that dumps __conReady().transfer.trail at game end. Then count hand-intake:quiet-out¦aborted, intake:no-trigger and prompt-receive:* per game.

### V2-token-motion — visual defects

*Scope:* I read the resource-transfer engine (consoleResourceTransfer.ts, resourceTransferDirector.ts, railReward.ts, transferEndVisible.ts), its 31 caller files (62 call sites), the action-commit rail, impulse and armed-effect code, startBonusGain, the classifier (classify.ts) and shapes.ts. I also read the CI build modes (autopilot.yml), the e2e transferEndsGuard and the bottle's LAYOUT_BLIND list. I grepped five Autopilot reports, the Monkey report and the e2e run's artefacts for token classes. No spec was run.

**V2/never-flew-not-counted** — A token that never flew is counted nowhere: engine degrades reach only a trail and caller flags; 46 of 62 call sites drop the reason  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* A token that never STARTED (no-source, no-destination, no-stage, detach-expired) is counted by no gate on a production/CI page. It leaves only a 60-entry in-memory trail and an optional caller onDegrade. About 16 of 61-62 call sites pass one, and those flags only warn in dev. The transfer/no-visible-end shape exists but its warn is dev-only, so it is dead in vite builds. A stalled in-flight token IS witnessed by the director's transfer:touchdown ceiling.  
*Evidence:* consoleResourceTransfer.ts:983-993: no-source → trail('run:earlyreturn') + run.onDegrade?.(…,'no-source'). :1026-1037: no-dest, same. :1116-1126: wave safety → trail('run:safety') only. :1136-1140: no-stage. :379-388: foldSentinel prints only if a band surface left. shapes.ts:461-475: transfer/* has no 'never flew' shape. Count: 16 of 62 call sites pass onDegrade (grep below).  
*Re-check:* cd .; for f in $(grep -rln 'runResourceTransfers(' src/client ¦ grep -v consoleResourceTransfer.ts); do echo $f $(grep -c 'runResourceTransfers(' $f) $(grep -c onDegrade $f); done; sed -n 983,993p src/client/console/resourceTransfer/consoleResourceTransfer.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* "transfer/* has no never-flew shape" is literally wrong: no-visible-end exists, though production builds never emit it. 'stalled' is not silent for started flights: the director's per-flight guarantee (budget+1200) fires before the wave safety (budget+2200+400n) and emits a counted transfer:touchdown ceiling with frame-witness tiering. The 46/62 figure comes from line counts, not a per-site audit.  
*Re-measure:* After the fix in proposal: Autopilot where=browser games=48, plus Playwright E2E. Read the new class counts: this is the first real number of silent no-flights on healthy passes.

**V2/dev-only-degrade-lines** — 18 'not shown as promised / ticked without a token / not flown' warns are dev-only, absent from the production bundle every real-browser lane and Sentry run  
`[metric-gap · medium (high) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* About 18 degrade warns about token stories ('not shown as promised', 'ticked without a token', 'not flown') only run when NODE_ENV is not 'production'. They are also NOTE-tagged, so no production lane prints or counts them (e2e, autopilot browser, monkey, Sentry). The degrade STATE still reaches production through window.__conReady, and targeted per-card e2e specs assert on it. The real gap is that nothing counts these degrades run-wide.  
*Evidence:* grep -rn -A2 "NODE_ENV !== 'production'" src/client/console src/client/components/console ¦ grep console.warn → 21 sites, 18 of them token stories. E.g. railReward.ts:337-338 '[rail-reward] … not shown as promised', vpShareLedger.ts:353-354 'ticked without a token', consoleResourceTransfer.ts:1986-1987 'no visible <end>'. autopilot.yml:106 NODE_ENV: development (bottle) vs :144 npm run build (browser). classify.ts:82-92 NOTE includes rail-reward, card-reaction, played-hero, score-cell, scale-swing, tile-placement.  
*Re-check:* cd .; grep -rn -A2 "NODE_ENV !== 'production'" src/client/console ¦ grep console.warn; sed -n 100,147p .github/workflows/autopilot.yml  
*Against it (the verifier):* Every site also sets a reactive *.degraded field, and window.__conReady exposes it in production builds. Per-card e2e specs assert that it never degraded, about 435 'degraded' mentions in tests/e2e. The transfer end has an ungated production sentinel that turns every spec red. So 'the whole C2 class has no counted witness' is overstated. What is missing is a generic count: these tags are not in the harness STALL_MARKERS, and the bottle and monkey reports do not read them.

**V2/no-visible-end-unobservable** — Shape transfer/no-visible-end (planned) can never fire in any CI lane: dev-gated in the browser, and the bottle's 0x0 rects read 'empty', which it never names  
`[metric-wrong · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* The shape is dead in the measurement. warnCoveredEnds prints only when NODE_ENV is not production, and only for covered, faded or leaving. Browser lanes are production. The bottle's verdict is 'empty' (width ≤ 4), which coveredEnds filters out. Zero planned lines for this law is a property of the instrument, not of the flow.  
*Evidence:* consoleResourceTransfer.ts:1984-1988 (dev gate); :1815-1818 (covered/faded/leaving only); transferEndVisible.ts:144 'r.width <= 4 … why: empty'; bottleDom.ts:23 'getBoundingClientRect() stays jsdom's 0×0'. grep -rl 'no-visible-end¦no visible source¦no visible destination' over all of <audit>/dl → no match.  
*Re-check:* grep -rl 'no visible source\¦no visible destination\¦no-visible-end' <audit>/dl; sed -n 1815,1818p and 1984,1988p of src/client/console/resourceTransfer/consoleResourceTransfer.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* bottleDom.ts has an opt-in synthetic layout (TM_BOTTLE_MONKEY_LAYOUT / TM_BOTTLE_AUTOPILOT_LAYOUT=synthetic) with 120x60 rects, under which a dev-build bottle could in principle print the line. So 'never anywhere' is too strong; 'never in CI' holds. The trail entry run:earlyreturn also records covered ends, but it is not a counted shape.

**V2/wave-safety-ends-done** — The transfer wave's safety (stalled) releases every counter, yet its kernel scope ends 'done', so 'expired 0' and the coverage keys cannot see it  
`[metric-wrong · medium (medium) · partly]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* A guarantee fired by its TIMER (e.g. the transfer wave's stall safety, consoleResourceTransfer.ts:1116) emits no kernel trace and does not change the scope's result (flowScope.ts:559-588). So kernel 'expired 0 · bounds 0' cannot show it. A stalled wave that recovers ends 'done'. One fired on screen-leave ends 'cancelled'. One that never recovers has no end key. Fix: timer-fired guarantees emit a trace, not 'every fired guarantee ends expired'.  
*Evidence:* consoleResourceTransfer.ts:1064 wave=open('scene','transfer-wave'); :1116-1126 the guarantee's release sets safetyFired; :1231 'wave.end();' (no how). flowScope.ts:559-588: guarantee fires release, and the scope result is unchanged. The e2e cause-classes file (run 46144266613) carries 'end scene:transfer-wave done'.  
*Re-check:* sed -n 1110,1127p and 1226,1233p src/client/console/resourceTransfer/consoleResourceTransfer.ts; sed -n 559,588p src/client/console/kernel/flowScope.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* "done either way" is overstated. guarantee() also fires via onEnd when the session scope ends (screen leaves), and the wave then ends 'cancelled'. A stall whose touchdowns never resolve leaves the scope open with no end key. The firing is still recorded outside the kernel: trail 'run:safety' and onDegrade 'stalled', which for card-reaction callers means cardReactionState.degraded plus a non-production console.warn. The proposal would mislabel onEnd fires as expired. The cited CI artefacts were not downloaded.

*Against it (the verifier):* There is only one browser record, in a CI run with a 48-game load, and the wait ended on its deadline. **V2/catchup-net-uncounted** — The flight catch-up (arc/settle/unflown) is a net that teleports or absorbs a token with 'never a line'; the 2026-11-23 cure removed a defect by widening it  
`[net-fires-on-healthy · low (medium) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Arc and settle catch-ups (resourceTransferDirector.ts:676-690) return true from the safety, so they never produce a line, even when painted.starved() is false. They are recorded only in trail flight:ends.caughtUp and no report counts them. 'unflown' is NOT silent: it returns false and produces the transfer:touchdown ceiling, which is planned if starved and a defect otherwise (:419-426; consoleResourceTransfer.ts:1350-1355). Gap: an arc caught up on a non-starved page should produce a line, and the catch-ups need a rate.

Settle: the chip is already on its destination, so absorbing it is harmless. The safety fires only at budget+1200 ms, after the motion has already overrun, so 'fires on a healthy pass' is not shown, and no run data shows an arc catch-up on a non-starved page. The journal never claims the arc catch-up is counted.  
*Re-measure:* Once the catch-up is counted: Playwright E2E with TM_E2E_CPU_THROTTLE=4 (and unthrottled) over the token specs (console-rail-landing, console-action-price-fold, console-card-reaction). Read caught-up arc/unflown per 1000 flights.

**V2/paint-sentinels-hold** — CONFIRMED: the 'flew but landed wrong' sentinels speak in every build and turn every e2e spec red; the counter is never lost  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage visual  
*Statement:* Holds. end-not-painted, surface-folded, painted-under, told-twice and frame-left-holding are ungated, defect-tier (shapes.ts:461-475) and enforced by transferEndsGuard on every spec. Every degrade branch calls onArrive or noteCardResourceLanding, so no counter strands. The start-bonus and touchdown ceilings are proper wait-graph ceilings and do count (seen in browser runs 46098613041 and 46072102662).  
*Evidence:* tests/e2e/consoleTest.ts:734-756 (transferEndsGuard); tests/console/transferLiveEnds.spec.ts:119-125; consoleResourceTransfer.ts:970-974, 1034-1036, 1119-1124, 1137-1139, 1239-1243 (onArrive on every path); autopilot-report-46098613041/autopilot-browser.txt:15 'wait-graph/ceiling/transfer:touchdown'.  
*Re-check:* cd . && export PATH="$APPDATA/fnm/node-versions/v24.22.3/installation:$PATH" && npx mocha --import=tsx --require tests/testing/setup.ts tests/console/transferLiveEnds.spec.ts

*Not examined by this auditor:* Rail value badges, track markers, Ares adjacency flights and card-bonus payout concurrency: I read only their transfer callers (adjacencyPayoutBeat, cityDataPayoutBeat — no onDegrade), not their own docs or motion code. · colonyLedger and colonyTrade internals beyond their runResourceTransfers calls and hold expiry. · The Parliament reward beat (parliamentRewardBeat.ts) and sittingDirector degrade paths beyond 'no onDegrade passed'. · Real-browser frequency of C1/C2 degrades: no existing artefact records them, and measuring needs the instrumentation proposed here plus a CI run. · Whether no-stage (layer not mounted) is reachable on a healthy pass: not determined. · The ci-finding issues (#161 etc.): not opened with gh. I relied on the pre-downloaded reports.

### V3-surface-scene-motion — visual defects

*Scope:* Read src/client/console/surfaceMotion/* (director, descend, handoff net, model), consoleActionOutcome/Focus/EffectsFocus motion, loadingScreenState scene holds, ConsoleBoardSection board-fit, classify.ts and shapes.ts tiers, monkey-report gateVerdict. Counted transitions, hooks and teleports, and classified the leave hooks that measure layout (scratch scripts). Read browser autopilot run 46144276012 (report txt/json, ci-findings) and issue #198. I ran no spec and did not use the client bundle. 20 site rows are in the sites JSON.

**V3/visual-defects-unowned-confirmed** — No stage owns visual defects: surfaceMotion prints nothing; degradation lines are NOTE tier, outside every gate  
`[claim-confirmed · high (critical) · partly]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* TRUE on the pin. src/client/console/surfaceMotion/** prints nothing. Its motion net (episodeNet → scope.guarantee) catches up silently. NOTE-tier degradation lines ('start-emerge' and others) reach only the log section, and gateVerdict reads that only under --zero, which no workflow passes. So a visual degradation never becomes a class or a ci-finding. PLAN:29 already lists this gap as open. (Run 46144276012 has 10 classes, none of them motion.)  
*Evidence:* `grep -n "console\.\(warn\¦debug\¦error\¦info\¦log\)" src/client/console/surfaceMotion/*.ts` → no output. classify.ts:79-99 NOTE «a scene that degraded honestly … Not an error». scripts/monkey-report.mjs:950-966 log check is inside `if (opts.zero === true)`. `grep -rn "\-\-zero" .github/workflows package.json scripts/ci` → none. Run 46144276012 autopilot-browser.txt:94 «[start-emerge] degraded to teleport» ×1 (game 594); autopilot-browser.json .classes holds only workspace-anchor/dropped and scene-transition/hold-bound.  
*Re-check:* Run the two greps above. Then: node -e "console.log(Object.keys(require('<audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.json').classes))"  
*Against it (the verifier):* The evidence says .classes holds «only workspace-anchor/dropped and scene-transition/hold-bound». That is false: there are 10 classes (wait-graph, leak-detector, watchdog, liveness stall, session-rebuild …), though none of them is a motion one. I did not verify the counts «≥13 fallbacks» and «86/64 NOTE sites». PLAN:29 already names this gap as unowned and leaves it to the fresh look, so this re-confirms a known gap rather than finding a hidden one. «critical» overstates it.

**V3/pick-bridge-strands-panel** — Pick-bridge re-show returns before the entrance's heal: a panel whose entrance was killed mid-tween stays invisible  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* At 74910b95e2 a pick-bridge leave (surfaceMotionDirector.ts:1051-1058) and surfaceEnterCancelledHook (:1241) only killLive() a live entrance. Nothing clears the panel's frozen inline autoAlpha/scale, and the re-show's early return at :366 skips every heal. A panel cut mid-entrance stays at the opacity it had reached (invisible only if cut in its first frame) until the next real entrance. No spec covers it. Unproven whether a real flow opens the bridge inside the ~210 ms window.  
*Evidence:* surfaceMotionDirector.ts:366 `if (isPickBridgeHidden() ¦¦ pickReturn ¦¦ variant === 'headless' ¦¦ variant === 'drawn') { killLive(el); done(); return; }` comes before :386 `const nested = panelsOf(el).slice(1)` / clearProps. :1051-1058 the leave under pickSuppressed does killLive(el); done(). Entrance tweens use fromTo autoAlpha:0 with clearProps only at completion (:458, :580). tests/client/console/surfaceShadeYield.spec.ts covers only the shade.  
*Re-check:* Read surfaceMotionDirector.ts:336-400 and :1040-1060. Then `grep -rn motionPickHidden tests` → only the shade specs.  
*Against it (the verifier):* "Stays invisible" is overstated: GSAP freezes the panel at whatever the tween had reached. With expo.out, a cut at 50 ms leaves about 0.8 opacity, so only a cut in the first frame gives full invisibility. The defect needs the pick bridge to start while an entrance is still live (about 210 ms, up to about 1.4 s for a section). The hand pick usually follows a player press, and I did not show a real flow that does this. The heal block at 380-398 covers only nested panels and anchors; each entrance's own fromTo heals the outer panel. The next real entrance re-poses it.  
*Re-measure:* Client unit (main session): mount a band surface, call surfaceEnterHook, advance 50 ms, set surfaceMotionState.pickSuppressed=true, run surfaceLeaveHook, unset it, run surfaceEnterHook, and assert the panel has no inline opacity/visibility.

`[harness-masks-product · medium (medium) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* #198: boardTweening has no bound; only the 6 s board-fit curtain hold names it. Seen once (run 46144276012, game 630), 6.6 s after a page rebuild in that game (walk :123, :127), so not on a healthy pass. Unknown whether the look stopped (:935) or polled with no convergence (:968); 02ac75db1e adds diagnostics to tell them apart. A latch outside a curtain is inferred, not observed.

Run 46144276012 autopilot-browser.txt:11 `1 test(s) 1 rec scene-transition/hold-bound/board-fit`.

One sighting only.

**V3/outcome-unfold-stale-pops** — Action outcome zone pops in full-size when its 1500 ms armed origin expires (it unfolds from its own rect, so no fade fallback either)  
`[blind-spot · low (high) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* The stale fallback is real: past FRESH_MS 1500 the zone unfolds from its own rect, an inset(0)→inset(0) no-op with no fade. But on the main card-action path the stage opens synchronously at confirm (CardActions onComposerConfirm sets outcomeFlow before submit-batch), so that path does not depend on machine speed. Only deferred openings are exposed: TR53 pendingAfterRail, an unclaimed drawSignal draw, and the colony focus-stage handoff (outcomeHandoffDue). All are untested.  
*Evidence:* src/client/console/consoleActionOutcomeMotion.ts:55 `const FRESH_MS = 1500;` · :88 `now() - armedAt < FRESH_MS` · :103 `const from = takeOrigin() ?? descendRectOf(zone);` · :111 `if (from === undefined ¦¦ !descendUnfold(...))` (the fade runs only when this is false). workspaceDescend.ts:298-306 descendUnfold animates only clipPath and returns true. No test references armOutcomeOrigin or playOutcomePhase (grep tests).  
*Re-check:* Read consoleActionOutcomeMotion.ts:55-115 and workspaceDescend.ts:287-306. Then `grep -rln armOutcomeOrigin tests` → none.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The finding's timing premise ("taken after SETTLE_AT 700 ms + server RTT + admission") is false for the main ConsoleActionComposer path. The claim is opened synchronously at submit, and the code comment says the pending stage opens "now, not when the batch lands". So the 1500 ms window is not reached there whatever the server speed. Even on the stale paths the items still cascade in, so the content is not a bare pop, though the zone frame itself gets no motion.  
*Re-measure:* Box: one card-action e2e with an artificial 1 s server delay (TM_E2E server latency env if one exists), sampling the outcome zone's clip-path on its first painted frame.

**V3/episode-net-silent** — The hook net episodeNet fires silently: a motion skipped to its end on a healthy pass is never counted  
`[metric-gap · low (medium) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* episodeNet (promptHandoffMotion.ts:59-103; callers promptHandoffMotion.ts:186, surfaceMotionDirector.ts:319) ends a live episode silently when its GSAP clock stops (two looks, no tick) or it passes motionCeilingMs+4000: progress(1)/finish() then done(), no line. guarantee (flowScope.ts:559-588) logs nothing. A replaced episode or ended owner only calls finish() and is normal lifecycle. Healthy-pass frequency unmeasured.  
*Evidence:* src/client/console/surfaceMotion/promptHandoffMotion.ts:89-101 `look()`: on the catch-up path `episode.progress(1)` / `args.finish()`, with no console line. src/client/console/kernel/flowScope.ts:559-588 `guarantee` records no diagnostic. tests/client/console/surfaceEpisodeNet.spec.ts asserts the catch-up, not its absence.  
*Re-check:* Read promptHandoffMotion.ts:59-104 and flowScope.ts:559-588.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The list of paths is too broad. A replaced or killed episode is not caught up (progress(1) runs only when current() is true), and a replacement or the owner's end is normal lifecycle, not a net firing. A stopped clock is expected in a hidden tab, because rAF stops there. The quoted law is not word for word in PLAN.md; it matches the owner's 2026-11-25 nets-are-insurance rule. No artefact shows the net firing on a healthy pass, so the harm is unproven.  
*Re-measure:* Autopilot browser lane with a counter: instrument the look() catch-up branch to emit a 'motion/net-catch-up' info line (one-line patch on a branch), then run gh workflow run Autopilot -f games=48 -f where=browser.

**V4/unshaped-degradations** — 38 console.warn families have no shape: degradations like [start-emerge] degraded to teleport get no class or tier and never reach the gate  
`[blind-spot · medium (medium) · partly]` stage visual

That is because of their kind, not because a shape is missing. Degrade/teleport fallbacks like [start-emerge] show up only in the autopilot LOG. CI runs --gate, which ignores the LOG, and --zero runs in no workflow. In run 46144276012 the fallback was listed (1× in 1 game) and failed nothing.  
*Evidence:* ConsoleStartScene.vue:9251 console.warn('[start-emerge] degraded to teleport'). shapes.ts has 39 family keys; 38 warn families in src/client have none (played-hero, hand-reveal, deck-draw, colony-trade, scale-swing, card-reaction, start-emerge …). .github/workflows/autopilot.yml:219,225 use --gate, not --zero. 46144276012 autopilot-browser.txt LOG: «1× in 1 game(s) warning [start-emerge] degraded to teleport».  
*Re-check:* grep -rhoE "console\.warn\(\s*[`'\"]\[[a-z0-9 -]*\]" src/client ¦ sort -u, then compare with the family keys of shapes.ts (scratch/V4-holds-ceilings/warnfam.txt vs shaped.txt).  
*Against it (the verifier):* causeOf is undefined because classifyLine says kind 'note'. shapes.ts is read only for stall/integrity, and a stall/integrity tag with no row is '<family>/unshaped' at tier DEFECT. All 38 tags are classified on purpose as notes ('a scene that degraded honestly'), and diagnosticsClassification.spec.ts already guards that every tag has a class. So the proposed 'every warn has a shape' guard would be inert. 'Counted nowhere' is wrong: the LOG counts it.
