The guard also refuses Promise.race, not only wait*/await* helpers.

**V4/hurry-end-state-unguarded** — Hurry: cutting card reactions lands the end state as a tick, but no guard covers the other hurry paths  
`[blind-spot · low (low) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* hurriedBeatPromiseGuard has one purpose: a Promise on scheduleHurriedParliamentBeat. No general hurry end-state law exists. Card-reaction cut (released holds), hurryColonyCity and hurryChairmanQuestReaction have end-state unit specs. hurryCeremony, hurryOwedWalk, hurryDescendEpisode, hurryParliamentVoteEnter, hurryColonyRoster have none. Dropped rivalQueue holds nothing of the viewer's; only rival stories are lost, under a 'reaction:cut' trail that does not list them.  
*Evidence:* tests/console/hurriedBeatPromiseGuard.spec.ts: `line.includes('new Promise') && line.includes('scheduleHurriedParliamentBeat(')`. cardReactionDirector.ts:1873-1899 `entry.held.splice(0).forEach((spec) => releasePanelRewardHold(spec)); rivalQueue.splice(0);`. Other paths: hurryColonyCity, hurryColonyRoster, hurryCeremony, hurryOwedWalk, hurryDescendEpisode, hurryChairmanQuestReaction, hurryParliamentVoteEnter.  
*Re-check:* grep -rn "export function hurry\¦function hurry" src/client; read the guard spec (40 lines).  
*Against it (the verifier):* 2 of the 7 'unguarded' paths, colonyCity and chairmanQuest, have unit specs on their hurry/abort end state (live false, holds empty, flow done). The rivalQueue drop releases nothing: per the playRivals comment, «Nothing of the viewer's is held». The cut also logs a 'reaction:cut' trail, so it is not silent. The quoted code is from cutCardReactions, not hurryCardReactions. The guard was never meant as a general law, so "narrow scope" is not a defect of the guard.

**V4/guards-baselines-zero** — Confirmed: the three ceiling ratchets are at zero and the primitive guard is green on the pin  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage 5  
*Statement:* Holds on the pin. All three baselines are {} (4 bytes). ceilingWaitGuard passes. Every hold declares an expire, every ceiling wait names what stands in its way, and no bound is scaled down by the motion setting. The zero covers the form of a ceiling, not whether it fires.  
*Evidence:* tests/console/ceilingWaitNamesBaseline.json, holdExpireBaseline.json and motionCeilingBaseline.json are each `{}`. Run: `npx mocha --import=tsx --require tests/testing/setup.ts tests/console/ceilingWaitGuard.spec.ts` → «2 passing (65ms)».  
*Re-check:* cd . && cat tests/console/*Baseline.json ¦ head; run the mocha line above.

*Not examined by this auditor:* The per-site player-visible fallback for most of the 59 ceiling waits: sites.json carries a generic description except for the classes that fired. · Hurry paths other than hurryCardReactions (colony city / roster, endgame ceremony, owed walk, descend, chairman quest, vote enter) — I did not read them. · Whether the rivalQueue entries dropped by cutCardReactions held rail rows. · presentation-ledger story-due and notifications starvation in depth, and screenHandoff ceiling semantics. · The performance mode as a designed degradation — not looked at. · No client-bundle spec was run, and nothing was remeasured on CI or the box (rules: read-only, time box).

### V5-presentation-delivery — visual defects

*Scope:* I read the presentation ledger, screenHandoff, boardBeatPark, remote placement handover, ceilingWait, the shapes/classify/cause tables, the notification and reveal drop sites, the card-reaction / rail-reward / played-hero verifiers and the autopilot workflow. I ran presentationLedgerDegrade.spec (4 passing) and grepped the pin's production build/ for the warn strings. I also read the Autopilot 46144276012 reports (bottle and browser, shards), four earlier autopilot reports and the nightly Monkey. 33 sites are in V5-presentation-delivery-sites.json.

**V5/promise-verifiers-dev-only** — «Not shown as promised» verifiers are compiled out of every CI-browser/production build and are NOTE family, so this class has no denominator  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* 18 presentation-promise verifiers print their console line only when NODE_ENV!=='production', so the line is compiled out of CI and production builds. Their degraded STATE is still set in production. Only railReward.degraded is exposed through __conReady, which targeted e2e specs read. Autopilot, monkey, Sentry and the bottle count none of this class, so the zero does not measure it. 'transfer' is an INTEGRITY family, not NOTE.  
*Evidence:* cardReactionDirector.ts:1146 `if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') { console.warn(`[card-reaction] not shown as recorded…`; railReward.ts:337; consolePlayedHero.ts:563/1703/1760/2610. Built JS on the pin: «not shown as recorded» js=0, «not shown as promised» js=0, «the token is not flown» js=0 (only in .js.map), while «degraded after» js=1. cause.ts:59 `if (cls.kind !== 'stall' && cls.kind !== 'integrity') return undefined`; classify.ts NOTE list holds 'card-reaction','rail-reward','played-hero','score-cell'… autopilot.yml:144 browser = `npm run build`.  
*Re-check:* cd . && grep -rn "process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production'" src/client ¦ wc -l (25). Then grep -rl --include=*.js "not shown as promised" build ¦ wc -l (0). Then sed -n 59,62p src/common/diagnostics/cause.ts.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The state is still written in production: cardReactionDirector.ts:1145, railReward.ts:336, consolePlayedHero.ts:562. railReward.degraded is readable through __conReady, and console-action-price-fold.spec.ts:116-132 reads it. Many targeted e2e specs assert "never degraded" through data attributes (arboretum:547, aquatic:771). So "e2e cannot see them" is overstated for part of the class. The transfer line is an INTEGRITY family, not NOTE. I did not check which commit build/ came from.

**V5/remote-landing-handover-silent** — The remote-landing planned line went to zero partly because the handover now ends the wait SILENTLY; the tile still appears without its flight  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* The law removed a real 20 s wait. The outcome the planned line used to count still happens: another seat's tile placed while the viewer reads the hand, then the generation scene, the draft door or the finale. It is now counted by nothing: wait.end() says nothing. Part of «planned: 15/38 → 0» is a line removed, not a story delivered.  
*Evidence:* consoleRemotePlacement.ts:1862 `const handed = behind && handedOver;` → :1865 `wait.end();`. ceilingWait.ts:50 `/** The wait was abandoned (its run is gone) — the node ends, nothing is said. */`. screenHandoff.ts:185 calls handRemoteLandingsOver() on every poll of a transition that waits for landings. PLAN 2305: formerly 15 lines in 38 games, 44 in the second hundred.  
*Re-check:* First sed -n 1858,1870p src/client/console/tilePlacement/consoleRemotePlacement.ts, then sed -n 49,51p src/client/console/waitGraph/ceilingWait.ts. Then grep -n handRemoteLandingsOver src/client/console/screenHandoff.ts.  
*Against it (the verifier):* PLAN states the silent no-flight reveal openly as design, so the journal does not claim these were flights. The rest of the old planned class really became flights: with no clock behind the screen, the tile flies when the board returns. What the player sees in the handed-over case is the same as before (the tile appears without its flight). What is new is a measurement gap, not a regression the player sees. The size of the handed-over share is unknown.  
*Re-measure:* Once a counter exists: gh workflow run Autopilot -f from=585 -f games=48 -f name=ap0 -f where=browser, then read the new handed-over count per game.

**V5/reaction-expired-silent** — An unclaimed card-reaction group is dropped at the next answer with no line, only a 16-entry in-memory ring  
`[blind-spot · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* expireAttached drops recorded reaction groups that no host claimed by the next apply block. The player sees the counters tick and no card rise. The drop goes only to reactionIdentity's letGo ring (16 entries) and the transfer trail. It is never counted, and no autopilot or e2e asserts letGo is empty on a healthy pass.  
*Evidence:* cardReactionDirector.ts:501-509 `attached = attached.filter((a) => !expired.includes(a)); expired.forEach((a) => noteReactionLetGo(..., 'expired'))`. reactionIdentity.ts:54-59 noteReactionLetGo pushes to `letGo`, keeps 16, no console line. `grep -rln letGo tests` → only cardReactionDirector.spec.ts and console-research-startups.spec.ts (which reads a different `letGo` local).  
*Re-check:* sed -n 495,510p src/client/console/cardReaction/cardReactionDirector.ts; sed -n 54,59p src/client/console/cardReaction/reactionIdentity.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* The doc comment calls this deliberate: the host that held the rows ticked them itself, so the amounts are shown and only the reactor's identity is lost. PL-1056 (l.1196) belongs to the summarized path, which is told by a feed card, and the finding correctly sets it apart. I could not measure how often expiry fires in practice: that needs autopilot or browser runs, which the audit rules forbid.  
*Re-measure:* Read `__conReady().reaction.letGo` at the end of each autopilot game; this needs a harness change, and the main session runs Autopilot browser 48 games.

**V5/bottle-baseline-gone-is-not-trend** — The bottle's «gone presentation-ledger/story-due/*» repeats in every run: it is a diff against a committed baseline, not progress  
`[metric-gap · low (low) · partly]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* In all 5 bottle reports (46072102662, 46079806794, 46098613041, 46132235664, 46144276012), the «against the earlier run» section lists the same 8 «gone» classes. monkey-report.mjs is run --against tests/client/bottle/autopilotBaseline.json (autopilot.yml:219), so «gone» means a baseline row not seen this run, not a class retired. The rows were never pruned and the label is misleading.  
*Evidence:* autopilot-report-4607210266*/autopilot-bottle.txt, 46079806794, 46098613041, 46132235664 and 46144276012 each contain «gone presentation-ledger/story-due/board-beat-park».  
*Re-check:* for d in <audit>/dl/autopilot-report-* ; do grep -c 'gone presentation-ledger' $d/autopilot-bottle.txt; done  
*Against it (the verifier):* The finding's detail is wrong: it is 8 repeated gone lines, not «the same three», and only 2 are presentation-ledger lines. «starvation/cell-resource» is really notifications/starvation/cell-resourcenotification-only. The recheck glob misses run 46144276012. The baseline is a reasoned allow-list for classes the bottle cannot see, so a stale row loosens the gate but has no effect on the product. Not checked: whether any class stopped firing because of a fix.

**V5/parliament-flight-degrade-note** — Parliament «settled without a flight» lines ARE emitted in production but are NOTE family, so they are never counted  
`[blind-spot · low (medium) · confirmed]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* The delegate removal/exchange, support discard and neutral rally scenes, plus the sitting renewal, confess «settled without a flight» when a place is unmeasurable. The figure's move is not shown. The line reaches the journal but the 'parliament' family is NOTE, so no cause class, no report row and no Sentry ticket.  
*Evidence:* ConsoleParliamentDelegateMode.vue:452-455 `console.warn(`[parliament] ${why} — settled without a flight`)`; sittingDirector.ts:412 the same; classify.ts NOTE list contains 'parliament'; cause.ts:59 returns undefined for notes. Built JS on the pin: «settled without a flight» js=1 (present).  
*Re-check:* grep -n "'parliament'" src/common/diagnostics/classify.ts; then sed -n 452,455p src/client/components/console/parliament/ConsoleParliamentDelegateMode.vue.  
*Against it (the verifier):* Scripted e2e specs do check the degrade: banned-delegate.spec:378 via data-parl-delegate-degraded, parliament-renewal.spec:311 and political-science.spec:217 assert no renewal settled without a flight. So it is caught in covered scenarios, not wholly uncounted. The delegate, support and rally paths have no hurry/reduced-motion exclusion, so promoting them needs one added. I did not check the built-JS presence claim.

**V5/park-coalesce-attribution** — Board-beat park coalesces N covered scale moves into one glide (first write wins), silently and with no spec on attribution  
`[other · low (low) · partly]` stage 2  
*Statement:* While the board is covered, the board-beat park holds the first pre-change value per parameter, so N covered moves play as one glide with no per-seat beat (authorship only in the feed). This is documented (boardBeatPark.ts:85-88) and spec-pinned (boardBeatPark.spec.ts:108). diagnose() has no merged-view count. The scene's drain cut drops no story: it plays the glide itself (spec :370).  
*Evidence:* boardBeatPark.ts:650-656 `if (oldGame[key] !== newGame[key] && boardBeatParkState.heldParams?.[key] === undefined) { … [key]: oldGame[key] }`; boardBeatPark.ts:706-716 «A DRAIN IN FLIGHT IS CUT HERE … drainCut».  
*Re-check:* sed -n 645,665p src/client/console/boardBeatPark.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* "Silently" and "no spec" are overstated: the code comment documents the merge and a unit spec pins it, and the park is one ledgered story. The drain cut is not a silent loss: releaseBoardScaleForScene applies the accents and plays the same BOARD_BEAT_SCALE_MS glide, and the parked batch waits for the next drain. No law found that requires per-seat beats.

**V5/counted-presentation-nets-zero-browser** — CONFIRMED: counted presentation nets (ledger, handoff, starvation, transfer, holds) are at zero in the real-browser autopilot of 983c3b60d0  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Browser run 46144276012 (983c3b60d0, 48 games, 43357 presses) has 0 records of presentation-ledger, screen-handoff, notifications, transfer, animation-hold and deferred-refresh. The board-beat-drain ceiling and starvation of run 46079806794 (fc219aa201) are absent. This covers only the COUNTED families (see the blind-spot findings).  
*Evidence:* dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt: DEFECT classes are 7, none of them a presentation family; PLANNED is only workspace-anchor/dropped/parliament/aside (3). autopilot-report-46079806794/autopilot-browser.txt: «NEW animation-hold/ceiling/board-beat-drain», «NEW notifications/starvation/board-beat-drainnotification-only».  
*Re-check:* grep -E 'presentation-ledger¦screen-handoff¦notifications/¦animation-hold' <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt (no lines).  
*Re-measure:* An Autopilot browser run on 74910b95e2 itself (the artefacts are three commits behind the pin).

*Not examined by this auditor:* boardSceneMembers.ts and boardCoverWitness.ts internals: only their role was read, through their callers. · MarsBot bot-turn review state: grepped only, found no drop vocabulary, no deeper trace. · Reconciliation after a reload or a late view: not traced through gameTransport and the session reset order. By reading, the ledger and park reset and the truth shows. · deferred-refresh «refreshing anyway / overruled» sites: shape rows read, code not traced. · Whether a park story owed behind the player's screen is told AFTER the generation scene (out of order): plausible from boardBeatStoryPending excluding out-of-view stories, not verified. · No client-bundle spec runs were used, and no new CI download was made. The artefacts are of 983c3b60d0, three commits before the pin. · How often the dev-only verifiers would fire in a real browser is unmeasured: it needs a dev-mode browser autopilot or the counted door proposed in the first finding.

### V6-witness-infrastructure — visual defects

*Scope:* I read every witness the mandate lists, plus the presentation ledger, the hold registry, the transfer end verdicts and the verify*() promise verdicts. I inventoried 28 window.__con* probes and their gates, the 13 e2e witness helpers and per-helper spec counts (598 specs), and checked story-keyword coverage against 52 motion directories. I traced whether whole games see stories: cause.ts, classify.ts, causeHarvest, the autopilot LOG book, the zero bar, ci-findings of run 46144276012, the production bundle strings and the bottle record shape. I ran motionWitness.spec (4 passing) and a tsx cause probe.

**V6/promise-verdicts-stripped-from-prod** — The product's own «not shown as promised» verdicts are compiled out of every browser run and unlogged in the bottle: no whole game can count a story not told  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Promise-verdict warns (played-hero x4, rail-reward, cell-resource, tile-placement, scale-step-rating, venus-bonus-cell, scale-swing "not flown") sit under NODE_ENV!=='production', so they are absent from production-build runs (browser autopilot, e2e, field). The dev bottle DOES record them (bottlePage tap → DiagJournal; autopilot.log uploaded) but as NOTE family, and its zero bar counts only defect/error, so no whole-game run counts them.  
*Evidence:* consolePlayedHero.ts:564,1704,1761,2611; railReward.ts:337 `if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') { console.warn(`[rail-reward] ${key}: not shown as promised`; scaleSwingDirector.ts:240; consoleTilePlacement.ts:734; cellResourceBeat.ts:184. autopilot.yml:144 and playwright-e2e.yml:102 run `npm run build`. `grep -l 'not shown as promised' build/main.js build/chunks/*.js` gives 0 files, while 'end not painted' gives 1. autopilot.yml:106 sets the bottle to NODE_ENV development, and its record bottle-autopilot--game-585-r0.json has no autopilot.log key.  
*Re-check:* cd . && git grep -n -B3 "not shown as promised" -- src/client ¦ grep NODE_ENV ; grep -l 'not shown as promised' build/main.js build/chunks/*.js ¦ wc -l (expect 0); grep -l 'end not painted' build/main.js build/chunks/*.js ¦ wc -l (expect 1)  
*Against it (the verifier):* "The bottle keeps no console log" is wrong: autopilot.yml tees and uploads autopilot.log, and bottlePage.ts records every warn into the page DiagJournal. The bottle misses these verdicts because they are classified as NOTE and not counted as defects, not because the strings are missing. In production, degraded state (railRewardDiag, data-*-degraded attributes) still survives, though no walker reads it. The scale-swing text is "not flown", not "not shown as promised".

**V6/story-degrade-is-note-never-a-cause** — 47 story-degradation warn sites (~34 tags) are NOTE kind: never a cause class, never a ci-finding, never a tier the program counts  
`[metric-gap · medium (high) · partly]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* cause.ts:59 drops NOTE lines, but the event is not uncounted everywhere. Of 46 sites (not 47), 3 are presentation-ledger (STALL). About 33 sit in a hold supplier's expire, which runs after the `[animation-hold] … force-released` line (STALL, tier defect), so those are counted. About 10-13 sites are truly uncounted, e.g. start-emerge teleport (run 46144276012, not filed), colony-build degraded, the deck-pick deal ceiling and six «not shown as promised» picture-vs-truth verdicts.  
*Evidence:* src/common/diagnostics/cause.ts:59 `if (cls.kind !== 'stall' && cls.kind !== 'integrity') {` returns undefined. scratch/V6-witness-infrastructure/cause-probe.mts gives `[deck-draw] … outlived its ceiling` kind note, cause NONE; `[colony-build] degraded` note, NONE; `[presentation-ledger] story … degraded` defect. scratch/…/story-degrade-sites.txt has 47 lines. dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt:94 `[start-emerge] degraded to teleport`; ci-findings-browser.json has no start-emerge entry.  
*Re-check:* git -C . grep -nE "console\.warn\(['\`]\[[a-zA-Z -]+\][^'\`]*(outlived its ceiling¦degraded¦not shown as promised)" -- src/client ¦ wc -l (expect 47). sed -n 55,62p src/common/diagnostics/cause.ts. grep -c start-emerge on the run's ci-findings-browser.json (expect 0).  
*Against it (the verifier):* The count is wrong (46, not 47), and 3 of the sites are STALL already. The finding's own example (deck-draw "outlived its ceiling") is counted through the animation-hold ceiling class. The design already has net-ended / ceilingWait doors, and netEnded.ts admits these notes are breadcrumbs nobody counts. I did not check every expire callback by hand; some, like the parliament vote flight, may use a different registry.

**V6/motion-witness-is-global-negative** — The motion witness is page-global and negative: it proves «something moves while a hold stands», never that THE promised story was told  
`[claim-confirmed · low (medium) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Confirmed only as a liveness check. pageMoves() counts any finite GSAP tween or CSS animation on the page, polled 1 Hz and only while a blocking hold stands. It cannot attribute motion to the hold or story that claims it. It is blind to a story that registered no hold or played at the wrong place. «0 still holds» is a liveness number, not a story number.  
*Evidence:* src/client/console/waitGraph/motionWitness.ts:56-70 pageMoves() loops over gsap.globalTimeline children and document.getAnimations() with no owner filter. supervisorController.ts:494 `const pageMoving = !blockingHeld ¦¦ transport?.requestInProgress === true ¦¦ pageMoves();`. Its only consumer is the supervisor and the wait graph (waitGraphModel.ts:254). Unit spec: `npx mocha … tests/console/motionWitness.spec.ts` gives 4 passing.  
*Re-check:* git -C . grep -n "pageMoves\¦witnessMotion" -- src (expect only supervisorController and the module itself)  
*Against it (the verifier):* The module header does not overclaim: it openly asks whether ANYTHING moves. A stuck hold hidden behind unrelated motion is still caught by ANIMATION_STALL_GRACE_MS (20 s) and its own painted maxHoldMs ceiling, so the harm is a slower verdict, not an unbounded freeze. "Blind to a story with no hold" is outside the witness's scope by design. I did not rerun the 4-passing unit spec.

**V6/transfer-ends-guard-holds** — Resource-token ENDS are the one story asserted in every build, every spec and every whole game (transferEndsGuard + integrity defect)  
`[claim-confirmed · low (medium) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Holds on the pin. The three [transfer] lines are in the production bundle and classify as integrity defects. transferEndsGuard is an auto fixture of consoleTest, which autopilot and monkey import. But it judges the ENDS of tokens that were born. A promised wave that bore zero tokens passes it.  
*Evidence:* tests/e2e/consoleTest.ts:734 `transferEndsGuard: [async ({context}, use, testInfo) => {` … `{auto: true}`. console-autopilot.spec.ts:1 and console-monkey.spec.ts:3 both `import {test, …} from './consoleTest'`. The cause probe gives `[transfer] end not painted` → transfer/end-not-painted/target/stock/plants tier=defect. build/: 'end not painted' found in 1 js file.  
*Re-check:* grep -n 'auto: true' tests/e2e/consoleTest.ts ¦ head; head -3 tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts; grep -l 'end not painted' build/main.js build/chunks/*.js  
*Against it (the verifier):* Minor: the bundle has 4 [transfer] markers, not 3. The unborn-token case is a designed fallback: the counter ticks with its chip, so no silent loss. It is a coverage gap, not a guard defect. I did not prove build/ came exactly from the pin (only that its mtime is after the commit).

**V6/presentation-ledger-is-the-promise-book** — A generic promise book already exists (presentationLedger.owePresentation, defect class story-due), but only 7 story owners use it  
`[claim-confirmed · low (medium) · partly]` stage 5  
*Statement:* presentationLedger.ts is the one debt book (header l.1-28 quoted exactly). Exactly 7 src files register owed stories with owePresentation: boardBeatPark, cellResourceBeat, scaleStepRatingBeat, scaleStepRewardBeat, venusBonusCellBeat, scaleSwingDirector, screenHandoff. story-due is tier defect by default, but shapes.ts downgrades it to planned when the line says planned or only the player covered the board. Hydro and transport already use the ledger through registerTruthWitness, just not as stories.  
*Evidence:* git grep -lE 'owePresentation\(' -- src gives 7 files besides the ledger. The cause probe gives `[presentation-ledger] story «board-beat-park» degraded …` → presentation-ledger/story-due/board-beat-park tier=defect. Browser coverage 46144276012: «story 7/9» flows opened, coverage.expired=[].  
*Re-check:* git -C . grep -lE "owePresentation\(" -- src  
*Against it (the verifier):* Hydro flow-close and transport freshness already sit on the ledger's witness half, so «hydro does not use it» is only true for owed stories. The defect tier is not unconditional: it drops to planned for a declared planned degrade or a board covered by the player. I did not confirm that each named non-user (deck, colony, start, discard, played-hero) actually has a degrade ceiling.

**V6/bottle-cannot-see-stories** — Half of every Autopilot run (the bottle, jsdom layout:none) cannot tell any measured story by construction; its story classes are baselined to info  
`[harness-masks-product · low (medium) · partly]` stage 6  
*Statement:* CI runs the bottle with layout none (0x0), so its zeros say nothing about measured flights or stories; only the browser half is evidence there. The harness already reports this: gate and baseline per world, LAYOUT_BLIND info rows (e.g. start-flight:no-source, 30/48 in run 46144276012), PLAN:59 bottle-only = candidate. Story-due rows are allowed 'defect' baseline rows, absent in that run, not info. Gap: the bottle report header does not say these axes are unmeasured.  
*Evidence:* tests/client/bottle/autopilot.bottle.ts:18,34 `TM_BOTTLE_AUTOPILOT_LAYOUT … default none: jsdom's 0×0`. tests/client/bottle/autopilotBaseline.json:15 «with no layout a drain cannot land, so a story the board did see («watchable») is never told». autopilot-bottle.txt: `30 test(s) 30 rec invariant/broken/start-flight:no-source` under INFO classes.  
*Re-check:* sed -n 15,28p tests/client/bottle/autopilotBaseline.json ¦ cut -c1-300; head -12 <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-bottle.txt  
*Against it (the verifier):* The harness does not mix the worlds: yml:187-191 says 'per world, each against its own baseline … the browser's holds nothing', and the baseline 'what' says 'the bottle is blind wherever a rect or a CSS clock is needed'. Story-due classes are not tiered info: they are allowed 'defect' rows, and were absent in the cited run. start-flight:no-source is a flight-source invariant, not a story class. I found no place that sums bottle and browser rows.  
*Re-measure:* Autopilot workflow with the bottle layout input set to synthetic (env TM_BOTTLE_AUTOPILOT_LAYOUT=synthetic), same seeds 585–632: see whether start-flight:no-source and story-due go to zero.

**V6/e2e-story-specs-sparse** — Several motion systems have one or no e2e assertion of their story (bot attack, Amazonis cell token, table impact, remote landing, board-card bonus, armed flight)  
`[blind-spot · low (low) · partly]` stage visual  
*Statement:* Few e2e specs assert these stories outright: bot attack 1 (one bridge-chip check, no recorded witness), remote landing 1, Amazonis cell-resource flight 0 (only a unit spec of its hold). The paint/ends laws are not sparse: the auto transferEndsGuard enforces them in every spec. The real gap is that no spec fails when a story it passed through was never told.  
*Evidence:* `git grep -lE 'botAttack¦bot-attack' -- 'tests/e2e/*.spec.ts'` gives only console-bot-attack.spec.ts, with no arm*Witness. tests/e2e/console-amazonis-planitia.spec.ts:233 polls `waitingFor?.type` ('the cell asks for a resource'), with no transfer witness. `git grep -l armPaintWitness -- 'tests/e2e/*.spec.ts'` gives 1. Counts: armWitness 300, armTransferWitness 86, of 598 specs.

### V7-flow-contracts — visual defects

No spec, no browser, no gh.

The recheck's sed range '6,24p' does not land on the workspace families, which are at lines 236-271.

**V7/phase-not-in-kernel** — The commit-boundary phase is derived six times; «Действия карт» never writes it to its frame — the kernel's machine and the dead-B judge are blind there  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage 4

Parliament, colonies and hydro derive and write theirs. Run 46144276012: card-actions opened 48/48, moved 4 (browse->executing). "Six derivations" counts call sites: stack:1202 reads the frame, and parliament, colonies and hydro write their phase. reportSurfaceBeat/surfacePhaseOf already bridges the phase to the beat-stall guard and scaleStepStageAway. B inside card-actions is handled correctly by the component. I did not check whether draft/endgame/repeat-pick have phases to carry.

**V7/protocol-is-descriptive** — FRAME_PROTOCOL records the moves the walks made, not the boundary's law: committed -> browse is legal, an off-protocol move is tier info  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · partly]` stage 4  
*Statement:* FRAME_PROTOCOL is openly descriptive (PLAN:1461-1463, header comment: rows «read off the walks»); it lists committed->browse/configure as legal and off-protocol is tier info. So no runtime/diagnostic net flags a past-commit fold back to browse; the commit-boundary law rests only on workspaceConclusionFor's unit spec and per-case e2e. The stage-4 coverage metric is not wrong; it just does not cover that law.  
*Evidence:* consoleWorkspaceFrame.ts:97-104 `committed: ['browse', 'configure', 'executing', 'verdict', 'completing']`. shapes.ts:259-261 `name: 'off-protocol' … tier: 'info'` «a decision for the protocol, not an incident». consoleWorkspaceStack.ts:2325-2330: a write of 'browse' only pushes a row into verbTrail (printed inside two other lines: outcomeController.ts:717, supervisorController.ts:856). Six direct writes of 'browse': ConsoleParliamentSection.vue:1865,3851; consoleParliamentSectionLogic.ts:294; ConsoleParliamentVoteMode.vue:1066; stdProjectsController.ts:394; colonyController.ts:2703.  
*Re-check:* sed -n '97,104p' src/client/console/consoleWorkspaceFrame.ts ; git grep -n -E "setWorkspaceFramePhase\([^)]*'browse'\)" -- src/client  
*Against it (the verifier):* PLAN never claims the protocol enforces the boundary: it says the protocol is read off walks and the metric is coverage (walked-or-declared), so «metric-wrong» overstates it. committed->browse is a legitimate reversible fold in some flows (e.g. Parliament), so a phase-pair protocol cannot express the law without per-door identity. The conclusion law does have a unit spec. Not checked: whether any walk actually saw a finished flow fold back.

`[harness-masks-product · medium (high) · partly]` stage 6

`[claim-stale · medium (medium) · partly]` stage 2

In nightly 46126088269 on 8395cdaf75, which contains a082066081, console-hydro-copied-colony (passed) logged it once at tier defect (12 s ceiling, atMs 29467). Sibling spec hydro-stage-bound-repeat took the planned hydro-stop-answered node. So the closure misses copied-colony's path at least once. Rate at the pin is unmeasured.  
*Evidence:* git -C tm-fz-p merge-base --is-ancestor a082066081 8395cdaf75 prints ANCESTOR. The 46126088269 cause-class file for console-hydro-copied-colony (status passed) has a defect record wait-graph/ceiling/card-reaction:hydro-story-quiet: «did not come in 12000 ms — the wait ended on its ceiling» (atMs 29467).  
*Re-check:* Download as above. Then grep -rl hydro-story-quiet <dir>, and open the file to see tier defect with status passed.  
*Against it (the verifier):* There is a single occurrence in a single nightly, so the evidence is thin for a "by rate" reading. The sibling spec in the same run took the planned path, so the fix works there. Hydronetwork commits landed between 8395cdaf75 and the pin, so the pin itself is unmeasured. The line number is off by one.  
*Re-measure:* Playwright E2E workflow_dispatch with spec console-hydro-copied-colony.spec.ts and repeat-each 10, to get the rate on the pin.

**V8/e2e-artefact-is-one-test** — The supplied «whole Playwright E2E run» 46144266613 is a 1-test dispatch; the whole-suite reading must come from 46126088269  
`[metric-wrong · low (medium) · partly]` stage metrics  
*Statement:* The audit mandate calls 46144266613 «the whole Playwright E2E run (success)». It is a workflow_dispatch with one shard and one test (console-parliament-water). The ledger shows total 1 and the cause-classes directory holds 1 file. The last whole suite is the nightly 46126088269 on 8395cdaf75, with 1872 tests and 1 failed. The repo's PLAN handover never cites 46144266613, so the mislabel is in the audit inputs only.  
*Evidence:* flake-ledger/e2e-flake-ledger.json has {"runId":"46144266613.1","total":1,"failed":[]}. gh run view 46144266613 --json event,jobs shows workflow_dispatch and «E2E shard 1/1». The cause-classes directory holds 1 file. scratch extract.js on 46126088269 prints e2e status {passed:1541, skipped:5, failed:3}.  
*Re-check:* Read dl/e2e-46144266613-983c3b60d0/flake-ledger/e2e-flake-ledger.json. Then: gh run list --workflow 'Playwright E2E' --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --limit 5 --json databaseId,event,conclusion.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The «handover» part does not hold: PLAN.md at the pin never names 46144266613, and the label comes from the audit brief. A prior verifier, M7, says the same. I did not re-derive the 26-class split (17 planted / 9 natural, 3/4/2). out1.txt lists 27 e2e classes, and the planted-vs-natural attribution is the auditor's own classification. The 3 failed files are retries of the single failing test.

**V8/bottle-monkey-baseline-holds-real-class** — The bottle monkey baseline tolerates console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n, which the real browser names in the never-cured finale  
`[check-weakened · low (low) · partly]` stage 4  
*Statement:* monkeyBaseline.json (freeze-gate) holds console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n. Its why: no-layout hero flight, CLEAN in a real browser 2026-11-14. Browser games 621/630 of 46144276012 name the class (#119, open, regression), by another road. No guard cross-checks rows against browser findings, so any road to the class passes the bottle monkey gate. The browser-check law is autopilotBaseline's; that baseline does not hold the class.  
*Evidence:* node -e on tests/client/bottle/monkeyBaseline.json: its classes include «console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n». In the browser report of 46144276012: «2 test(s) 2 rec console-foreground-watchdog/expired/lease:mandatory-choice×n, first: game 621».  
*Re-check:* cd tm-fz-p and run node -e "console.log(Object.keys(require('./tests/client/bottle/monkeyBaseline.json').classes))". Then read the DEFECT classes in autopilot-browser.txt.  
*Against it (the verifier):* The quoted claim is from autopilotBaseline.json's "what", not monkeyBaseline's; monkeyBaseline only justifies rows one by one. The bottle row's road (hero flight with no rect, fz0:soil-bacteria-wg) is a different mechanism from the browser's (stranded choice, failed submit or parked start), so its no-layout reason may still be true for that road. The bottle autopilot does not baseline the class: #119 was first filed from it. Only the bottle monkey gate is affected.

**V8/finale-diverted-green-over-unserved** — The «nothing stranded» finale spec passes while its own shadow records the never-cured finale signature (choice:generic, cure none:no-door:task)  
`[harness-masks-product · info (medium) · refuted — NOT A FINDING]` stage 6  
*Statement:* On 8395cdaf75 (CI 46126088269, before the pin), 2 of the 3 console-finale-diverted tests passed while carrying one screen-shadow unserved/task/nothing record. That record was the shadow's own false positive on the RX35 finale offer stage (#193, triage:noise), fixed by 3e34ede433, which is in the pin. It is not the cause of the 621/630 stalls (the leak detector, liveness and a rebuild fired there). No post-fix CI run of the spec has been read.  
*Evidence:* 46126088269, console-finale-diverted files: defect screen-shadow/finding/unserved/task/nothing, "cure":"plan:none:no-door:task","wf":"or","frames":[] at about 4.9 s. Browser report of 46144276012, THE LOG: «[screen-shadow] cure-plan «none:no-door:task» by shadow ... for «choice:generi...»» in games 621 and 630, which ended in «[session-rebuild] page — «choice» (or#2015)».  
*Re-check:* Open the two console-finale-diverted JSONs in the 46126088269 download and read records[0].line. Compare with autopilot-walk.txt of game 621 (lines 178-189).  
*Against it (the verifier):* #193 found the shadow lacked the finale offer owner, so it reported «nothing» while the offer served the question. The fix is in the pin. The spec walks have no leak-detector strand and no liveness stall, so they differ from 621. The no-door:task plan is a generic class string. Game 630 was not shown to be a finale stall: it stranded after a failed submit at gen 9. The spec has 3 tests, not 2. Not measured: a post-fix CI run of the spec. 46144266613 ran only shard1.  
*Re-measure:* Playwright E2E dispatch with spec console-finale-diverted.spec.ts and repeat-each 10: count how many runs have the unserved record and how long it stood.

**V8/bottle-only-blind-rows-hold** — Six bottle-only blind classes never appear in any of the 5 browser halves; the healthy browser game is near zero  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage 6  
*Statement:* Holds for these six. Each run prints them in 2–36 of 48 bottle games, and none of the 5 browser reports names any of them. A healthy browser game (44 of 48 in 46144276012) prints on average 0.045 planned and 0.023 info class lines, plus 0.14 raw page warnings.  
*Evidence:* autopilot-bottle.txt of 46144276012: start-flight:no-source 30 tests, summary-tiles 30, take-turn 21, reward-place 15, hover 9, ma-price 3. The classes in autopilot-browser.json of all 5 reports contain none of them. scratch per-game totals: healthy 44, planned lines 2, info lines 1, log lines 6.  
*Re-check:* node -e "console.log(Object.keys(require('<report>/autopilot-browser.json').classes))" for each of the 5 report directories.

*Not examined by this auditor:* The bottle shards' autopilot.log files and browser autopilot-walk.txt beyond the stall window of game 621. · Older nightly whole-suite corpora (e.g. 45971250987), so there is no trend of natural classes under green tests over time. · A per-site check that every NOTE «outlived its ceiling» has a paired hold or stall line. · The 324-test gap between the e2e ledger total and the cause-class file count. · No spec was run: none of these claims needed one, and the hydro and finale rates need CI (see remeasure).

### F1-field-readiness — the field

*Scope:* Read the diagnostics contract (docs/claude/diagnostics.md §3, .claude/rules/diagnostics.md) and the code: hub.ts policy/budgets/consent, sessionLedger.ts, sentryEvent.ts sessionEventOf, privacy.ts, classify.ts, diag-lib aggregateTrend/renderTrend, consoleLiveness/supervisorController/frameWitness/foreground watchdog, the autopilot spec + monkey-report. Measured: rates over 5 real-browser Autopilot reports (48.9 h), ledger JSON size, a sleep-gap probe of observeLiveness, the secret grep, A7 history, gh issue/run lists (read-only).

**F1/note-tier-motion-loss-invisible-in-field** — A motion that did not play (note tier) reaches the field ledger only as +1 on kinds.note; diag trend never prints it  
`[blind-spot · medium (high) · partly]` stage field  
*Statement:* NOTE-tier motion-loss lines ([parliament] «settled without a flight» x5, [start-emerge] degraded to teleport, [draft-pick] will teleport, [colony-build] degraded) have no cause. The field ledger counts them only as +1 on kinds.note, with no family, and diag trend never prints kinds.note: it reads only kinds.error. The [presentation-ledger] «degraded» lines are stall classes with shapes and transfer/discard-count losses are integrity classes, so both are counted. long-frame perf is shown per screen.  
*Evidence:* src/server/diagnostics/hub/sessionLedger.ts:150-152 `if (entry.cause === undefined) { if (before === undefined) { ledger.kinds[entry.kind] = …+1`; classify.ts NOTE list l.83-100 holds start-emerge, draft-pick, parliament, colony-build, console play; sites: ConsoleStartScene.vue:9251 `[start-emerge] degraded to teleport`, ConsoleDraftWorkspace.vue:948, ConsoleParliamentVoteMode.vue:1665 `settled without a flight` (+4 more); scripts/diag-lib.mjs aggregateTrend `acc.errors += num(l.kinds?.error)`, the only kinds read. The CI autopilot DOES see them (run 46144276012 THE LOG: `[start-emerge] degraded to teleport` 1×).  
*Re-check:* git -C . grep -n -i -E "console\.warn\(['\`]\[[a-zA-Z -]+\][^'\`]{0,80}(degrad¦teleport¦without a flight)" -- src/client ; then read sessionLedger.ts l.146-170 and grep kinds in scripts/diag-lib.mjs.  
*Against it (the verifier):* presentation-ledger «degraded» lines are a STALL family (classify.ts:43) with shapes story-due (defect) and story-degraded-by (info), so they are counted per class and shown in trend. Motion losses in transfer and discard-count are INTEGRITY classes. long-frame perf has a per-screen map that trend renders. A player's report journal.json keeps the note text, though per report and never summed. I did not check the cited CI run.

**F1/browser-autopilot-rebuilds-always-zero** — Real-browser autopilot report prints «rebuilds 0» while the same run had 2 session rebuilds — the browser spec never writes rebuilds  
`[metric-wrong · medium (high) · confirmed]` stage 6  
*Statement:* On the real-browser lane «rebuilds» is structurally 0: monkey-report sums f.autopilot.rebuilds, which only the bottle sets. Run 46144276012 printed «rebuilds 0» beside «episodes 2 (rebuilt 2) · NEVER CURED 2». The findings rule «the last line was taken in a whole game» cannot fire from the browser lane.  
*Evidence:* autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt: `47 to the final scoring · 1 capped · 0 STUCK · rebuilds 0` and header `episodes 2 (rebuilt 2) · NEVER CURED 2`, class `session-rebuild/page/choice 2 rec`. scripts/monkey-report.mjs:730 `out.rebuilds += Number(a.rebuilds ?? 0)`; tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts:1954-1959 `autopilot: {...played, table, pilot, cards, slow, slowFrames, sent, weather, log}`; `played` (game 621 harvest r0.json) has no rebuilds key; only tests/client/bottle/autopilot.bottle.ts:97,237 sets it.  
*Re-check:* grep -n rebuilds <audit>/dl/autopilot-46144276012-983c3b60d0/autopilot-report-46144276012-1/autopilot-browser.txt; git -C . grep -n rebuilds -- tests/e2e/console-autopilot.spec.ts (no hit).  
*Against it (the verifier):* The rebuilds were not invisible: the same report lists them as episodes rebuilt, as a NEW defect class and in the log book, and the gate failed. The masking bites only once the class is baselined and the episode is cured. Because of that, severity is medium rather than high. I did not check whether a cured rebuilt episode alone fails the gate.

**F1/a7-release-probe-never-done** — A7 (TM_DIAG_PROBE=stall on an installed release) was never done — the field road of a stall is unproven since 2026-11-14  
`[claim-not-met · medium (high) · partly]` stage field  
*Statement:* A7 (TM_DIAG_PROBE=stall on an installed release) is still open at the pin: no commit or doc records a run, even though daily releases have long met its precondition. The release road for selftest and report was proven on 2026-11-14 (§9.2), and real stall events reached Sentry on 11-14/15 (#58–#63). What has never been proven is the controlled stall plus a renderer stop with its trace, and nothing has been proven after the 11-19 hub change.  
*Evidence:* git -C . log --since=2026-11-14 -i --grep="PROBE=stall\¦\bA7\b\¦control stall" → empty. PLAN.md:55 «open». gh issue list --label ci-finding --state all → labels `src:sentry: 6` (#58–#63, all 2026-11-18); «Sentry findings» runs 44538157862…46150561517 (11-19…11-28) all success with nothing new filed.  
*Re-check:* sed -n 55p docs/claude/console/freeze-zero/PLAN.md ¦ cut -c1-300 ; the git log above; gh issue list --repo victor-zelinskiy/terraforming-mars --label src:sentry --state all.  
*Against it (the verifier):* "The field road of a stall is unproven since 11-14" overstates it. The release report road was proven on the box on 11-14. Real sessions also delivered liveness, wait-graph and renderer-hang events to Sentry on 11-14/15, and those were filed as issues, so the stall road has worked end to end at least once. Seeing no Sentry issues since 11-18 does not prove the road is broken; it is only ambiguous.  
*Re-measure:* On the box with the installed current release: docs/claude/diagnostics.md §9.2 with TM_DIAG_PROBE=stall, then npm run diag -- classes / trend --env box to read the episode AND the next launch's session event.

**F1/liveness-wall-clock-across-suspend** — The liveness arbiter is not starve/suspend-aware: one look after a 1 h sleep during a beat is a 3601 s «unserved» stall episode  
`[net-fires-on-healthy · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 1  
*Statement:* PLAUSIBLE. Motion ceilings, the foreground watchdog, the pulse and the wait graph read page starvation; tickLiveness gets neither pageStarved nor a suspend-aware clock. The pure function, fed a beat owed with no motion registered and a 1 h gap, returns stalled/unserved quietMs 3601000, then «recovered» — a defect episode plus a 1 h «stood» in the ledger.  
*Evidence:* supervisorController.ts:609 passes `pageStarved` to tickWaitGraph only; l.612-640 tickLiveness({now: Date.now(), …}) has no starved field; consoleLiveness.ts:244-245 unserved = (promptLive¦¦beatOwed) && !accepting && quietMs >= LIVENESS_GRACE_MS. Probe scratch/F1-field-readiness/sleep.mts (tsx from the pin): `t=1h+2s after resume … {"stalled":true,"reason":"unserved","quietMs":3601000,…}` then `{"recovered":{"afterMs":3602000,"rungs":1}}`.  
*Re-check:* cd . && node node_modules/tsx/dist/cli.mjs ../tm-fz-p-audit/scratch/F1-field-readiness/sleep.mts  
*Against it (the verifier):* I did not measure this on a device, and the order of timers versus animation frames on resume is unknown. A request still on the wire with no response stays in flight and would hide the stall. The probe hand-sets inFlight to empty, but the real code reaches the same state because its wall-clock limits expire. The defect needs a beat to be owed while the device is suspended. A player who is simply asked a question puts the screen in an accepting state, so that case is not affected.  
*Re-measure:* A server-runner unit spec over observeLiveness with a suspend gap; then on a Steam Deck/box: suspend the device mid-commit beat (A on a card action) and read the journal for [liveness] stall.

**F1/ledger-one-extra-value-16kb** — The session ledger leaves as ONE extra value, against the measured ~16 KB per-value cut; the worst sessions may arrive unreadable  
`[metric-gap · medium (medium) · partly]` stage field  
*Statement:* sessionEventOf (sentryEvent.ts:409) sends the whole ledger as one extra value, with no parts and no size spec. At its caps (120 classes / 60 episodes) it estimates to ~22 KB, over the measured ~16 KB per-value cut. A trimmed ledger likely still reads as valid (run, classes) while it silently loses episodes, outcomes and longFrames, so aggregateTrend undercounts it instead of skipping it. The collector's behaviour on objects was not measured. hub.ts:580 drops, not defers, a ledger refused by the daily cap.  
*Evidence:* src/server/diagnostics/hub/sentryEvent.ts:409 `extra: {ledger},` vs l.66-71 «THE COLLECTOR KEEPS ~16 KB OF ONE `extra` VALUE … EXTRA_LIST_BYTES = 12_000»; sessionLedger.ts:21-25 LEDGER_CLASSES_MAX 120, LEDGER_EPISODES_MAX 60; scratch ledgerSize.mjs output `classes 120 episodes 60 sections 12: 21929 bytes`. Also hub.ts:580 drops (not defers) the ledger when dailyRoom() is false.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/F1-field-readiness/ledgerSize.mjs ; sed -n 385,414p src/server/diagnostics/hub/sentryEvent.ts  
*Against it (the verifier):* The trend reader skips a ledger only when run or classes are missing, both of which sit early in the object. The measured cut drops items and keeps the structure valid. So the claim "skipped as unreadable" probably does not hold: the likely result is a silent undercount of episodes, outcomes and long frames. The ~22 KB figure assumes ~50-char class names and needs roughly >90 classes. No field data shows sessions get that large. contexts.session carries summary counts but the trend does not read it.  
*Re-measure:* scripts/diag-probe.mjs on the real project with a synthetic ledger of 120 classes/60 episodes, read back with diag issue --event (cutPaths).

**F1/no-reader-for-field-trend** — Nobody reads the field ledgers automatically: session events are archived, planned/info growth per release has no alarm  
`[claim-unmeasured · medium (medium) · confirmed]` stage 7  
*Statement:* Only DEFECT classes get an alarm (Sentry issue → GitHub finding). Session ledgers are excluded from filing and archived; no workflow runs trend. A rise of planned/info classes, notes, long frames or rebuilds per hour in a release is visible only if someone runs diag trend by hand.  
*Evidence:* scripts/ci/findings.mjs:551 `NOT_A_DEFECT = /…¦^session — /`; scripts/diag.mjs:116 `action: 'archive', why: 'the sessions\' ledger — a record, not a class'`; `grep -rn "trend\¦tm-session" .github/workflows scripts/ci` → only findings.mjs:551.  
*Re-check:* cd . && grep -rn -E "trend¦tm-session" .github/workflows scripts/ci  
*Against it (the verifier):* A new defect-tier class does alarm through Sentry, then sentry-findings.yml files a finding; the finding admits this. Minor evidence errors: the recheck grep matches nothing (findings.mjs:551 matches neither pattern), and the archive return is at diag.mjs:117, not 116. Not checked: whether rebuilds are sent as defect events.

**F1/rate-no-flood-long-tail** — No Sentry flood at today's rate: ≈35 defect events per 100 h, but a long tail — 14 defect classes in 49 h, none repeating across runs  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage field  
*Statement:* Over 5 real-browser Autopilot runs (2934 min): 18 defect / 6 planned / 6 info records ≈ 37 / 12 / 12 per 100 h; budgets (40/run, 2/class/run, 200/day) never bite at this rate, so volume is no risk. The risk is the shape: every defect class appeared in ONE run only (≈ a new Sentry issue per 3–4 h of play), and one incident fans out (game 621: 5 defect records). Autopilot is a lower bound for players (no idle/sleep/hidden page).  
*Evidence:* scratch/F1-field-readiness/rate.cjs → `TOTAL {"defect":18,"planned":6,"info":6,"min":2934} hours 48.9` · `per 100 h: defect 37 planned 12 info 12` · `distinct classes 20 defect classes 14 seen in >1 run workspace-anchor/dropped/parliament/aside(2) liveness/still-hold(2) wait-graph/ceiling/transfer:touchdown(2)`. Runs 46144276012 (983c3b60d0) 11 defects, 46132235664 0, 46098613041 4, 46079806794 3, 46072102662 0. hub.ts:57-60 budgets.  
*Re-check:* node <audit>/scratch/F1-field-readiness/rate.cjs

**F1/policy-privacy-hold** — Policy by tier, the silent player, the scrubbers and «no DSN in the tree» hold as written  
`[claim-confirmed · info · not verified]` stage field  
*Statement:* Confirmed on the pin: autoDecision skips non-defect tiers and note/perf; the dev notice is opt-in OFF; ids p/g/s+8–16 hex are fingerprinted, table names/OS account/hostname replaced, keys of maps scrubbed; the secret grep hits only URL templates and a fake test DSN. Note: automatic reports are opt-OUT (consent defaults ON).  
*Evidence:* hub.ts:135 `if (entry.cause !== undefined && entry.cause.tier !== 'defect') return {skip:'policy'}`; hub.ts:210 `let consent = stored.consent !== false;`; diagnosticsPrefs.ts:7 «OFF by default»; privacy.ts ID regex `/\b([pgs])([0-9a-f]{8,16})\b/g`; git grep for ingest.sentry.io¦sentry_key¦SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN= → hubConfig.ts:83,84 (templates) and diagnosticsHub.spec.ts:23,194,195 (fake `abc123@o1…/42`).  
*Re-check:* git -C . grep -n -I -E "ingest\.(us\.¦de\.)?sentry\.io¦sentry_key¦SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN=" -- . ":(exclude)node_modules" ¦ cut -d: -f1,2

*Not examined by this auditor:* envelopeSender/outbox persistence and the offline path of the session event · reportPackage budgets and the «Сообщить о проблеме» surface flow in detail · sentry-housekeeping.yml logic beyond its step names · the bottle autopilot rates (only the real-browser lane was used) · whether a >16 KB nested extra.ledger is actually cut on the real collector (needs diag-probe, talks to Sentry) · leak detector / wait graph wall-clock graces across a suspend; slow Wi-Fi paths · git history scan for a once-pushed DSN · no guard spec was run (only a pure-function tsx probe), to stay within the shared-laptop budget
